`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/VirgolaAntiCheat.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.PacketEvents;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckManager;
import it.virgola.anticheat.commands.AcCommand;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.*;
import it.virgola.anticheat.gui.AcGui;
import it.virgola.anticheat.listeners.BlockPlaceListener;
import it.virgola.anticheat.listeners.CoreListener;
import it.virgola.anticheat.listeners.DamageListener;
import it.virgola.anticheat.listeners.FreezeListener;
import it.virgola.anticheat.listeners.MovementExemptionListener;
import it.virgola.anticheat.listeners.MovementListener;
import it.virgola.anticheat.packets.PacketIngestor;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

public final class VirgolaAntiCheat extends JavaPlugin {

    private Configs configs;

    private TickService tickService;
    private LagCompensation lag;

    private PlayerDataManager playerDataManager;
    private AlertService alertService;
    private ViolationService violationService;
    private CheckManager checkManager;

    private PacketIngestor packetIngestor;
    private AcGui gui;

    @Override
    public void onEnable() {
        // Configs
        saveDefaultConfig();
        this.configs = new Configs(this);
        configs.init();

        // Core services
        this.tickService = new TickService();
        tickService.start(this);

        double pingBase = getConfig().getDouble("lag-compensation.ping_multiplier.base", 1.0);
        double pingPer100 = getConfig().getDouble("lag-compensation.ping_multiplier.per_100ms", 0.15);
        double tpsBase = getConfig().getDouble("lag-compensation.tps_multiplier.base", 1.0);
        double tpsPerBelow = getConfig().getDouble("lag-compensation.tps_multiplier.per_tps_below_20", 0.10);
        this.lag = new LagCompensation(tickService, pingBase, pingPer100, tpsBase, tpsPerBelow);

        this.playerDataManager = new PlayerDataManager();
        
        ExemptionService exemptionService = new ExemptionService();

        this.alertService = new AlertService(configs);
        this.violationService = new ViolationService(configs, alertService);

        // Pass 'this' plugin reference for async scheduling
        this.checkManager = new CheckManager(configs, violationService, lag, exemptionService, this);

        this.packetIngestor = new PacketIngestor(configs, playerDataManager, checkManager);

        // PacketEvents: we do NOT create an instance here (PacketEvents is an external plugin).
        PacketEvents.getAPI().getEventManager().registerListener(packetIngestor);

        // Listeners
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(new CoreListener(playerDataManager), this);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(new BlockPlaceListener(playerDataManager, checkManager), this);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(new MovementListener(playerDataManager, checkManager), this);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(new DamageListener(playerDataManager, checkManager), this);
        
        getServer().getPluginManager().registerEvents(new MovementExemptionListener(exemptionService), this);


        // GUI
        this.gui = new AcGui(configs, checkManager, tickService, this);
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(gui, this);

        // Command
        AcCommand ac = new AcCommand(configs, checkManager, tickService, violationService, alertService, gui);
        getCommand("ac").setExecutor(ac);
        getCommand("ac").setTabCompleter(ac);
        
        // Freeze listener (needs AcCommand reference)
        Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(new FreezeListener(ac), this);

        getLogger().info("VirgolaAntiCheat enabled. Paper 1.21.11 - PacketEvents hooked.");
    }

    @Override
    public void onDisable() {
        // Shutdown check async resources
        if (checkManager != null) {
            checkManager.shutdown();
        }
        
        try {
            if (packetIngestor != null) {
                PacketEvents.getAPI().getEventManager().unregisterListener(packetIngestor);
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // If PacketEvents is shutting down, unregister may fail.
        }
        getLogger().info("VirgolaAntiCheat disabled.");
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/AbstractCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks;

import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;

public abstract class AbstractCheck implements Check {

    protected final Configs configs;
    private boolean enabled;
    private boolean debug;

    protected AbstractCheck(Configs configs) {
        this.configs = configs;
        reloadFromConfig();
    }

    public void reloadFromConfig() {
        String base = "checks." + id().name();
        this.enabled = configs.checks().getBoolean(base + ".enabled", true);
        this.debug = configs.checks().getBoolean(base + ".debug", false);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() { return enabled; }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) { this.enabled = enabled; }

    public boolean debug() { return debug; }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/Check.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;

import java.util.UUID;

import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;

/**
 * Contract for a single anti-cheat check.
 */
public interface Check {

    CheckId id();

    boolean enabled();

    void setEnabled(boolean enabled);


    void reload();

    default void onPacketReceive(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        // default no-op
    }

    default void onBlockPlace(PlayerData data, BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        // default no-op
    }
    
    default void onMove(PlayerData data, PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        // default no-op
    }
    
    default void onDamage(PlayerData data, EntityDamageEvent event) {
        // default no-op
    }

    default void onQuit(UUID uuid) {
        // default no-op
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/CheckId.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks;

public enum CheckId {
    SCAFFOLD("Movement", "Detects scaffold/bridging hacks by analyzing block placement angles"),
    FLY("Movement", "Detects fly hacks by monitoring vertical movement patterns"),
    SPEED("Movement", "Detects speed hacks by comparing movement to maximum allowed speeds"),
    JESUS("Movement", "Detects water-walking hacks by checking liquid interactions"),
    NOFALL("Movement", "Detects NoFall hacks by monitoring fall damage"),
    BADPACKETS("Packet", "Detects invalid pitch values that exceed normal limits"),
    KILLAURA("Combat", "Detects combat hacks: Reach (hit distance), Angle (aim direction), NoSwing (missing animation), GCD (rotation patterns), MultiAura (multiple targets), Pattern (robotic consistency)");
    
    private final String type;
    private final String description;
    
    CheckId(String type, String description) {
        this.type = type;
        this.description = description;
    }
	
	public String path() {
	    return "checks." + name();
	}
	
	public String type() {
	    return type;
	}
	
	public String description() {
	    return description;
	}

    public static CheckId fromString(String s) {
        if (s == null) return null;
        try {
            return CheckId.valueOf(s.toUpperCase());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) {
            return null;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/CheckManager.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl.*;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ExemptionService;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.LagCompensation;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;

import java.util.*;

/**
 * Central registry + router for all checks.
 */
public class CheckManager {

    private final Configs configs;
    private final Map<CheckId, Check> checks = new EnumMap<>(CheckId.class);
    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private final Plugin plugin;

    public CheckManager(Configs configs, ViolationService violations, LagCompensation lag, ExemptionService exemptionService, Plugin plugin) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.plugin = plugin;

        register(new ScaffoldCheck(configs, violations, lag));
        register(new BadPacketsCheck(configs, violations));
        register(new FlyCheck(configs, violations, lag));
        register(new SpeedCheck(configs, violations, lag));
        register(new JesusCheck(configs, violations, lag));
        
        // NoFallCheck needs plugin reference for async scheduling
        NoFallCheck noFallCheck = new NoFallCheck(configs, violations, lag);
        noFallCheck.setPlugin(plugin);
        register(noFallCheck);
        
        // KillAuraCheck needs plugin reference for async scheduling
        KillAuraCheck killAuraCheck = new KillAuraCheck(configs, violations, lag);
        killAuraCheck.setPlugin(plugin);
        register(killAuraCheck);

        reloadAll();
    }

    private void register(Check check) {
        checks.put(check.id(), check);
    }

    public Collection<Check> all() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableCollection(checks.values());
    }

    public Check get(CheckId id) {
        return checks.get(id);
    }

    public boolean isEnabled(CheckId id) {
        Check c = checks.get(id);
        return c != null && c.enabled();
    }

    public int enabledCount() {
        int n = 0;
        for (Check c : checks.values()) {
            if (c.enabled()) n++;
        }
        return n;
    }

    public boolean toggle(CheckId id) {
        Check c = checks.get(id);
        if (c == null) return false;

        boolean newState = !c.enabled();
        c.setEnabled(newState);

        // Persist to checks.yml
        configs.checks().set(id.path() + ".enabled", newState);
        configs.checks().save();
        return newState;
    }

    public void reloadAll() {
        for (Check c : checks.values()) {
            c.reload();
        }
    }

    public void handlePacket(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        for (Check c : checks.values()) {
            if (!c.enabled()) continue;
            try {
                c.onPacketReceive(data, event);
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                // Never allow a single check to kill packet pipeline.
                // Any real anti-cheat should log this; we keep it silent for now.
                t.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }
    
    public void handleMove(PlayerData data, org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (data == null || event == null) return;

        for (Check check : checks.values()) {
            if (!check.enabled()) continue;
            try {
                check.onMove(data, event);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                // fail-safe: non deve mai crashare un check
            }
        }
    }

    public void handleQuit(java.util.UUID uuid) {
        if (uuid == null) return;

        for (Check check : checks.values()) {
            try {
                check.onQuit(uuid);
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            }
        }
    }


    public void handleBlockPlace(PlayerData data, BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        for (Check c : checks.values()) {
            if (!c.enabled()) continue;
            try {
                c.onBlockPlace(data, event);
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                t.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }
    
    public void handleDamage(PlayerData data, org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent event) {
        for (Check c : checks.values()) {
            if (!c.enabled()) continue;
            try {
                c.onDamage(data, event);
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                t.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Shutdown all checks that have async resources.
     * Should be called when the plugin disables.
     */
    public void shutdown() {
        for (Check c : checks.values()) {
            if (c instanceof NoFallCheck noFall) {
                noFall.shutdown();
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/impl/BadPacketsCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.packettype.PacketTypeCommon;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;

public final class BadPacketsCheck implements Check {

    private final Configs configs;
    private final ViolationService violations;

    private volatile boolean enabled;

    public BadPacketsCheck(Configs configs, ViolationService violations) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.violations = violations;
        this.enabled = true;
    }

    @Override
    public CheckId id() {
        return CheckId.BADPACKETS;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        // niente di speciale: la configurazione viene letta live dai file
    }

    @Override
    public void onPacketReceive(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        if (data == null || event == null) return;

        PacketTypeCommon type = event.getPacketType();

        // IMPORTANT: only handle flying-related packets
        if (!WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying.isFlying(type)) return;

        try {
            WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying w = new WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event);

            // DO NOT call w.read() on PacketEvents 2.11.1 (can cause IndexOutOfBounds on some variants)

            if (!w.hasRotationChanged()) return;

            float pitch = w.getLocation().getPitch();

            // Minecraft pitch legit range is [-90, +90]
            // Allow a tiny tolerance to avoid float weirdness.
            double min = configs.checks().getDouble(id().path() + ".params.min_pitch", -90.0);
            double max = configs.checks().getDouble(id().path() + ".params.max_pitch", 90.0);
            double tol = configs.checks().getDouble(id().path() + ".params.tolerance", 0.001);

            if (pitch < (min - tol) || pitch > (max + tol)) {
                violations.flag(data, id().name(), 1,
                        "pitch=" + pitch + " range=[" + min + "," + max + "]");
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            // Fail-safe: never break PacketEvents pipeline
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/impl/FlyCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.LagCompensation;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.data.BlockData;
import org.bukkit.block.data.Levelled;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * FLY - Flight Detection (Air + Water)
 * 
 * Handles:
 * - Air fly detection (hover, ascend, no gravity)
 * - Water fly detection (only if player is truly stationary in water)
 * 
 * Does NOT flag for:
 * - Swimming/moving horizontally in water
 * - Ascending/descending in water currents or waterfalls
 * - Dolphin's Grace effect
 * - Players partially submerged or at water surface
 */
public final class FlyCheck implements Check {

    private final Configs configs;
    private final ViolationService violations;
    private final LagCompensation lag;

    private volatile boolean enabled = true;

    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastX = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastY = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastZ = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastPacketTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> airTicks = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> stableAirTicks = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> waterStableTicks = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> waterNoMoveTicks = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> buffer = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastFlagTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastDy = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> elytraEndTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public FlyCheck(Configs configs, ViolationService violations, LagCompensation lag) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.violations = violations;
        this.lag = lag;
    }

    @Override
    public CheckId id() {
        return CheckId.FLY;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        this.enabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.FLY.enabled", true);
    }

    @Override
    public void onPacketReceive(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        if (!WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying.isFlying(event.getPacketType())) return;

        Player player = data.player();
        if (player == null || !player.isOnline()) return;

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Exemptions
        if (data.isExempt() || player.isFlying() || player.getAllowFlight() ||
            player.isInsideVehicle() || player.isRiptiding()) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Elytra grace
        if (player.isGliding()) {
            elytraEndTime.put(uuid, now);
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }
        if (now - elytraEndTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L) < 1500) {
            return;
        }

        // Parse packet
        WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying wrapper;
        try {
            wrapper = new WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return;
        }
        if (!wrapper.hasPositionChanged()) return;

        double x = wrapper.getLocation().getX();
        double y = wrapper.getLocation().getY();
        double z = wrapper.getLocation().getZ();
        boolean onGround = wrapper.isOnGround();

        // Get previous position
        Double prevX = lastX.get(uuid);
        Double prevY = lastY.get(uuid);
        Double prevZ = lastZ.get(uuid);
        Long prevTime = lastPacketTime.get(uuid);

        // Update tracking
        lastX.put(uuid, x);
        lastY.put(uuid, y);
        lastZ.put(uuid, z);
        lastPacketTime.put(uuid, now);

        if (prevX == null || prevY == null || prevZ == null || prevTime == null) {
            return;
        }

        double dx = x - prevX;
        double dy = y - prevY;
        double dz = z - prevZ;
        double hDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
        long dt = now - prevTime;

        double m = lag.multiplier(player);

        if (dt < 10 || dt > 300) return;

        World world = player.getWorld();
        Location loc = new Location(world, x, y, z);

        // On ground = reset
        if (onGround || isOnGround(loc, world)) {
            airTicks.put(uuid, 0);
            stableAirTicks.put(uuid, 0);
            waterStableTicks.put(uuid, 0);
            waterNoMoveTicks.put(uuid, 0);
            decay(uuid, 0.3);
            lastDy.put(uuid, dy);
            return;
        }

        // Environment
        boolean inWater = isInWater(loc, world);
        boolean inLava = isInLava(loc, world);
        boolean hasClimbable = hasClimbable(loc, world);

        // Water fly detection
        if (inWater && !player.isSwimming()) {
            checkWaterFly(data, uuid, now, dy, hDist, m, player, loc, world);
            lastDy.put(uuid, dy);
            return;
        }

        // Swimming = legit
        if (player.isSwimming()) {
            reset(uuid);
            lastDy.put(uuid, dy);
            return;
        }

        // Air fly detection
        if (!inWater && !inLava && !hasClimbable) {
            checkAirFly(data, uuid, now, dy, m, player);
        }

        lastDy.put(uuid, dy);
    }

    private void checkAirFly(PlayerData data, UUID uuid, long now, double dy, double m, Player player) {
        int air = airTicks.merge(uuid, 1, (a, b) -> a + b);

        if (air < 6) return;

        // Levitation/Slow Falling
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.LEVITATION) ||
            player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SLOW_FALLING)) {
            decay(uuid, 0.2);
            return;
        }

        Double prevDy = lastDy.getOrDefault(uuid, -0.08);
        double expectedDy = prevDy - 0.08;
        double tolerance = 0.15 * m;

        boolean suspicious = false;
        String reason = "";

        // Ascending without jump
        if (dy > 0.3 && air > 10) {
            suspicious = true;
            reason = "ASCEND";
        }

        // Hover
        if (!suspicious && Math.abs(dy) < 0.05 * m && air > 12) {
            int stable = stableAirTicks.merge(uuid, 1, (a, b) -> a + b);
            if (stable >= getInt("stable_air_min", 6)) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason = "HOVER";
            }
        } else {
            stableAirTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        }

        // No gravity
        if (!suspicious && dy > expectedDy + tolerance && air > 15 && dy > -0.3) {
            suspicious = true;
            reason = "NO_GRAVITY";
        }

        if (!suspicious) {
            decay(uuid, 0.15);
            return;
        }

        double weight = getDouble("air_weight", 1.0);
        double buf = buffer.merge(uuid, weight, (a, b) -> a + b);

        double threshold = getDouble("air_threshold", 4.0);
        long cooldown = (long) (getLong("alert_cooldown_ms", 1000) * m);
        long lastFlag = lastFlagTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L);

        if (buf >= threshold && (now - lastFlag) >= cooldown) {
            lastFlagTime.put(uuid, now);
            violations.flag(data, id().name() + " AIR", 1,
                    reason + " dy=" + fmt(dy) + " air=" + air + " buf=" + fmt(buf));
            buffer.put(uuid, Math.max(0, buf - getDouble("post_flag_reduce", 2.0)));
        }
    }

    private void checkWaterFly(PlayerData data, UUID uuid, long now, double dy, double hDist, double m, Player player, Location loc, World world) {
        // === EXEMPTIONS FOR WATER ===
        
        // Dolphin's Grace = very fast swimming, exempt
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.DOLPHINS_GRACE)) {
            resetWater(uuid);
            decay(uuid, 0.3);
            return;
        }
        
        // Conduit Power also affects water movement
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.CONDUIT_POWER)) {
            resetWater(uuid);
            decay(uuid, 0.2);
            return;
        }
        
        // Check for water currents (flowing water / waterfall)
        if (hasWaterCurrent(loc, world)) {
            resetWater(uuid);
            decay(uuid, 0.3);
            return;
        }
        
        // At water surface = bobbing is normal
        if (isAtWaterSurface(loc, world)) {
            resetWater(uuid);
            decay(uuid, 0.2);
            return;
        }
        
        // === WATER FLY DETECTION ===
        // In water without swimming: player should sink (~0.02 per tick)
        // BUT player can actively swim up by holding space - this is legit
        // Fly allows: hovering, impossibly fast movement, rising faster than swim allows
        
        // Check for Depth Strider
        int depthStrider = getDepthStriderLevel(player);
        double maxHSpeed = getDouble("water_max_h_speed", 0.18) + (depthStrider * 0.06);
        maxHSpeed *= m;
        
        // Max legit rise speed when swimming up: ~0.3 blocks/tick
        // With certain conditions can be slightly higher
        double maxRiseSpeed = getDouble("water_max_rise_speed", 0.35) * m;
        
        boolean suspicious = false;
        String reason = "";
        double weight = 0;
        
        // Detection 1: Hovering in deep water (very stable Y)
        // Normal: you sink, or swim which causes Y changes
        // Fly: perfectly stable Y
        double hoverThreshold = getDouble("water_hover_threshold", 0.005) * m;
        if (Math.abs(dy) < hoverThreshold && hDist < 0.02) {
            // Almost completely still - suspicious
            int stable = waterStableTicks.merge(uuid, 1, (a, b) -> a + b);
            int minStable = getInt("water_hover_min_ticks", 20);
            if (stable >= minStable) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason = "HOVER";
                weight = getDouble("water_hover_weight", 0.9);
            }
        } else {
            waterStableTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        }
        
        // Detection 2: Moving horizontally with unnaturally stable Y
        // Normal swimming: you move but Y varies (you sink a bit or swim up)
        // Fly: horizontal movement with perfectly stable Y (not sinking, not rising)
        if (!suspicious && hDist > getDouble("water_h_move_check_min", 0.06)) {
            double dyAbs = Math.abs(dy);
            // If moving horizontally but Y is suspiciously stable (not swimming up, not sinking)
            // Normal range: either sinking (dy < -0.01) or actively swimming up (dy > 0.03)
            if (dyAbs < getDouble("water_stable_while_moving", 0.015) * m) {
                int noChange = waterNoMoveTicks.merge(uuid, 1, (a, b) -> a + b);
                int minNoChange = getInt("water_no_y_change_min", 15);
                if (noChange >= minNoChange) {
                    suspicious = true;
                    reason = "STABLE_MOVE";
                    weight = getDouble("water_stable_move_weight", 0.8);
                }
            } else {
                // Y is changing (sinking or swimming up) - this is normal
                waterNoMoveTicks.put(uuid, 0);
            }
        }
        
        // Detection 3: Excessive horizontal speed in water
        if (!suspicious && hDist > maxHSpeed * 1.4) {
            suspicious = true;
            reason = "H_SPEED";
            weight = getDouble("water_speed_weight", 1.0);
        }
        
        // Detection 4: Rising faster than maximum swim speed
        // Normal swim up: max ~0.3-0.35 blocks/tick
        // Fly: can go faster
        if (!suspicious && dy > maxRiseSpeed) {
            suspicious = true;
            reason = "FAST_RISE";
            weight = getDouble("water_fast_rise_weight", 1.0);
        }
        
        if (!suspicious) {
            decay(uuid, 0.2);
            return;
        }
        
        // Add to buffer
        double buf = buffer.merge(uuid, weight, (a, b) -> a + b);

        double threshold = getDouble("water_threshold", 5.0);
        long cooldown = (long) (getLong("alert_cooldown_ms", 1200) * m);
        long lastFlag = lastFlagTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L);

        if (buf >= threshold && (now - lastFlag) >= cooldown) {
            lastFlagTime.put(uuid, now);
            violations.flag(data, id().name() + " WATER", 1,
                    reason + " dy=" + fmt(dy) + " hDist=" + fmt(hDist) + " buf=" + fmt(buf));
            buffer.put(uuid, Math.max(0, buf - getDouble("post_flag_reduce", 2.0)));
        }
    }
    
    private int getDepthStriderLevel(Player player) {
        var boots = player.getInventory().getBoots();
        if (boots == null) return 0;
        return boots.getEnchantmentLevel(org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.DEPTH_STRIDER);
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if there's flowing water (current) at the location
     */
    private boolean hasWaterCurrent(Location loc, World world) {
        int bx = loc.getBlockX();
        int by = loc.getBlockY();
        int bz = loc.getBlockZ();
        
        // Check surrounding water blocks for flow
        for (int ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) {
            for (int oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {
                for (int oz = -1; oz <= 1; oz++) {
                    Block b = world.getBlockAt(bx + ox, by + oy, bz + oz);
                    if (b.getType() == Material.WATER) {
                        BlockData bd = b.getBlockData();
                        if (bd instanceof Levelled) {
                            Levelled levelled = (Levelled) bd;
                            // Level 0 = source block, 1-7 = flowing, 8+ = falling
                            int level = levelled.getLevel();
                            if (level > 0) {
                                return true; // Flowing water detected
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if player is at water surface (bobbing area)
     */
    private boolean isAtWaterSurface(Location loc, World world) {
        int bx = loc.getBlockX();
        int by = loc.getBlockY();
        int bz = loc.getBlockZ();
        
        // Check if there's air above and water at/below player
        Block above = world.getBlockAt(bx, by + 1, bz);
        Block at = world.getBlockAt(bx, by, bz);
        Block below = world.getBlockAt(bx, by - 1, bz);
        
        boolean airAbove = above.getType() == Material.AIR || above.getType() == Material.CAVE_AIR;
        boolean waterAt = at.getType() == Material.WATER;
        boolean waterBelow = below.getType() == Material.WATER;
        
        // At surface = air above, water at feet or below
        if (airAbove && (waterAt || waterBelow)) {
            return true;
        }
        
        // Also check if player's head is just above water
        Block atHead = world.getBlockAt(bx, by + 1, bz);
        Block atFeet = world.getBlockAt(bx, by, bz);
        if (atHead.getType() != Material.WATER && atFeet.getType() == Material.WATER) {
            return true;
        }
        
        return false;
    }
    
    private void resetWater(UUID uuid) {
        waterStableTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        waterNoMoveTicks.put(uuid, 0);
    }

    private boolean isOnGround(Location loc, World world) {
        int bx = loc.getBlockX(), by = loc.getBlockY(), bz = loc.getBlockZ();
        for (int ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) {
            for (int oz = -1; oz <= 1; oz++) {
                if (world.getBlockAt(bx + ox, by - 1, bz + oz).getType().isSolid()) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean isInWater(Location loc, World world) {
        int bx = loc.getBlockX(), by = loc.getBlockY(), bz = loc.getBlockZ();
        for (int oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {
            if (world.getBlockAt(bx, by + oy, bz).getType() == Material.WATER) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean isInLava(Location loc, World world) {
        int bx = loc.getBlockX(), by = loc.getBlockY(), bz = loc.getBlockZ();
        for (int oy = -1; oy <= 1; oy++) {
            if (world.getBlockAt(bx, by + oy, bz).getType() == Material.LAVA) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private boolean hasClimbable(Location loc, World world) {
        Block at = world.getBlockAt(loc);
        Material mat = at.getType();
        return mat == Material.LADDER || mat == Material.VINE ||
               mat == Material.TWISTING_VINES || mat == Material.WEEPING_VINES ||
               mat == Material.SCAFFOLDING;
    }

    private void decay(UUID uuid, double amount) {
        buffer.merge(uuid, -amount, (old, delta) -> Math.max(0, old + delta));
    }

    private void reset(UUID uuid) {
        airTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        stableAirTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        waterStableTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        waterNoMoveTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        buffer.put(uuid, 0.0);
    }

    @Override
    public void onQuit(UUID uuid) {
        lastX.remove(uuid);
        lastY.remove(uuid);
        lastZ.remove(uuid);
        lastPacketTime.remove(uuid);
        airTicks.remove(uuid);
        stableAirTicks.remove(uuid);
        waterStableTicks.remove(uuid);
        waterNoMoveTicks.remove(uuid);
        buffer.remove(uuid);
        lastFlagTime.remove(uuid);
        lastDy.remove(uuid);
        elytraEndTime.remove(uuid);
    }

    private double getDouble(String key, double def) {
        return configs.checks().getDouble("checks.FLY.params." + key, def);
    }

    private int getInt(String key, int def) {
        return configs.checks().getInt("checks.FLY.params." + key, def);
    }

    private long getLong(String key, long def) {
        return configs.checks().getLong("checks.FLY.params." + key, def);
    }

    private static String fmt(double d) {
        return String.format("%.3f", d);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/impl/JesusCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.LagCompensation;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * JESUS - Water Walking Detection
 * Detects players walking on water without sinking
 */
public final class JesusCheck implements Check {

    private final Configs configs;
    private final ViolationService violations;
    private final LagCompensation lag;

    private volatile boolean enabled = true;

    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastY = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastPacketTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> flatTicks = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> buffer = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastFlagTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public JesusCheck(Configs configs, ViolationService violations, LagCompensation lag) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.violations = violations;
        this.lag = lag;
    }

    @Override
    public CheckId id() {
        return CheckId.JESUS;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        this.enabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.JESUS.enabled", true);
    }

    @Override
    public void onPacketReceive(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        if (!WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying.isFlying(event.getPacketType())) return;

        Player player = data.player();
        if (player == null || !player.isOnline()) return;

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Exemptions
        if (data.isExempt() || player.isFlying() || player.getAllowFlight() ||
            player.isInsideVehicle() || player.isRiptiding() || player.isSwimming()) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Parse packet
        WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying wrapper;
        try {
            wrapper = new WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return;
        }
        if (!wrapper.hasPositionChanged()) return;

        double x = wrapper.getLocation().getX();
        double y = wrapper.getLocation().getY();
        double z = wrapper.getLocation().getZ();
        boolean onGround = wrapper.isOnGround();

        Double prevY = lastY.put(uuid, y);
        Long prevTime = lastPacketTime.put(uuid, now);
        if (prevY == null || prevTime == null) return;

        double dy = y - prevY;
        long dt = now - prevTime;
        double m = lag.multiplier(player);

        if (dt < 10 || dt > 300) return;

        World world = player.getWorld();
        Location loc = new Location(world, x, y, z);

        // Check water conditions
        WaterInfo water = checkWater(loc, world);
        
        if (!water.nearWater) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Frost Walker exemption
        if (hasFrostWalker(player)) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Has solid support = legit
        if (water.hasSolidBelow) {
            decay(uuid, 0.3);
            return;
        }

        // Detection: onGround=true on water OR very stable Y on water surface
        boolean suspicious = false;
        String reason = "";

        // Check 1: onGround=true while on water
        if (onGround && water.atSurface) {
            suspicious = true;
            reason = "GROUND_ON_WATER";
        }

        // Check 2: Stable Y on water surface
        if (!suspicious && water.atSurface) {
            double dyAbs = Math.abs(dy);
            if (dyAbs < 0.005 * m) {
                int flat = flatTicks.merge(uuid, 1, (a, b) -> a + b);
                if (flat >= getInt("min_flat_ticks", 8)) {
                    suspicious = true;
                    reason = "FLAT_Y";
                }
            } else {
                flatTicks.put(uuid, 0);
            }
        }

        if (!suspicious) {
            decay(uuid, 0.2);
            return;
        }

        // Add to buffer
        double weight = getDouble("weight", 1.2);
        double buf = buffer.merge(uuid, weight, (a, b) -> a + b);

        double threshold = getDouble("threshold", 3.0);
        long cooldown = (long) (getLong("alert_cooldown_ms", 1200) * m);
        long lastFlag = lastFlagTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L);

        if (buf >= threshold && (now - lastFlag) >= cooldown) {
            lastFlagTime.put(uuid, now);
            violations.flag(data, id().name(), 1,
                    reason + " dy=" + fmt(dy) + " ground=" + onGround + " buf=" + fmt(buf));
            buffer.put(uuid, Math.max(0, buf - getDouble("post_flag_reduce", 1.5)));
        }
    }

    private WaterInfo checkWater(Location loc, World world) {
        WaterInfo info = new WaterInfo();
        
        int bx = loc.getBlockX();
        int by = loc.getBlockY();
        int bz = loc.getBlockZ();
        double fracY = loc.getY() - by;

        Block atFeet = world.getBlockAt(bx, by, bz);
        Block below = world.getBlockAt(bx, by - 1, bz);
        Block above = world.getBlockAt(bx, by + 1, bz);

        boolean waterAtFeet = isWater(atFeet.getType());
        boolean waterBelow = isWater(below.getType());

        info.nearWater = waterAtFeet || waterBelow;
        if (!info.nearWater) return info;

        boolean airAbove = above.getType().isAir() || above.getType() == Material.LILY_PAD;

        // At surface check
        if (waterBelow && fracY >= 0.0 && airAbove) {
            info.atSurface = true;
        } else if (waterAtFeet && fracY >= 0.8 && airAbove) {
            info.atSurface = true;
        }

        // Check solid support
        for (int ox = -1; ox <= 1; ox++) {
            for (int oz = -1; oz <= 1; oz++) {
                Block check = world.getBlockAt(bx + ox, by - 1, bz + oz);
                Material mat = check.getType();
                if (mat.isSolid() && !isWater(mat) && mat != Material.LAVA) {
                    info.hasSolidBelow = true;
                    break;
                }
                Block checkAbove = world.getBlockAt(bx + ox, by, bz + oz);
                if (checkAbove.getType() == Material.LILY_PAD) {
                    info.hasSolidBelow = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        return info;
    }

    private static class WaterInfo {
        boolean nearWater = false;
        boolean atSurface = false;
        boolean hasSolidBelow = false;
    }

    private boolean isWater(Material mat) {
        return mat == Material.WATER;
    }

    private boolean hasFrostWalker(Player player) {
        var boots = player.getInventory().getBoots();
        if (boots == null) return false;
        return boots.containsEnchantment(Enchantment.FROST_WALKER);
    }

    private void decay(UUID uuid, double amount) {
        buffer.merge(uuid, -amount, (old, delta) -> Math.max(0, old + delta));
    }

    private void reset(UUID uuid) {
        flatTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        buffer.put(uuid, 0.0);
    }

    @Override
    public void onQuit(UUID uuid) {
        lastY.remove(uuid);
        lastPacketTime.remove(uuid);
        flatTicks.remove(uuid);
        buffer.remove(uuid);
        lastFlagTime.remove(uuid);
    }

    private double getDouble(String key, double def) {
        return configs.checks().getDouble("checks.JESUS.params." + key, def);
    }

    private int getInt(String key, int def) {
        return configs.checks().getInt("checks.JESUS.params." + key, def);
    }

    private long getLong(String key, long def) {
        return configs.checks().getLong("checks.JESUS.params." + key, def);
    }

    private static String fmt(double d) {
        return String.format("%.4f", d);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/impl/KillAuraCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.packettype.PacketType;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientInteractEntity;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.LagCompensation;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.GameMode;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * KILLAURA - Combat Hack Detection (Packet-Based, Async-Optimized)
 * 
 * Sub-checks:
 * - REACH: Detects hits from distances beyond vanilla 3.0 blocks
 * - ANGLE: Detects hits when player is not looking at target
 * - NOSWING: Detects hits without arm swing animation packet
 * - GCD: Detects rotations that bypass Minecraft's GCD (sensitivity divisor)
 * - MULTIAURA: Detects hitting multiple entities in impossible timeframes
 * - PATTERN: Detects robotic consistency in rotation/timing patterns
 * 
 * All thresholds and messages are configurable.
 * Lag compensation is applied to all distance/timing calculations.
 */
public final class KillAuraCheck implements Check {

    // Sub-check types for specific alerts
    public enum SubCheck {
        REACH("Reach", "Hit distance exceeds maximum"),
        ANGLE("Angle", "Hit direction doesn't match aim"),
        NOSWING("NoSwing", "Attack without swing animation"),
        GCD("GCD", "Invalid rotation sensitivity pattern"),
        MULTIAURA("MultiAura", "Multiple targets in short timeframe"),
        PATTERN("Pattern", "Robotic rotation consistency");
        
        private final String name;
        private final String description;
        
        SubCheck(String name, String description) {
            this.name = name;
            this.description = description;
        }
        
        public String getName() { return name; }
        public String getDescription() { return description; }
    }

    private final Configs configs;
    private final ViolationService violations;
    private final LagCompensation lag;
    
    private Plugin plugin;
    private volatile boolean enabled = true;
    
    // Sub-check enable flags
    private volatile boolean reachEnabled = true;
    private volatile boolean angleEnabled = true;
    private volatile boolean noSwingEnabled = true;
    private volatile boolean gcdEnabled = true;
    private volatile boolean multiAuraEnabled = true;
    private volatile boolean patternEnabled = true;

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // TRACKING DATA (all ConcurrentHashMap for thread safety)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    
    // Rotation tracking (from packets)
    private final Map<UUID, Float> lastYaw = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Float> lastPitch = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastRotationTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, List<Float>> recentYawChanges = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, List<Float>> recentPitchChanges = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    // Position tracking (from packets)
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastX = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastY = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastZ = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    // Swing tracking
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastSwingTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    // Hit tracking
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastHitTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> lastHitEntityId = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, List<Long>> recentHitTimes = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Set<Integer>> entitiesHitInWindow = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    // GCD analysis
    private final Map<UUID, List<Double>> gcdSamples = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private static final double MINECRAFT_GCD = 0.15; // Minecraft's rotation GCD factor
    
    // Buffer tracking per sub-check
    private final Map<UUID, Map<SubCheck, Double>> buffers = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    // Alert cooldowns per sub-check
    private final Map<UUID, Map<SubCheck, Long>> lastAlertTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public KillAuraCheck(Configs configs, ViolationService violations, LagCompensation lag) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.violations = violations;
        this.lag = lag;
    }
    
    public void setPlugin(Plugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }

    @Override
    public CheckId id() {
        return CheckId.KILLAURA;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        this.enabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.KILLAURA.enabled", true);
        
        // Load sub-check enables
        this.reachEnabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.reach.enabled", true);
        this.angleEnabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.angle.enabled", true);
        this.noSwingEnabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.noswing.enabled", true);
        this.gcdEnabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.gcd.enabled", true);
        this.multiAuraEnabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.multiaura.enabled", true);
        this.patternEnabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.pattern.enabled", true);
    }

    @Override
    public void onPacketReceive(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        
        Player player = data.player();
        if (player == null || !player.isOnline()) return;
        if (data.isExempt()) return;
        
        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        
        // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // SWING ARM PACKET - Track swing timing
        // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        if (event.getPacketType() == PacketType.Play.Client.ANIMATION) {
            lastSwingTime.put(uuid, now);
            return;
        }
        
        // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // FLYING PACKET - Track rotation and position
        // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        if (WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying.isFlying(event.getPacketType())) {
            WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying wrapper;
            try {
                wrapper = new WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return;
            }
            
            // Track position from packet
            if (wrapper.hasPositionChanged()) {
                lastX.put(uuid, wrapper.getLocation().getX());
                lastY.put(uuid, wrapper.getLocation().getY());
                lastZ.put(uuid, wrapper.getLocation().getZ());
            }
            
            // Track rotation from packet
            if (wrapper.hasRotationChanged()) {
                float newYaw = wrapper.getLocation().getYaw();
                float newPitch = wrapper.getLocation().getPitch();
                
                Float oldYaw = lastYaw.get(uuid);
                Float oldPitch = lastPitch.get(uuid);
                
                if (oldYaw != null && oldPitch != null) {
                    float deltaYaw = Math.abs(wrapAngle(newYaw - oldYaw));
                    float deltaPitch = Math.abs(newPitch - oldPitch);
                    
                    // Store recent rotation changes for pattern analysis
                    addToRotationHistory(uuid, deltaYaw, deltaPitch);
                    
                    // GCD Analysis - ONLY during active combat (within 3 seconds of attack)
                    // This prevents false positives from normal mouse movement
                    Long lastHit = lastHitTime.get(uuid);
                    boolean inCombat = lastHit != null && (now - lastHit) < 3000;
                    
                    if (gcdEnabled && inCombat && (deltaYaw > 0.1 || deltaPitch > 0.1)) {
                        analyzeGCD(uuid, deltaYaw, deltaPitch, player, now);
                    }
                }
                
                lastYaw.put(uuid, newYaw);
                lastPitch.put(uuid, newPitch);
                lastRotationTime.put(uuid, now);
            }
            return;
        }
        
        // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        // INTERACT ENTITY PACKET - Main combat analysis
        // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
        if (event.getPacketType() == PacketType.Play.Client.INTERACT_ENTITY) {
            WrapperPlayClientInteractEntity wrapper;
            try {
                wrapper = new WrapperPlayClientInteractEntity(event);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return;
            }
            
            // Only check ATTACK action
            if (wrapper.getAction() != WrapperPlayClientInteractEntity.InteractAction.ATTACK) {
                return;
            }
            
            int entityId = wrapper.getEntityId();
            
            // Get lag multiplier for this player
            double lagMult = lag.multiplier(player);
            
            // Schedule entity lookup and checks on main thread
            scheduleSync(() -> {
                Entity target = findEntityById(player.getWorld(), entityId);
                if (target == null || !(target instanceof LivingEntity)) return;
                if (target.equals(player)) return; // Self-hit
                
                LivingEntity livingTarget = (LivingEntity) target;
                
                // Get stored packet position (more accurate than player.getLocation())
                Double px = lastX.get(uuid);
                Double py = lastY.get(uuid);
                Double pz = lastZ.get(uuid);
                
                if (px == null || py == null || pz == null) {
                    // Fallback to player location
                    Location loc = player.getLocation();
                    px = loc.getX();
                    py = loc.getY();
                    pz = loc.getZ();
                }
                
                // Create position vectors
                Vector playerPos = new Vector(px, py + player.getEyeHeight(), pz);
                Vector targetPos = target.getLocation().toVector().add(new Vector(0, livingTarget.getEyeHeight() * 0.5, 0));
                
                // Run all sub-checks
                checkReach(uuid, player, playerPos, targetPos, lagMult, now);
                checkAngle(uuid, player, playerPos, targetPos, lagMult, now);
                checkNoSwing(uuid, player, now);
                checkMultiAura(uuid, player, entityId, now);
                checkPattern(uuid, player, now);
                
                // Update hit tracking
                lastHitTime.put(uuid, now);
                lastHitEntityId.put(uuid, entityId);
                addHitToHistory(uuid, now);
            });
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // SUB-CHECK: REACH
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private void checkReach(UUID uuid, Player player, Vector playerPos, Vector targetPos, double lagMult, long now) {
        if (!reachEnabled) return;
        if (isSubCheckIgnoredForMode(player, SubCheck.REACH)) return;
        
        double distance = playerPos.distance(targetPos);

        // Base max reach + lag compensation
        double maxReach = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.REACH, "max_distance", 3.0);
        double lagBuffer = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.REACH, "lag_buffer", 0.3);
        double allowedReach = maxReach + (lagBuffer * lagMult);

        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.CREATIVE) {
            double creativeMaxReach = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.REACH, "creative_max_distance", 5.0);
            double creativeReach = creativeMaxReach + (lagBuffer * lagMult);
            allowedReach = Math.max(allowedReach, creativeReach);
        }

        if (distance > allowedReach) {
            double excess = distance - allowedReach;
            double weight = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.REACH, "weight", 1.0);
            double threshold = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.REACH, "threshold", 3.0);
            
            // Weight increases with excess distance
            double points = weight * (1 + excess);
            
            if (addBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.REACH, points, threshold)) {
                String detail = String.format("dist=%.2f max=%.2f", distance, allowedReach);
                flagSubCheck(player, SubCheck.REACH, detail, now);
            }
        } else {
            // Decay buffer
            double decay = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.REACH, "decay", 0.5);
            decayBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.REACH, decay);
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // SUB-CHECK: ANGLE
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private void checkAngle(UUID uuid, Player player, Vector playerPos, Vector targetPos, double lagMult, long now) {
        if (!angleEnabled) return;
        if (isSubCheckIgnoredForMode(player, SubCheck.ANGLE)) return;
        
        Float yaw = lastYaw.get(uuid);
        Float pitch = lastPitch.get(uuid);
        if (yaw == null || pitch == null) return;
        
        // Calculate direction player is looking
        Vector lookDir = getDirectionFromRotation(yaw, pitch);
        
        // Calculate direction to target
        Vector toTarget = targetPos.clone().subtract(playerPos).normalize();
        
        // Calculate angle between look direction and target direction
        double angle = Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, lookDir.dot(toTarget)))));
        
        // Max allowed angle + lag compensation
        double maxAngle = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.ANGLE, "max_angle", 60.0);
        double lagAngleBuffer = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.ANGLE, "lag_buffer", 10.0);
        double allowedAngle = maxAngle + (lagAngleBuffer * lagMult);
        
        if (angle > allowedAngle) {
            double excess = angle - allowedAngle;
            double weight = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.ANGLE, "weight", 1.5);
            double threshold = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.ANGLE, "threshold", 4.0);
            
            double points = weight * (1 + excess / 30.0);
            
            if (addBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.ANGLE, points, threshold)) {
                String detail = String.format("angle=%.1f° max=%.1f°", angle, allowedAngle);
                flagSubCheck(player, SubCheck.ANGLE, detail, now);
            }
        } else {
            double decay = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.ANGLE, "decay", 0.4);
            decayBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.ANGLE, decay);
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // SUB-CHECK: NOSWING
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private void checkNoSwing(UUID uuid, Player player, long now) {
        if (!noSwingEnabled) return;
        if (isSubCheckIgnoredForMode(player, SubCheck.NOSWING)) return;
        
        Long lastSwing = lastSwingTime.get(uuid);
        long maxSwingDelay = getModeLong(player, SubCheck.NOSWING, "max_delay_ms", 100);
        
        boolean hasRecentSwing = lastSwing != null && (now - lastSwing) <= maxSwingDelay;
        
        if (!hasRecentSwing) {
            double weight = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.NOSWING, "weight", 2.0);
            double threshold = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.NOSWING, "threshold", 4.0);
            
            if (addBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.NOSWING, weight, threshold)) {
                long timeSinceSwing = lastSwing == null ? -1 : (now - lastSwing);
                String detail = String.format("no_swing_in=%dms", timeSinceSwing);
                flagSubCheck(player, SubCheck.NOSWING, detail, now);
            }
        } else {
            double decay = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.NOSWING, "decay", 1.0);
            decayBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.NOSWING, decay);
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // SUB-CHECK: GCD (Rotation Sensitivity)
    // Only runs during active combat to avoid false positives
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private void analyzeGCD(UUID uuid, float deltaYaw, float deltaPitch, Player player, long now) {
        if (isSubCheckIgnoredForMode(player, SubCheck.GCD)) return;
        List<Double> samples = gcdSamples.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> new ArrayList<>());

        // Calculate GCD of the rotation
        double gcd = gcd(deltaYaw, deltaPitch);
        
        // Filter out very small rotations (likely normal aiming)
        if (gcd < 0.001) return;
        
        samples.add(gcd);
        
        // Keep only recent samples
        int maxSamples = getModeInt(player, SubCheck.GCD, "sample_size", 30);
        while (samples.size() > maxSamples) {
            samples.remove(0);
        }
        
        // Need more samples for accurate analysis (reduces false positives)
        int minSamples = getModeInt(player, SubCheck.GCD, "min_samples", 20);
        if (samples.size() < minSamples) return;
        
        // Analyze GCD consistency
        double avgGcd = samples.stream().mapToDouble(Double::doubleValue).average().orElse(0);
        double variance = samples.stream().mapToDouble(d -> Math.pow(d - avgGcd, 2)).average().orElse(0);
        double stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);
        
        // Valid Minecraft rotations have some natural variance
        // Aimbots have extremely low GCD and very consistent (low variance)
        double minValidGcd = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.GCD, "min_valid_gcd", 0.005);
        double maxStdDev = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.GCD, "max_std_dev", 0.0005);
        
        // Flag only if GCD is suspiciously low AND variance is robotically consistent
        boolean invalidGcd = avgGcd < minValidGcd && stdDev < maxStdDev;
        
        if (invalidGcd) {
            double weight = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.GCD, "weight", 1.0);
            double threshold = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.GCD, "threshold", 6.0);
            
            if (addBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.GCD, weight, threshold)) {
                String detail = String.format("gcd=%.4f stdDev=%.6f", avgGcd, stdDev);
                scheduleSync(() -> flagSubCheck(player, SubCheck.GCD, detail, now));
                // Clear samples after flag to require fresh evidence
                samples.clear();
            }
        } else {
            double decay = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.GCD, "decay", 0.5);
            decayBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.GCD, decay);
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // SUB-CHECK: MULTIAURA
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private void checkMultiAura(UUID uuid, Player player, int entityId, long now) {
        if (!multiAuraEnabled) return;
        if (isSubCheckIgnoredForMode(player, SubCheck.MULTIAURA)) return;
        
        long windowMs = getModeLong(player, SubCheck.MULTIAURA, "window_ms", 500);
        int maxTargets = getModeInt(player, SubCheck.MULTIAURA, "max_targets", 2);
        
        Set<Integer> entities = entitiesHitInWindow.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet());
        
        // Clean old entities from window
        Long lastHit = lastHitTime.get(uuid);
        if (lastHit != null && (now - lastHit) > windowMs) {
            entities.clear();
        }
        
        entities.add(entityId);
        
        if (entities.size() > maxTargets) {
            double weight = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.MULTIAURA, "weight", 2.0);
            double threshold = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.MULTIAURA, "threshold", 3.0);
            
            if (addBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.MULTIAURA, weight, threshold)) {
                String detail = String.format("targets=%d in %dms", entities.size(), windowMs);
                flagSubCheck(player, SubCheck.MULTIAURA, detail, now);
            }
            entities.clear();
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // SUB-CHECK: PATTERN (Rotation Consistency)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private void checkPattern(UUID uuid, Player player, long now) {
        if (!patternEnabled) return;
        if (isSubCheckIgnoredForMode(player, SubCheck.PATTERN)) return;
        
        List<Float> yawChanges = recentYawChanges.get(uuid);
        List<Float> pitchChanges = recentPitchChanges.get(uuid);
        
        if (yawChanges == null || pitchChanges == null) return;
        
        int minSamples = getModeInt(player, SubCheck.PATTERN, "min_samples", 10);
        if (yawChanges.size() < minSamples) return;
        
        // Calculate variance of rotation changes
        double yawVariance = calculateVariance(yawChanges);
        double pitchVariance = calculateVariance(pitchChanges);
        
        // Humans have natural variance, bots are consistent
        double minVariance = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.PATTERN, "min_variance", 0.5);
        
        // Both yaw AND pitch must be robotic
        boolean robotic = yawVariance < minVariance && pitchVariance < minVariance;
        
        // Also check if rotations are "too perfect" (exactly same values repeating)
        boolean repeating = hasRepeatingPattern(yawChanges) || hasRepeatingPattern(pitchChanges);
        
        if (robotic || repeating) {
            double weight = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.PATTERN, "weight", 1.0);
            double threshold = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.PATTERN, "threshold", 5.0);
            
            if (addBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.PATTERN, weight, threshold)) {
                String detail = String.format("yaw_var=%.3f pitch_var=%.3f repeat=%s", 
                    yawVariance, pitchVariance, repeating);
                flagSubCheck(player, SubCheck.PATTERN, detail, now);
            }
        } else {
            double decay = getModeDouble(player, SubCheck.PATTERN, "decay", 0.2);
            decayBuffer(uuid, SubCheck.PATTERN, decay);
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // HELPER METHODS
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    private boolean isSubCheckIgnoredForMode(Player player, SubCheck subCheck) {
        if (player == null) return false;
        // Always ignore in spectator
        if (player.getGameMode() == GameMode.SPECTATOR) return true;

        String key = String.format("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.%s.ignore_in_modes", subCheck.name().toLowerCase());
        java.util.List<String> modes = configs.checks().getStringList(key);
        if (modes == null || modes.isEmpty()) return false;
        String current = player.getGameMode().name();
        for (String m : modes) {
            if (m == null) continue;
            if (m.trim().equalsIgnoreCase(current)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    private double getModeDouble(Player player, SubCheck sub, String key, double def) {
        if (player == null) return def;
        String mode = player.getGameMode().name();
        String modePath = String.format("checks.KILLAURA.mode_overrides.%s.sub_checks.%s.%s", mode, sub.name().toLowerCase(), key);
        double v = configs.checks().getDouble(modePath, Double.NaN);
        if (!Double.isNaN(v)) return v;
        return configs.checks().getDouble(String.format("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.%s.%s", sub.name().toLowerCase(), key), def);
    }

    private int getModeInt(Player player, SubCheck sub, String key, int def) {
        if (player == null) return def;
        String mode = player.getGameMode().name();
        String modePath = String.format("checks.KILLAURA.mode_overrides.%s.sub_checks.%s.%s", mode, sub.name().toLowerCase(), key);
        int v = configs.checks().getInt(modePath, Integer.MIN_VALUE);
        if (v != Integer.MIN_VALUE) return v;
        return configs.checks().getInt(String.format("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.%s.%s", sub.name().toLowerCase(), key), def);
    }

    private long getModeLong(Player player, SubCheck sub, String key, long def) {
        if (player == null) return def;
        String mode = player.getGameMode().name();
        String modePath = String.format("checks.KILLAURA.mode_overrides.%s.sub_checks.%s.%s", mode, sub.name().toLowerCase(), key);
        long v = configs.checks().getLong(modePath, Long.MIN_VALUE);
        if (v != Long.MIN_VALUE) return v;
        return configs.checks().getLong(String.format("checks.KILLAURA.sub_checks.%s.%s", sub.name().toLowerCase(), key), def);
    }
    
    private void flagSubCheck(Player player, SubCheck subCheck, String detail, long now) {
        // Check alert cooldown for this specific sub-check
        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        Map<SubCheck, Long> cooldowns = lastAlertTime.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> new ConcurrentHashMap<>());
        
        long cooldownMs = configs.checks().getLong("checks.KILLAURA.alert_cooldown_ms", 1000);
        Long lastAlert = cooldowns.get(subCheck);
        
        if (lastAlert != null && (now - lastAlert) < cooldownMs) {
            return;
        }
        
        cooldowns.put(subCheck, now);
        
        // Format: "KillAura (Reach)" or "KillAura (NoSwing)" etc.
        String checkName = String.format("KillAura (%s)", subCheck.getName());
        violations.flag(player, checkName, 1, detail);
        
        // Reset buffer after flag
        double postFlagReduce = configs.checks().getDouble("checks.KILLAURA.post_flag_reduce", 2.0);
        Map<SubCheck, Double> playerBuffers = buffers.get(uuid);
        if (playerBuffers != null) {
            playerBuffers.merge(subCheck, 0.0, (old, v) -> Math.max(0, old - postFlagReduce));
        }
    }
    
    private boolean addBuffer(UUID uuid, SubCheck subCheck, double amount, double threshold) {
        Map<SubCheck, Double> playerBuffers = buffers.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> new ConcurrentHashMap<>());
        @SuppressWarnings("null")
        double newValue = playerBuffers.merge(subCheck, amount, Double::sum);
        return newValue >= threshold;
    }
    
    private void decayBuffer(UUID uuid, SubCheck subCheck, double amount) {
        Map<SubCheck, Double> playerBuffers = buffers.get(uuid);
        if (playerBuffers != null) {
            playerBuffers.merge(subCheck, 0.0, (old, v) -> Math.max(0, old - amount));
        }
    }
    
    private void addToRotationHistory(UUID uuid, float deltaYaw, float deltaPitch) {
        int maxHistory = 20;
        
        List<Float> yawList = recentYawChanges.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>()));
        List<Float> pitchList = recentPitchChanges.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>()));
        
        synchronized (yawList) {
            yawList.add(deltaYaw);
            while (yawList.size() > maxHistory) yawList.remove(0);
        }
        
        synchronized (pitchList) {
            pitchList.add(deltaPitch);
            while (pitchList.size() > maxHistory) pitchList.remove(0);
        }
    }
    
    private void addHitToHistory(UUID uuid, long time) {
        List<Long> hits = recentHitTimes.computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>()));
        synchronized (hits) {
            hits.add(time);
            // Keep last 20 hits
            while (hits.size() > 20) hits.remove(0);
        }
    }
    
    private Entity findEntityById(org.bukkit.World world, int entityId) {
        for (Entity entity : world.getEntities()) {
            if (entity.getEntityId() == entityId) {
                return entity;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    
    private Vector getDirectionFromRotation(float yaw, float pitch) {
        double yawRad = Math.toRadians(yaw);
        double pitchRad = Math.toRadians(pitch);
        
        double x = -Math.sin(yawRad) * Math.cos(pitchRad);
        double y = -Math.sin(pitchRad);
        double z = Math.cos(yawRad) * Math.cos(pitchRad);
        
        return new Vector(x, y, z).normalize();
    }
    
    private float wrapAngle(float angle) {
        angle = angle % 360.0f;
        if (angle >= 180.0f) angle -= 360.0f;
        if (angle < -180.0f) angle += 360.0f;
        return angle;
    }
    
    private double gcd(double a, double b) {
        a = Math.abs(a);
        b = Math.abs(b);
        
        while (b > 0.0001) {
            double temp = b;
            b = a % b;
            a = temp;
        }
        return a;
    }
    
    private double calculateVariance(List<Float> values) {
        if (values == null || values.isEmpty()) return Double.MAX_VALUE;
        
        double mean;
        double sumSquares;
        
        synchronized (values) {
            if (values.isEmpty()) return Double.MAX_VALUE;
            mean = values.stream().mapToDouble(Float::doubleValue).average().orElse(0);
            sumSquares = values.stream().mapToDouble(v -> Math.pow(v - mean, 2)).sum();
        }
        
        return sumSquares / values.size();
    }
    
    private boolean hasRepeatingPattern(List<Float> values) {
        if (values == null || values.size() < 4) return false;
        
        synchronized (values) {
            int repeats = 0;
            for (int i = 1; i < values.size(); i++) {
                if (Math.abs(values.get(i) - values.get(i-1)) < 0.001) {
                    repeats++;
                }
            }
            // More than 50% identical = suspicious
            return repeats > values.size() / 2;
        }
    }
    
    private void scheduleSync(Runnable task) {
        if (plugin != null && plugin.isEnabled()) {
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, task);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onQuit(UUID uuid) {
        lastYaw.remove(uuid);
        lastPitch.remove(uuid);
        lastRotationTime.remove(uuid);
        recentYawChanges.remove(uuid);
        recentPitchChanges.remove(uuid);
        lastX.remove(uuid);
        lastY.remove(uuid);
        lastZ.remove(uuid);
        lastSwingTime.remove(uuid);
        lastHitTime.remove(uuid);
        lastHitEntityId.remove(uuid);
        recentHitTimes.remove(uuid);
        entitiesHitInWindow.remove(uuid);
        gcdSamples.remove(uuid);
        buffers.remove(uuid);
        lastAlertTime.remove(uuid);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/impl/NoFallCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.LagCompensation;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.data.Waterlogged;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;

/**
 * NOFALL - NoFall Hack Detection (Packet + Damage Event Based)
 * 
 * ASYNC-OPTIMIZED: Block checks are cached and updated periodically via main thread
 * to avoid blocking the packet processing thread.
 * 
 * Detection Method:
 * 1. Track real fall distance via packets (Y position changes)
 * 2. When player lands from height that should cause damage, expect EntityDamageEvent(FALL)
 * 3. If no fall damage event received within grace period -> NoFall hack detected
 * 
 * Legitimate exemptions:
 * - Landing in water/lava/bubble column/waterlogged blocks
 * - Landing on slime/hay/honey/bed/powder snow
 * - Climbing (ladder, vine, scaffolding)
 * - Mace hit during fall (negates fall damage in 1.21+)
 * - Slow Falling / Levitation effects
 * - Elytra gliding / Riptide / Swimming
 * - Creative/Spectator/Flying
 */
public final class NoFallCheck implements Check {

    // Blocks that completely negate fall damage
    private static final Set<Material> FALL_NEGATING_BLOCKS = Set.of(
        Material.SLIME_BLOCK,
        Material.HAY_BLOCK,
        Material.HONEY_BLOCK,
        Material.POWDER_SNOW,
        Material.COBWEB
    );
    
    // Climbable blocks
    private static final Set<Material> CLIMBABLE_BLOCKS = Set.of(
        Material.LADDER, Material.VINE, Material.SCAFFOLDING,
        Material.CAVE_VINES, Material.CAVE_VINES_PLANT,
        Material.WEEPING_VINES, Material.WEEPING_VINES_PLANT,
        Material.TWISTING_VINES, Material.TWISTING_VINES_PLANT
    );
    
    // Beds (bounce)
    private static final Set<Material> BEDS = Set.of(
        Material.WHITE_BED, Material.ORANGE_BED, Material.MAGENTA_BED,
        Material.LIGHT_BLUE_BED, Material.YELLOW_BED, Material.LIME_BED,
        Material.PINK_BED, Material.GRAY_BED, Material.LIGHT_GRAY_BED,
        Material.CYAN_BED, Material.PURPLE_BED, Material.BLUE_BED,
        Material.BROWN_BED, Material.GREEN_BED, Material.RED_BED,
        Material.BLACK_BED
    );

    private Configs configs;
    private ViolationService violations;
    private LagCompensation lag;

    // Async executor for non-blocking operations (scheduled)
    private final java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService asyncExecutor = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor();

    // Plugin reference for scheduling sync tasks
    private Plugin plugin;

    private volatile boolean enabled = true;

    // Fall tracking (from packet coordinates - no Bukkit API needed)
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastX = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastY = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastZ = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> fallStartY = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> maxFallDistance = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Boolean> isFalling = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    // Expectation / damage tracking
    private final Map<UUID, Long> expectingDamageUntil = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> expectedFallDistance = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> expectedDamageAmount = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Boolean> expectingDripstone = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Boolean> receivedFallDamage = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastDamageTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastFlagTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Boolean> hitWithMaceDuringFall = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastMaceHitTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastGlideTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private boolean debugLogs = false;

    // Block exemption cache (updated via scheduled main thread task)
```
