```java
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastBlockCheckTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Boolean> isNearExemptBlock = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    // Cache whether there's pointed dripstone under last known position
    private final Map<UUID, Boolean> hasDripstoneBelow = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private static final long BLOCK_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 100; // Check blocks every 100ms
    private org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask cacheUpdaterTask = null;
    
    public NoFallCheck(Configs configs, ViolationService violations, LagCompensation lag) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.violations = violations;
        this.lag = lag;
        if (this.configs != null) {
            this.debugLogs = this.configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.NOFALL.params.debug_logs", false);
        }
    }

    public void setPlugin(Plugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        // Start a lightweight main-thread cache updater for block state under players.
        try {
            if (this.cacheUpdaterTask != null) {
                this.cacheUpdaterTask.cancel();
            }
            if (this.plugin != null) {
                this.cacheUpdaterTask = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(this.plugin, () -> {
                    try {
                        for (UUID uuid : lastX.keySet()) {
                            Player p = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);
                            if (p == null || !p.isOnline()) continue;
                            Double lx = lastX.get(uuid);
                            Double ly = lastY.get(uuid);
                            Double lz = lastZ.get(uuid);
                            if (lx == null || ly == null || lz == null) continue;
                            int bx = (int) Math.floor(lx);
                            int by = (int) Math.floor(ly);
                            int bz = (int) Math.floor(lz);
                            World w = p.getWorld();
                            try {
                                Block below = w.getBlockAt(bx, by - 1, bz);
                                Material mat = below.getType();
                                hasDripstoneBelow.put(uuid, mat == Material.POINTED_DRIPSTONE);
                                boolean exempt = checkExemptBlocks(w, bx, by, bz);
                                isNearExemptBlock.put(uuid, exempt);
                            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                        }
                    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                }, 1L, 2L);
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }
    
    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    @Override
    public void onPacketReceive(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        if (data == null) return;
        Player player = data.player();
        if (player == null) return;

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Only interested in player position packets
        if (!WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying.isFlying(event.getPacketType())) return;

        // NOTE: expiry checks are scheduled when expectations are set to avoid races.
        // === BASIC EXEMPTIONS (player state - some need main thread) ===
        if (shouldExemptCompletely(data, player)) {
            resetFall(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Parse packet (no Bukkit API, pure PacketEvents)
        WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying wrapper;
        try {
            wrapper = new WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return;
        }

        if (!wrapper.hasPositionChanged()) return;

        // Get position from PACKET (not player.getLocation() which needs sync)
        double newX = wrapper.getLocation().getX();
        double newY = wrapper.getLocation().getY();
        double newZ = wrapper.getLocation().getZ();
        boolean clientOnGround = wrapper.isOnGround();
        
        Double prevY = lastY.get(uuid);

        // Store packet position
        lastX.put(uuid, newX);
        lastY.put(uuid, newY);
        lastZ.put(uuid, newZ);

        if (prevY == null) return;

        // Lag check (async-safe)
        if (lag != null && lag.multiplier(player) > 1.5) {
            resetFall(uuid);
            return;
        }

        double deltaY = newY - prevY;
        
        // Detect teleport (async-safe)
        if (Math.abs(deltaY) > 10) {
            if (deltaY < 0) {
                resetFall(uuid);
            }
            return;
        }

        boolean wasAlreadyFalling = isFalling.getOrDefault(uuid, false);
        boolean currentlyDescending = deltaY < -0.1;

        // === ASYNC BLOCK CHECKS ===
        // Schedule block check periodically on main thread to update cache
        if (now - lastBlockCheckTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L) > BLOCK_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS) {
            lastBlockCheckTime.put(uuid, now);
            
            final int bx = (int) Math.floor(newX);
            final int by = (int) Math.floor(newY);
            final int bz = (int) Math.floor(newZ);
            
            scheduleSync(() -> {
                try {
                    World world = player.getWorld();
                    boolean exempt = checkExemptBlocks(world, bx, by, bz);
                    isNearExemptBlock.put(uuid, exempt);
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            });
        }

        // Use cached block exemption result
        if (isNearExemptBlock.getOrDefault(uuid, false)) {
            resetFall(uuid);
            return;
        }
        
        // Mace grace period config
        long maceGrace = configs != null ? configs.checks().getLong("checks.NOFALL.params.mace_grace_ms", 2000) : 2000;
        long elytraGrace = configs != null ? configs.checks().getLong("checks.NOFALL.params.elytra_land_grace_ms", 1000) : 1000;

        // Elytra grace period
        if (now - lastGlideTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L) < elytraGrace) {
            resetFall(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // === FALL TRACKING (pure computation, no Bukkit API) ===
        if (currentlyDescending) {
            if (!wasAlreadyFalling) {
                // Started falling
                fallStartY.put(uuid, prevY);
                maxFallDistance.put(uuid, 0.0);
                isFalling.put(uuid, true);
                hitWithMaceDuringFall.put(uuid, false);
            }
            
            // Update max fall
            double currentFall = fallStartY.getOrDefault(uuid, newY) - newY;
            double maxFall = maxFallDistance.getOrDefault(uuid, 0.0);
            if (currentFall > maxFall) {
                maxFallDistance.put(uuid, currentFall);
            }
        }

        // === LANDING DETECTION ===
        if (clientOnGround && wasAlreadyFalling) {
            double realFallDist = maxFallDistance.getOrDefault(uuid, 0.0);
            double minFallForDamage = configs != null ? configs.checks().getDouble("checks.NOFALL.params.min_fall_blocks", 3.5) : 3.5;
            
            // Check if player used Mace during this fall
            boolean usedMace = hitWithMaceDuringFall.getOrDefault(uuid, false) && 
                              (now - lastMaceHitTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L) < maceGrace);
            
            long damageGraceMs = configs != null ? configs.checks().getLong("checks.NOFALL.params.damage_grace_ms", 200) : 200;
            double minDripstoneFall = configs != null ? configs.checks().getDouble("checks.NOFALL.params.min_dripstone_fall", 1.3) : 1.3;
            double expectedNormal = computeExpectedFallDamage(realFallDist, false);
            if (realFallDist >= minFallForDamage && !usedMace && expectedNormal >= 1.0) {
                // Player should receive fall damage (normal fall) — only if expected damage >= 1
                expectingDamageUntil.put(uuid, now + damageGraceMs);
                expectedFallDistance.put(uuid, realFallDist);
                expectedDamageAmount.put(uuid, expectedNormal);
                expectingDripstone.put(uuid, false);
                receivedFallDamage.put(uuid, false);
                // Ensure expiry check runs even if player stops sending packets
                scheduleExpectationExpiry(uuid, damageGraceMs);
                logDebug(String.format("set normal-fall expectation player=%s uuid=%s fall=%.2f expected=%.2f grace=%d", player.getName(), uuid, realFallDist, expectedNormal, damageGraceMs));
                // For normal falls we can reset immediately (no main-thread scan needed)
                resetFall(uuid);
            } else {
                // Check the block under player on main thread: if POINTED_DRIPSTONE and fall >= minDripstoneFall,
                // expect dripstone damage (small falls can still damage)
                final double rfd = realFallDist;
                final long grace = damageGraceMs;
                final double nx = newX;
                final double ny = newY;
                final double nz = newZ;
                // Use cached dripstone result updated by a lightweight main-thread updater.
                boolean dripBelow = hasDripstoneBelow.getOrDefault(uuid, false);
                double startY = fallStartY.getOrDefault(uuid, ny);
                double fallToTip = startY - (ny - 1.0); // approximate
                double slack = configs != null ? configs.checks().getDouble("checks.NOFALL.params.dripstone_slack", 0.15) : 0.15;
                boolean meetsByRfd = rfd + slack >= minDripstoneFall;
                if (dripBelow && (meetsByRfd)) {
                    long landingTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    expectingDamageUntil.put(uuid, landingTime + grace);
                    expectedFallDistance.put(uuid, rfd);
                    double expected = computeExpectedFallDamage(Math.max(rfd, fallToTip), true);
                    expectedDamageAmount.put(uuid, expected);
                    expectingDripstone.put(uuid, true);
                    receivedFallDamage.put(uuid, false);

                    // Schedule expiry asynchronously
                    scheduleExpectationExpiry(uuid, grace);
                    logDebug(String.format("set dripstone expectation (cached) player=%s uuid=%s rfd=%.3f expected=%.2f grace=%d", player.getName(), uuid, rfd, expected, grace));
                }
                // Clear fall tracking now that scan decision is made
                resetFall(uuid);
            }
            
        }
        
        // Reset if going up
        if (!currentlyDescending && !clientOnGround && deltaY > 0.1) {
            resetFall(uuid);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Called when player receives damage - check if it's fall damage we were expecting.
     * This runs on main thread (Bukkit event).
     */
    @Override
    public void onDamage(PlayerData data, EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;

        Player player = data.player();
        if (player == null) return;

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();

        // Only handle if we were expecting damage
        if (!expectingDamageUntil.containsKey(uuid)) return;

        // Any damage event during the expectation window counts as satisfying the fall expectation.
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        lastDamageTime.put(uuid, now);
        logDebug(String.format("damage event (marking received) player=%s uuid=%s cause=%s dmg=%.2f", player.getName(), uuid, event.getCause().name(), event.getDamage()));
        // Mark received; do NOT clear the expectation maps here so the scheduled expiry can see it
        // and will clear the expectation without flagging.
        receivedFallDamage.put(uuid, true);
    }

    /**
     * Check player state exemptions.
     * Note: Some of these methods may access Bukkit API, but they're fast cached values.
     */
    private boolean shouldExemptCompletely(PlayerData data, Player player) {
        if (data.isExempt()) return true;
        if (player.isFlying() || player.getAllowFlight()) return true;
        if (player.isGliding()) {
            lastGlideTime.put(player.getUniqueId(), System.currentTimeMillis());
            return true;
        }
        if (player.isInsideVehicle()) return true;
        if (player.isRiptiding()) return true;
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SLOW_FALLING)) return true;
        if (player.hasPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.LEVITATION)) return true;
        if (player.isSwimming()) return true;
        return false;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private boolean isHoldingMace(Player player) {
        ItemStack mainHand = player.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
        return mainHand != null && mainHand.getType().name().equals("MACE");
    }
    
    /**
     * Unified block exemption check - MUST be called from main thread.
     * Checks for liquids, climbables, and fall-negating blocks in one optimized pass.
     */
    private boolean checkExemptBlocks(World world, int bx, int by, int bz) {
        // Check liquids (vertical range only - less iterations)
        for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
            Block block = world.getBlockAt(bx, by + dy, bz);
            if (block.isLiquid() || block.getType() == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN) return true;
            if (block.getBlockData() instanceof Waterlogged && ((Waterlogged) block.getBlockData()).isWaterlogged()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        
        // Check climbables and fall-negating blocks (3x3 area)
        for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
            for (int dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {
                // Current level - climbables
                Block block = world.getBlockAt(bx + dx, by, bz + dz);
                Material mat = block.getType();
                if (CLIMBABLE_BLOCKS.contains(mat) || mat.name().contains("VINE")) return true;
                
                // Below level - climbables and fall negating
                Block below = world.getBlockAt(bx + dx, by - 1, bz + dz);
                Material belowMat = below.getType();
                if (CLIMBABLE_BLOCKS.contains(belowMat)) return true;
                if (FALL_NEGATING_BLOCKS.contains(belowMat) || BEDS.contains(belowMat)) return true;
                
                // Two blocks below - fall negating only
                Block below2 = world.getBlockAt(bx + dx, by - 2, bz + dz);
                Material below2Mat = below2.getType();
                if (FALL_NEGATING_BLOCKS.contains(below2Mat) || BEDS.contains(below2Mat)) return true;
            }
        }
        
        return false;
    }
    
    /**
     * Schedule a task to run on the main server thread.
     */
    private void scheduleSync(Runnable task) {
        Plugin p = getPluginOrFallback();
        if (p != null && p.isEnabled()) {
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(p, task);
        }
    }

    private void logDebug(String msg) {
        if (!debugLogs) return;
        Plugin p = getPluginOrFallback();
        if (p != null && p.isEnabled()) {
            p.getLogger().info("[NoFallDebug] " + msg);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Schedule an expiry check for an expected damage event on the main thread.
     * This ensures we flag even if the player stops sending packets after landing.
     */
    private void scheduleExpectationExpiry(UUID uuid, long graceMs) {
        // Schedule expiry asynchronously; only flag on main thread if needed.
        asyncExecutor.schedule(() -> {
            try {
                boolean received = receivedFallDamage.getOrDefault(uuid, false);
                double fallDist = expectedFallDistance.getOrDefault(uuid, 0.0);
                Boolean isDrip = expectingDripstone.getOrDefault(uuid, false);
                long lastDmg = lastDamageTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L);
                long cooldown = configs != null ? configs.checks().getLong("checks.NOFALL.params.alert_cooldown_ms", 1500) : 1500;

                logDebug(String.format("expiry running async uuid=%s received=%b lastDamage=%d fall=%.3f drip=%b now=%d", uuid, received, lastDmg, fallDist, isDrip, System.currentTimeMillis()));

                if (!received) {
                    long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
                    if (now - lastFlagTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L) >= cooldown) {
                        lastFlagTime.put(uuid, now);
                        Player player = Bukkit.getPlayer(uuid);
                        if (player != null && player.isOnline()) {
                            String detail = String.format("no fall damage received, fall=%.1f drip=%b", fallDist, isDrip);
                            Plugin p = getPluginOrFallback();
                            if (p != null) {
                                final Player fp = player;
                                final String fdetail = detail;
                                Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(p, () -> {
                                    try {
                                        violations.flag(fp, id().name(), 1, fdetail);
                                        logDebug(String.format("flagged player=%s uuid=%s fall=%.3f drip=%b (sync)", fp.getName(), uuid, fallDist, isDrip));
                                    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                                });
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        logDebug(String.format("expiry skipped due cooldown for uuid=%s", uuid));
                    }
                } else {
                    logDebug(String.format("expiry saw damage for uuid=%s, clearing without flag", uuid));
                }

                // Clear expectation data
                expectingDamageUntil.remove(uuid);
                expectedFallDistance.remove(uuid);
                expectedDamageAmount.remove(uuid);
                expectingDripstone.remove(uuid);
                receivedFallDamage.remove(uuid);
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        }, Math.max(1, graceMs), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
    }

    /**
     * Return the configured plugin or attempt to find the plugin by name as a fallback.
     */
    private Plugin getPluginOrFallback() {
        if (this.plugin != null) return this.plugin;
        try {
            Plugin p = Bukkit.getPluginManager().getPlugin("VirgolaAntiCheat");
            if (p != null && p.isEnabled()) return p;
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        return null;
    }

    private double computeExpectedFallDamage(double fallDistance, boolean dripstone) {
        // Vanilla fall damage: damage = floor(fallDistance - 3)
        // For dripstone, small falls can still deal 1 damage starting from ~1.3 blocks.
        if (dripstone) {
            double base = Math.floor(fallDistance - 3.0);
            double dmg = Math.max(1.0, base);
            return dmg;
        } else {
            return Math.max(0.0, Math.floor(fallDistance - 3.0));
        }
    }

    private void resetFall(UUID uuid) {
        isFalling.put(uuid, false);
        fallStartY.remove(uuid);
        maxFallDistance.put(uuid, 0.0);
        hitWithMaceDuringFall.put(uuid, false);
    }

    @Override
    public void onQuit(UUID uuid) {
        lastX.remove(uuid);
        lastY.remove(uuid);
        lastZ.remove(uuid);
        fallStartY.remove(uuid);
        maxFallDistance.remove(uuid);
        isFalling.remove(uuid);
        expectingDamageUntil.remove(uuid);
        expectedFallDistance.remove(uuid);
        receivedFallDamage.remove(uuid);
        lastMaceHitTime.remove(uuid);
        hitWithMaceDuringFall.remove(uuid);
        lastFlagTime.remove(uuid);
        lastBlockCheckTime.remove(uuid);
        isNearExemptBlock.remove(uuid);
        lastGlideTime.remove(uuid);
    }
    
    /**
     * Shutdown the async executor when plugin disables.
     */
    public void shutdown() {
        asyncExecutor.shutdown();
        try {
            if (!asyncExecutor.awaitTermination(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                asyncExecutor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            asyncExecutor.shutdownNow();
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        try {
            if (cacheUpdaterTask != null) cacheUpdaterTask.cancel();
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
    }

    @Override
    public CheckId id() {
        return CheckId.NOFALL;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        // Reload config-driven flags if available
        if (configs != null) {
            this.debugLogs = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.NOFALL.params.debug_logs", false);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Mark that the player hit an entity with a mace during a fall.
     * This will be used to ignore fall damage expectations for a short grace window.
     */
    public void markMaceHit(UUID uuid) {
        if (uuid == null) return;
        hitWithMaceDuringFall.put(uuid, true);
        lastMaceHitTime.put(uuid, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/impl/ScaffoldCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl;

import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.LagCompensation;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.BlockFace;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.block.BlockPlaceEvent;
import org.bukkit.util.Vector;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * SCAFFOLD - Scaffold/Tower Hack Detection
 * 
 * Logica semplice:
 * 1. Il blocco è stato piazzato SOTTO al player?
 * 2. Se sì: il player stava guardando la faccia TOP del blocco "against"?
 * 3. Se non stava guardando la faccia (angolo troppo alto / pitch troppo basso) = CHEAT
 */
public final class ScaffoldCheck implements Check {

    private final Configs configs;
    private final ViolationService violations;
    private final LagCompensation lag;

    private volatile boolean enabled = true;

    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastFlagMs = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> violationCount = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public ScaffoldCheck(Configs configs, ViolationService violations, LagCompensation lag) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.violations = violations;
        this.lag = lag;
    }

    @Override
    public CheckId id() {
        return CheckId.SCAFFOLD;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        this.enabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.SCAFFOLD.enabled", true);
    }

    @Override
    public void onBlockPlace(PlayerData data, BlockPlaceEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        if (data == null || event == null) return;

        Player player = event.getPlayer();
        if (player == null) return;

        if (data.isExempt()) return;

        Block placed = event.getBlockPlaced();
        Block against = event.getBlockAgainst();
        
        if (placed == null || against == null) return;

        Material type = placed.getType();
        if (!type.isBlock() || type.isAir() || !type.isSolid()) return;

        if (placed.getLocation().equals(against.getLocation())) return;

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // Lag multiplier
        double lagMult = 1.0;
        try {
            lagMult = lag != null ? lag.multiplier(player) : 1.0;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        lagMult = Math.max(1.0, Math.min(lagMult, 2.5));

        Location loc = player.getLocation();
        Location eyeLoc = player.getEyeLocation();
        float pitch = loc.getPitch();
        
        int placedY = placed.getY();
        int playerFeetY = (int) Math.floor(loc.getY());

        // CHECK 1: Il blocco è SOTTO al player?
        boolean isBlockUnderPlayer = (placedY == playerFeetY - 1);
        
        double hDistX = loc.getX() - (placed.getX() + 0.5);
        double hDistZ = loc.getZ() - (placed.getZ() + 0.5);
        double hDistSq = hDistX * hDistX + hDistZ * hDistZ;
        boolean isPlayerAboveBlock = hDistSq <= 2.25;
        
        if (!isBlockUnderPlayer || !isPlayerAboveBlock) {
            return;
        }

        // Determina la faccia cliccata
        BlockFace clickedFace = against.getFace(placed);
        if (clickedFace == null) {
            clickedFace = calculateClickedFace(against, placed);
        }

        // CHECK 2: Verifica se il player stava GUARDANDO la faccia cliccata
        boolean suspicious = false;
        String reason = "";

        Vector faceCenter = getBlockFaceCenter(against, clickedFace);
        Vector eyePos = eyeLoc.toVector();
        Vector lookDir = eyeLoc.getDirection().normalize();
        
        Vector toFaceCenter = faceCenter.clone().subtract(eyePos);
        double distToFace = toFaceCenter.length();
        
        if (distToFace > 0.01) {
            Vector toFaceDir = toFaceCenter.clone().multiply(1.0 / distToFace);
            
            double dot = lookDir.dot(toFaceDir);
            dot = Math.max(-1.0, Math.min(1.0, dot));
            double angleDeg = Math.toDegrees(Math.acos(dot));
            
            Vector faceNormal = getFaceNormal(clickedFace);
            double lookDotNormal = lookDir.dot(faceNormal);
            
            double maxAngle = getDouble("max_angle", 80.0) * lagMult;
            
            if (angleDeg > maxAngle) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason = "angle=" + fmt(angleDeg) + "°";
            }
            
            if (lookDotNormal > 0.1) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason += " wrongSide";
            }
            
            if (clickedFace == BlockFace.UP && pitch < 0) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason += " pitch=" + fmt(pitch) + "°(UP)";
            }
            
            if (clickedFace == BlockFace.DOWN && pitch > 0) {
                suspicious = true;
                reason += " pitch=" + fmt(pitch) + "°(DOWN)";
            }
            
            boolean isLateralFace = (clickedFace == BlockFace.NORTH || clickedFace == BlockFace.SOUTH ||
                                     clickedFace == BlockFace.EAST || clickedFace == BlockFace.WEST);
            
            if (isLateralFace) {
                double minLookDotForLateral = getDouble("min_look_dot_lateral", -0.1);
                
                if (lookDotNormal > minLookDotForLateral) {
                    suspicious = true;
                    reason += " lateralFace=" + clickedFace.name() + "+lookDot=" + fmt(lookDotNormal);
                }
            }
        }

        if (!suspicious) {
            int current = violationCount.getOrDefault(uuid, 0);
            if (current > 0) {
                violationCount.put(uuid, current - 1);
            }
            return;
        }

        int vl = violationCount.getOrDefault(uuid, 0) + 1;
        violationCount.put(uuid, vl);

        int minViolations = getInt("min_violations", 2);
        if (vl < minViolations) {
            return;
        }

        long cooldown = getLong("alert_cooldown_ms", 1000L);
        long lastFlag = lastFlagMs.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L);
        
        if ((now - lastFlag) < cooldown) {
            return;
        }

        lastFlagMs.put(uuid, now);
        violationCount.put(uuid, 0);

        violations.flag(data, id().name(), 1, reason.trim());
    }

    private BlockFace calculateClickedFace(Block against, Block placed) {
        int dx = placed.getX() - against.getX();
        int dy = placed.getY() - against.getY();
        int dz = placed.getZ() - against.getZ();
        
        if (dy == 1) return BlockFace.UP;
        if (dy == -1) return BlockFace.DOWN;
        if (dx == 1) return BlockFace.EAST;
        if (dx == -1) return BlockFace.WEST;
        if (dz == 1) return BlockFace.SOUTH;
        if (dz == -1) return BlockFace.NORTH;
        
        return BlockFace.UP;
    }
    
    private Vector getBlockFaceCenter(Block block, BlockFace face) {
        double x = block.getX() + 0.5;
        double y = block.getY() + 0.5;
        double z = block.getZ() + 0.5;
        
        switch (face) {
            case UP:    return new Vector(x, block.getY() + 1.0, z);
            case DOWN:  return new Vector(x, block.getY(), z);
            case NORTH: return new Vector(x, y, block.getZ());
            case SOUTH: return new Vector(x, y, block.getZ() + 1.0);
            case WEST:  return new Vector(block.getX(), y, z);
            case EAST:  return new Vector(block.getX() + 1.0, y, z);
            default:    return new Vector(x, y, z);
        }
    }
    
    private Vector getFaceNormal(BlockFace face) {
        switch (face) {
            case UP:    return new Vector(0, 1, 0);
            case DOWN:  return new Vector(0, -1, 0);
            case NORTH: return new Vector(0, 0, -1);
            case SOUTH: return new Vector(0, 0, 1);
            case WEST:  return new Vector(-1, 0, 0);
            case EAST:  return new Vector(1, 0, 0);
            default:    return new Vector(0, 1, 0);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void onQuit(UUID uuid) {
        lastFlagMs.remove(uuid);
        violationCount.remove(uuid);
    }

    private String base(String key) {
        return "checks." + id().name() + ".params." + key;
    }

    private long getLong(String key, long def) {
        return configs.checks().getLong(base(key), def);
    }

    private double getDouble(String key, double def) {
        return configs.checks().getDouble(base(key), def);
    }

    private int getInt(String key, int def) {
        return configs.checks().getInt(base(key), def);
    }

    private static String fmt(double d) {
        return String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1f", d);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/checks/impl/SpeedCheck.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.LagCompensation;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * SPEED - Speed Hack Detection (Packet-Based)
 *
 * Uses official Minecraft movement values:
 * - Walking: 4.317 m/s
 * - Sprinting: 5.612 m/s (+30%)
 * - Sneaking: 1.3 m/s (30% of walking)
 * - Speed potion: +20% per level
 * - Soul Speed: (Level * 0.105 + 1.3) multiplier
 * - Swift Sneak: +15% per level of sneaking speed
 * - Various block modifiers (ice, soul sand, honey, slime, etc.)
 */
public final class SpeedCheck implements Check {

    // === OFFICIAL MINECRAFT SPEED VALUES (blocks per second) ===
    private static final double WALK_SPEED = 4.317;
    private static final double SPRINT_SPEED = 5.612;  // 4.317 * 1.3
    private static final double SNEAK_SPEED = 1.3;     // 30% of walking
    private static final double DIAGONAL_MULTIPLIER = 1.45;  // ~sqrt(2) for diagonal movement

    // Block-based speeds (without enchants)
    private static final double SOUL_SAND_SPEED = 2.508;
    private static final double HONEY_SPEED = 2.508;
    private static final double SLIME_SPEED = 3.040;

    // Potion multipliers
    private static final double SPEED_POTION_PER_LEVEL = 0.20;  // +20% per level
    private static final double SLOWNESS_PER_LEVEL = 0.15;      // -15% per level

    // Enchantment multipliers
    private static final double SWIFT_SNEAK_PER_LEVEL = 0.15;   // +15% of WALK speed per level

    // Fixed grace windows (NO new config keys)
    private static final long SOUL_SPEED_EQUIP_GRACE_MS = 650;

    // Fixed ratio tuning (NO new config keys)
    private static final double IGNORE_RATIO_DEFAULT = 1.10;   // ignore tiny deviations
    private static final double BORDERLINE_RATIO_DEFAULT = 1.15; // accumulate slowly
    private static final double IGNORE_RATIO_SOUL = 1.07;      // tighter on recent soul blocks
    private static final double BORDERLINE_RATIO_SOUL = 1.12;

    private final Configs configs;
    private final ViolationService violations;
    private final LagCompensation lag;

    private volatile boolean enabled = true;

    // Tracking per player
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastX = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastY = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> lastZ = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastPacketTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> groundTicks = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Double> buffer = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastFlagTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    // Grace tracking
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastIceTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastBounceTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastDamageTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastSoulBlockTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastBlockChangeTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Material> lastBlockBelow = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastSneakStartTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Boolean> lastSneakState = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastNotSneakingTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    // Soul Speed equip/unequip tracking (no config)
    private final Map<UUID, Integer> lastSoulSpeedLevel = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<UUID, Long> lastSoulSpeedChangeTime = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public SpeedCheck(Configs configs, ViolationService violations, LagCompensation lag) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.violations = violations;
        this.lag = lag;
    }

    @Override
    public CheckId id() {
        return CheckId.SPEED;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean enabled() {
        return enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void setEnabled(boolean enabled) {
        this.enabled = enabled;
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        this.enabled = configs.checks().getBoolean("checks.SPEED.enabled", true);
    }

    @Override
    public void onPacketReceive(PlayerData data, PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        if (!enabled) return;
        if (!WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying.isFlying(event.getPacketType())) return;

        Player player = data.player();
        if (player == null || !player.isOnline()) return;

        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        // === EXEMPTIONS ===
        if (data.isExempt()) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Creative/Spectator/Flying
        if (player.isFlying() || player.getAllowFlight()) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Elytra
        if (player.isGliding()) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Vehicle
        if (player.isInsideVehicle()) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Riptide
        if (player.isRiptiding()) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Parse packet
        WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying wrapper;
        try {
            wrapper = new WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return;
        }

        if (!wrapper.hasPositionChanged()) return;

        double x = wrapper.getLocation().getX();
        double y = wrapper.getLocation().getY();
        double z = wrapper.getLocation().getZ();
        boolean onGround = wrapper.isOnGround();

        // Get previous data
        Double prevX = lastX.get(uuid);
        Double prevY = lastY.get(uuid);
        Double prevZ = lastZ.get(uuid);
        Long prevTime = lastPacketTime.get(uuid);

        // Update tracking
        lastX.put(uuid, x);
        lastY.put(uuid, y);
        lastZ.put(uuid, z);
        lastPacketTime.put(uuid, now);

        if (prevX == null || prevY == null || prevZ == null || prevTime == null) {
            return;
        }

        // Calculate deltas
        double dx = x - prevX;
        double dz = z - prevZ;
        double hDist = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
        long dt = now - prevTime;

        // Skip weird packet timing
        double m = lag.multiplier(player);
        if (dt < 20 || dt > (200 * m)) {
            return;
        }

        // Convert to blocks per second
        double bps = (hDist / dt) * 1000.0;

        // === DOMAIN SEPARATION ===
        // Air handled by FLY
        if (!onGround) {
            int gt = groundTicks.getOrDefault(uuid, 0) - 1;
            groundTicks.put(uuid, Math.max(0, gt));
            decay(uuid, getDouble("decay_air", 0.15));
            return;
        }

        int gt = groundTicks.getOrDefault(uuid, 0) + 1;
        groundTicks.put(uuid, gt);

        // Skip early ground ticks (just landed)
        if (gt < getInt("min_ground_ticks", 3)) {
            return;
        }

        World world = player.getWorld();
        Location loc = new Location(world, x, y, z);

        // Liquid handled by JESUS
        if (isInLiquid(loc, world)) {
            reset(uuid);
            return;
        }

        // Skip negligible movement
        if (bps < getDouble("min_bps", 0.5)) {
            decay(uuid, getDouble("decay_still", 0.1));
            return;
        }

        // === CALCULATE MAX LEGITIMATE SPEED ===
        double maxBps = calculateMaxSpeed(player, loc, world, now, uuid, dx, dz);

        // === SOUL SPEED EQUIP/UNEQUIP GRACE (fixed window, no config) ===
        Long ssChange = lastSoulSpeedChangeTime.get(uuid);
        if (ssChange != null && (now - ssChange) < SOUL_SPEED_EQUIP_GRACE_MS) {
            // Aggressive decay to avoid carrying over buffer across legit speed changes
            decay(uuid, 1.25);
            return;
        }

        // === SNEAK TRANSITION GRACE (uses existing config key sneak_grace_ms) ===
        boolean currentSneak = player.isSneaking();
        Boolean lastSneak = lastSneakState.get(uuid);

        if (!currentSneak) {
            lastNotSneakingTime.put(uuid, now);
        }
        if (lastSneak != null && !lastSneak && currentSneak) {
            lastSneakStartTime.put(uuid, now);
        }
        lastSneakState.put(uuid, currentSneak);

        Long lastNotSneak = lastNotSneakingTime.get(uuid);
        long sneakGraceMs = getLong("sneak_grace_ms", 800);
        if (currentSneak && lastNotSneak != null && (now - lastNotSneak) < sneakGraceMs) {
            // IMPORTANT: don't multiply by lag here; lag applied below once.
            maxBps = Math.max(maxBps, WALK_SPEED);
        }

        // Apply lag compensation (once)
        maxBps *= Math.max(1.0, m);

        // === CHECK ===
        if (bps <= maxBps) {
            decay(uuid, getDouble("decay_ok", 0.25));
            return;
        }

        double excessRatio = bps / maxBps;

        // Tighter ratios if (recently) on soul blocks to catch small-margin hacks there
        boolean recentSoul = false;
        Long lastSoul = lastSoulBlockTime.get(uuid);
        if (lastSoul != null && (now - lastSoul) < 250) {
            recentSoul = true;
        }

        double ignoreRatio = recentSoul ? IGNORE_RATIO_SOUL : IGNORE_RATIO_DEFAULT;
        double borderlineRatio = recentSoul ? BORDERLINE_RATIO_SOUL : BORDERLINE_RATIO_DEFAULT;

        if (excessRatio < ignoreRatio) {
            decay(uuid, getDouble("decay_slight_over", 0.15));
            return;
        }

        double weight;
        if (excessRatio < borderlineRatio) {
            weight = getDouble("weight_borderline", 0.25);
        } else if (excessRatio < 1.20) {
            weight = getDouble("weight_low", 0.5);
        } else if (excessRatio < 1.35) {
            weight = getDouble("weight_medium", 0.8);
        } else {
            weight = getDouble("weight_high", 1.2);
        }

        double buf = buffer.getOrDefault(uuid, 0.0) + weight;
        buffer.put(uuid, buf);

        double threshold = getDouble("threshold", 4.5);
        long cooldown = (long) (getLong("alert_cooldown_ms", 1500) * m);
        long lastFlag = lastFlagTime.getOrDefault(uuid, 0L);

        if (buf >= threshold && (now - lastFlag) >= cooldown) {
            lastFlagTime.put(uuid, now);

            violations.flag(data, id().name(), 1,
                    "bps=" + fmt(bps) +
                            " max=" + fmt(maxBps) +
                            " ratio=" + fmt(excessRatio) +
                            " buf=" + fmt(buf));

            buffer.put(uuid, buf - getDouble("post_flag_reduce", 2.5));
        }
    }

    /**
     * Calculate maximum legitimate horizontal speed for current conditions.
     * Handles Soul Speed and soul-sand slowdown correctly (including sprint).
     */
    private double calculateMaxSpeed(Player player, Location loc, World world, long now, UUID uuid, double dx, double dz) {
        // === DETERMINE IF PLAYER CAN ACTUALLY SPRINT ===
        float yaw = player.getLocation().getYaw();
        double facingX = -Math.sin(Math.toRadians(yaw));
        double facingZ = Math.cos(Math.toRadians(yaw));

        double movementMagnitude = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz);
        boolean movingForward = false;
        if (movementMagnitude > 0.01) {
            double dotProduct = (dx * facingX + dz * facingZ) / movementMagnitude;
            movingForward = dotProduct > 0.5; // within ~60 degrees
        }

        // === CHECK ALL BLOCKS UNDER PLAYER HITBOX ===
        double px = loc.getX();
        double py = loc.getY();
        double pz = loc.getZ();
        double halfWidth = 0.3;

        int yBelow = (int) Math.floor(py - 0.1);
        int yAt = (int) Math.floor(py);

        Block[] blocksBelow = new Block[]{
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px - halfWidth), yBelow, (int) Math.floor(pz - halfWidth)),
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px + halfWidth), yBelow, (int) Math.floor(pz - halfWidth)),
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px - halfWidth), yBelow, (int) Math.floor(pz + halfWidth)),
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px + halfWidth), yBelow, (int) Math.floor(pz + halfWidth))
        };

        Block[] blocksAt = new Block[]{
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px - halfWidth), yAt, (int) Math.floor(pz - halfWidth)),
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px + halfWidth), yAt, (int) Math.floor(pz - halfWidth)),
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px - halfWidth), yAt, (int) Math.floor(pz + halfWidth)),
                world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px + halfWidth), yAt, (int) Math.floor(pz + halfWidth))
        };

        boolean onSoulBlock = false;
        boolean onIce = false;
        boolean onBlueIce = false;
        boolean onPackedIce = false;
        boolean onBouncy = false;
        boolean onHoney = false;
        boolean onSlime = false;
        boolean inCobweb = false;
        boolean inBerryBush = false;
        boolean onNormalBlock = false;

        for (Block below : blocksBelow) {
            Material mat = below.getType();
            if (mat == Material.SOUL_SAND || mat == Material.SOUL_SOIL) onSoulBlock = true;
            if (mat == Material.BLUE_ICE) {
                onIce = true;
                onBlueIce = true;
            }
            if (mat == Material.PACKED_ICE) {
                onIce = true;
                onPackedIce = true;
            }
            if (mat == Material.ICE || mat == Material.FROSTED_ICE) onIce = true;
            if (mat == Material.SLIME_BLOCK) {
                onBouncy = true;
                onSlime = true;
            }
            if (mat.name().endsWith("_BED")) onBouncy = true;
            if (mat == Material.HONEY_BLOCK) onHoney = true;

            if (mat.isSolid() && !isSoulBlock(mat) && !isIce(mat)
                    && mat != Material.SLIME_BLOCK && mat != Material.HONEY_BLOCK) {
                onNormalBlock = true;
            }
        }

        for (Block at : blocksAt) {
            Material mat = at.getType();
            if (mat == Material.SOUL_SAND || mat == Material.SOUL_SOIL) onSoulBlock = true;
            if (mat == Material.HONEY_BLOCK) onHoney = true;
            if (mat == Material.COBWEB) inCobweb = true;
            if (mat == Material.SWEET_BERRY_BUSH) inBerryBush = true;
        }

        int soulSpeed = getSoulSpeedLevel(player);
        trackSoulSpeedChange(uuid, soulSpeed, now);

        // If touching ANY soul block, Soul Speed bonus applies.
        boolean soulSpeedApplies = onSoulBlock && soulSpeed > 0;

        // Soul sand slowdown applies only if FULLY on soul block without Soul Speed
        boolean soulSlowdownApplies = onSoulBlock && !onNormalBlock && soulSpeed == 0;

        if (onSoulBlock) {
            lastSoulBlockTime.put(uuid, now);
        }

        double base = WALK_SPEED;
        boolean soulBlockHandled = false;

        // === SOUL BLOCK WITH SOUL SPEED ===
        if (soulSpeedApplies) {
            soulBlockHandled = true;

            double soulBonus = (soulSpeed * 0.105) + 1.3;

            if (player.isSneaking()) {
                base = (SNEAK_SPEED * DIAGONAL_MULTIPLIER) * soulBonus;
            } else if (player.isSprinting() && movingForward) {
                base = SPRINT_SPEED * soulBonus;
            } else {
                base = WALK_SPEED * soulBonus;
            }
        }
        // === SOUL SAND/SOIL WITHOUT SOUL SPEED ===
        else if (soulSlowdownApplies) {
            soulBlockHandled = true;

            if (player.isSneaking()) {
                base = SNEAK_SPEED * DIAGONAL_MULTIPLIER * getDouble("soul_no_enchant_sneak_mult", 0.7);
            } else if (player.isSprinting() && movingForward) {
                // FIX: sprint is still possible under slowdown; apply +30% to slowed walk
                base = SOUL_SAND_SPEED * 1.30;
            } else {
                base = SOUL_SAND_SPEED;
            }
        }
        // Edge case: on edge between soul block and normal block w/out Soul Speed -> fall through

        // === NORMAL BASE SPEED ===
        if (!soulBlockHandled) {
            if (player.isSneaking()) {
                base = SNEAK_SPEED * DIAGONAL_MULTIPLIER;

                int swiftSneak = getSwiftSneakLevel(player);
                if (swiftSneak > 0) {
                    double sneakPercent = 0.30 + (swiftSneak * SWIFT_SNEAK_PER_LEVEL);
                    sneakPercent = Math.min(sneakPercent, 1.0);
                    base = WALK_SPEED * sneakPercent * DIAGONAL_MULTIPLIER;
                }
            } else {
                boolean serverSprinting = player.isSprinting() && movingForward;
                boolean clientLikelySprinting = movingForward && movementMagnitude > 0.2;

                if (serverSprinting || clientLikelySprinting) {
                    base = SPRINT_SPEED;
                } else {
                    base = WALK_SPEED;
                }
            }
        }

        // === POTION EFFECTS ===
        PotionEffect speed = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SPEED);
        if (speed != null) {
            int amp = speed.getAmplifier() + 1;
            base *= (1.0 + amp * SPEED_POTION_PER_LEVEL);
        }

        PotionEffect slow = player.getPotionEffect(PotionEffectType.SLOWNESS);
        if (slow != null) {
            int amp = slow.getAmplifier() + 1;
            double reduction = amp * SLOWNESS_PER_LEVEL;
            base *= Math.max(0.0, 1.0 - reduction);
        }

        // === BLOCK-BASED MODIFIERS ===
        Block centerBelow = world.getBlockAt((int) Math.floor(px), (int) Math.floor(py) - 1, (int) Math.floor(pz));
        Material belowMat = centerBelow.getType();
        Material prevBlock = lastBlockBelow.get(uuid);
        if (prevBlock != null && prevBlock != belowMat) {
            lastBlockChangeTime.put(uuid, now);
        }
        lastBlockBelow.put(uuid, belowMat);

        Long lastChange = lastBlockChangeTime.get(uuid);
        boolean inBlockTransition = lastChange != null && (now - lastChange) < getLong("block_change_grace_ms", 600);

        double blockModifier = 1.0;
        boolean hasSpecialBlock = soulBlockHandled;

        // Soul Speed momentum grace when leaving soul blocks (existing config key)
        if (!hasSpecialBlock && soulSpeed > 0) {
            Long lastSoul = lastSoulBlockTime.get(uuid);
            if (lastSoul != null && (now - lastSoul) < getLong("soul_grace_ms", 400)) {
                hasSpecialBlock = true;
                blockModifier = 1.0 + (soulSpeed * 0.03);
            }
        }

        // Ice blocks
        if (!hasSpecialBlock && onIce) {
            lastIceTime.put(uuid, now);
            hasSpecialBlock = true;
            if (onBlueIce) {
                blockModifier = getDouble("blue_ice_mult", 1.4);
            } else if (onPackedIce) {
                blockModifier = getDouble("packed_ice_mult", 1.3);
            } else {
                blockModifier = getDouble("ice_mult", 1.2);
            }
        } else if (!hasSpecialBlock) {
            Long lastIce = lastIceTime.get(uuid);
            if (lastIce != null && (now - lastIce) < getLong("ice_grace_ms", 500)) {
                hasSpecialBlock = true;
                blockModifier = getDouble("ice_grace_mult", 1.2);
            }
        }

        // Slime
        if (!hasSpecialBlock && onSlime) {
            blockModifier = SLIME_SPEED / WALK_SPEED;
        }

        // Honey
        if (!hasSpecialBlock && onHoney) {
            blockModifier = HONEY_SPEED / WALK_SPEED;
        }

        base *= blockModifier;

        // Bounce grace
        if (onBouncy) {
            lastBounceTime.put(uuid, now);
        }
        Long lastBounce = lastBounceTime.get(uuid);
        if (lastBounce != null && (now - lastBounce) < getLong("bounce_grace_ms", 800)) {
            base *= getDouble("bounce_grace_mult", 1.3);
        }

        // Cobweb
        if (inCobweb) {
            base *= getDouble("cobweb_mult", 0.15);
        }

        // Berry bush
        if (inBerryBush) {
            base *= getDouble("berry_mult", 0.25);
        }

        // Block transition grace (only if not special block)
        if (inBlockTransition && !hasSpecialBlock) {
            base *= getDouble("block_transition_mult", 1.25);
        }

        // Damage grace
        Long lastDmg = lastDamageTime.get(uuid);
        if (lastDmg != null && (now - lastDmg) < getLong("damage_grace_ms", 600)) {
            base *= getDouble("damage_grace_mult", 1.4);
        }

        // Hard cap
        double hardCap = getDouble("hard_cap_bps", 20.0);
        base = Math.min(base, hardCap);

        return Math.max(base, getDouble("min_max_bps", 1.5));
    }

    private void trackSoulSpeedChange(UUID uuid, int level, long now) {
        Integer prev = lastSoulSpeedLevel.put(uuid, level);
        if (prev != null && prev != level) {
            lastSoulSpeedChangeTime.put(uuid, now);
        }
    }

    private boolean isInLiquid(Location loc, World world) {
        Block at = world.getBlockAt(loc.getBlockX(), loc.getBlockY(), loc.getBlockZ());
        Block below = world.getBlockAt(loc.getBlockX(), loc.getBlockY() - 1, loc.getBlockZ());
        Material m1 = at.getType();
        Material m2 = below.getType();
        return m1 == Material.WATER || m1 == Material.LAVA ||
                m2 == Material.WATER || m2 == Material.LAVA;
    }

    private boolean isIce(Material mat) {
        return mat == Material.ICE || mat == Material.PACKED_ICE ||
                mat == Material.BLUE_ICE || mat == Material.FROSTED_ICE;
    }

    private boolean isSoulBlock(Material mat) {
        return mat == Material.SOUL_SAND || mat == Material.SOUL_SOIL;
    }

    private int getSwiftSneakLevel(Player player) {
        var leggings = player.getInventory().getLeggings();
        if (leggings == null) return 0;
        try {
            @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
            var ench = org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.getByName("SWIFT_SNEAK");
            if (ench != null) {
                return leggings.getEnchantmentLevel(ench);
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private int getSoulSpeedLevel(Player player) {
        var boots = player.getInventory().getBoots();
        if (boots == null) return 0;
        return boots.getEnchantmentLevel(org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.SOUL_SPEED);
    }

    /**
     * Called by damage listener to track knockback
     */
    public void onDamage(UUID uuid) {
        lastDamageTime.put(uuid, System.currentTimeMillis());
    }

    private void decay(UUID uuid, double amount) {
        double current = buffer.getOrDefault(uuid, 0.0);
        buffer.put(uuid, Math.max(0.0, current - amount));
    }

    private void reset(UUID uuid) {
        groundTicks.put(uuid, 0);
        buffer.put(uuid, 0.0);
    }

    @Override
    public void onQuit(UUID uuid) {
        lastX.remove(uuid);
        lastY.remove(uuid);
        lastZ.remove(uuid);
        lastPacketTime.remove(uuid);
        groundTicks.remove(uuid);
        buffer.remove(uuid);
        lastFlagTime.remove(uuid);

        lastIceTime.remove(uuid);
        lastBounceTime.remove(uuid);
        lastDamageTime.remove(uuid);
        lastSoulBlockTime.remove(uuid);
        lastBlockChangeTime.remove(uuid);
        lastBlockBelow.remove(uuid);

        lastSneakStartTime.remove(uuid);
        lastSneakState.remove(uuid);
        lastNotSneakingTime.remove(uuid);

        lastSoulSpeedLevel.remove(uuid);
        lastSoulSpeedChangeTime.remove(uuid);
    }

    // Config helpers (unchanged)
    private double getDouble(String key, double def) {
        return configs.checks().getDouble("checks.SPEED.params." + key, def);
    }

    private int getInt(String key, int def) {
        return configs.checks().getInt("checks.SPEED.params." + key, def);
    }

    private long getLong(String key, long def) {
        return configs.checks().getLong("checks.SPEED.params." + key, def);
    }

    private static String fmt(double d) {
        return String.format("%.2f", d);
    }
}

```

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.commands;

import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckManager;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.AlertService;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.TickService;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ViolationService;
import it.virgola.anticheat.gui.AcGui;
import it.virgola.anticheat.util.ColorUtil;
import net.md_5.bungee.api.chat.ClickEvent;
import net.md_5.bungee.api.chat.HoverEvent;
import net.md_5.bungee.api.chat.TextComponent;
import net.md_5.bungee.api.chat.hover.content.Text;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
```
