`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/AlertService.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.util.ColorUtil;
import it.virgola.anticheat.util.MsgUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;

public final class AlertService {

    private final Configs configs;
    private final DiscordService discordService;
    
    // Players who have disabled alerts
    private final Set<UUID> alertsDisabled = new HashSet<>();
    // Players who have enabled verbose/debug mode
    private final Set<UUID> verboseEnabled = new HashSet<>();

    public AlertService(Configs configs) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.discordService = new DiscordService(configs);
    }

    public void alert(Player suspect, String check, int addVl, String info) {
        String prefix = configs.plugin().getConfig().getString("prefix", "&d&lVirgolaAC &8» &7");
        String fmt = configs.messages().getString("alerts.format", "{prefix}[AC] {player} -> {check} (+{vl}) {info}");

        Map<String, String> vars = new HashMap<>();
        vars.put("prefix", prefix);
        vars.put("player", suspect.getName());
        vars.put("check", check);
        vars.put("vl", String.valueOf(addVl));
        vars.put("info", info == null ? "" : info);

        String msg = MsgUtil.format(fmt, vars);

        String perm = configs.plugin().getConfig().getString("alerts.permission", "virgolaac.alerts");
        boolean console = configs.plugin().getConfig().getBoolean("alerts.console", true);

        if (console) {
            Bukkit.getLogger().info(stripColor(msg));
        }

        for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if (p.hasPermission(perm) && !alertsDisabled.contains(p.getUniqueId())) {
                p.sendMessage(msg);
            }
        }

        // Send to Discord if enabled
        try {
            discordService.sendAlert(suspect, check, addVl, info);
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            // Prevent any exceptions in Discord integration from affecting server
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Toggle alerts for a player.
     * @return true if alerts are now enabled, false if disabled
     */
    public boolean toggleAlerts(UUID uuid) {
        if (alertsDisabled.contains(uuid)) {
            alertsDisabled.remove(uuid);
            return true;
        } else {
            alertsDisabled.add(uuid);
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if player has alerts enabled
     */
    public boolean hasAlertsEnabled(UUID uuid) {
        return !alertsDisabled.contains(uuid);
    }
    
    /**
     * Toggle verbose/debug mode for a player.
     * @return true if verbose is now enabled, false if disabled
     */
    public boolean toggleVerbose(UUID uuid) {
        if (verboseEnabled.contains(uuid)) {
            verboseEnabled.remove(uuid);
            return false;
        } else {
            verboseEnabled.add(uuid);
            return true;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if player has verbose mode enabled
     */
    public boolean hasVerboseEnabled(UUID uuid) {
        return verboseEnabled.contains(uuid);
    }
    
    /**
     * Send a verbose/debug message to players with verbose enabled
     */
    public void verbose(String message) {
        String prefix = configs.plugin().getConfig().getString("prefix", "&d&lVirgolaAC &8» &7");
        String msg = ColorUtil.color(prefix + "&7[DEBUG] " + message);
        
        for (Player p : Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if (verboseEnabled.contains(p.getUniqueId())) {
                p.sendMessage(msg);
            }
        }
    }

    private static String stripColor(String s) {
        return s.replaceAll("§.", "");
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/AsyncBlockCache.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.*;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Asynchronous block cache to avoid blocking the packet thread with world.getBlockAt() calls.
 * 
 * This service maintains a short-lived cache of block types and provides async methods
 * to check block conditions without blocking the calling thread.
 */
public final class AsyncBlockCache {

    // Cache key: "worldUUID:x:y:z" -> Material
    private final Map<String, CachedBlock> blockCache = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    
    // Cache expiration time in milliseconds
    private static final long CACHE_TTL_MS = 500;
    
    // Thread pool for async block lookups
    private final ExecutorService executor;
    
    // Plugin reference for scheduling sync tasks
    private final Plugin plugin;
    
    public AsyncBlockCache(Plugin plugin) {
        this.plugin = plugin;
        // Use a cached thread pool that can handle bursts but shrinks when idle
        this.executor = Executors.newCachedThreadPool(r -> {
            Thread t = new Thread(r, "VAC-BlockCache");
            t.setDaemon(true);
            return t;
        });
    }
    
    /**
     * Get block type from cache or schedule async lookup.
     * Returns cached value if available, otherwise returns null and schedules lookup.
     */
    public Material getCached(World world, int x, int y, int z) {
        String key = cacheKey(world.getUID(), x, y, z);
        CachedBlock cached = blockCache.get(key);
        
        if (cached != null && !cached.isExpired()) {
            return cached.material;
        }
        
        return null;
    }
    
    /**
     * Check block conditions asynchronously.
     * The callback will be invoked on the main thread with the result.
     * 
     * @param world The world
     * @param x Block X coordinate
     * @param y Block Y coordinate
     * @param z Block Z coordinate
     * @param callback Called with the Material when available
     */
    public void getBlockAsync(World world, int x, int y, int z, Consumer<Material> callback) {
        String key = cacheKey(world.getUID(), x, y, z);
        CachedBlock cached = blockCache.get(key);
        
        if (cached != null && !cached.isExpired()) {
            callback.accept(cached.material);
            return;
        }
        
        // Schedule sync task to read block (Bukkit API must be called from main thread)
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                Block block = world.getBlockAt(x, y, z);
                Material mat = block.getType();
                
                // Cache the result
                blockCache.put(key, new CachedBlock(mat, System.currentTimeMillis()));
                
                // Invoke callback
                callback.accept(mat);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                callback.accept(Material.AIR);
            }
        });
    }
    
    /**
     * Check multiple blocks and return result via callback.
     * Useful for checking area around player.
     */
    public void checkBlocksInArea(World world, int centerX, int centerY, int centerZ,
                                   int radiusX, int radiusY, int radiusZ,
                                   Set<Material> targetMaterials,
                                   Consumer<Boolean> callback) {
        
        // First check cache for all positions
        boolean allCached = true;
        for (int dx = -radiusX; dx <= radiusX && allCached; dx++) {
            for (int dy = -radiusY; dy <= radiusY && allCached; dy++) {
                for (int dz = -radiusZ; dz <= radiusZ && allCached; dz++) {
                    Material cached = getCached(world, centerX + dx, centerY + dy, centerZ + dz);
                    if (cached == null) {
                        allCached = false;
                    } else if (targetMaterials.contains(cached)) {
                        callback.accept(true);
                        return;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        if (allCached) {
            callback.accept(false);
            return;
        }
        
        // Need to fetch from world - schedule sync
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                for (int dx = -radiusX; dx <= radiusX; dx++) {
                    for (int dy = -radiusY; dy <= radiusY; dy++) {
                        for (int dz = -radiusZ; dz <= radiusZ; dz++) {
                            int bx = centerX + dx;
                            int by = centerY + dy;
                            int bz = centerZ + dz;
                            
                            Block block = world.getBlockAt(bx, by, bz);
                            Material mat = block.getType();
                            
                            // Cache it
                            String key = cacheKey(world.getUID(), bx, by, bz);
                            blockCache.put(key, new CachedBlock(mat, System.currentTimeMillis()));
                            
                            if (targetMaterials.contains(mat)) {
                                callback.accept(true);
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                callback.accept(false);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                callback.accept(false);
            }
        });
    }
    
    /**
     * Check if any block in area is liquid (async).
     */
    public void checkLiquidInArea(World world, int centerX, int centerY, int centerZ,
                                   int radiusY, Consumer<Boolean> callback) {
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                for (int dy = -radiusY; dy <= radiusY; dy++) {
                    Block block = world.getBlockAt(centerX, centerY + dy, centerZ);
                    if (block.isLiquid()) {
                        callback.accept(true);
                        return;
                    }
                }
                callback.accept(false);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                callback.accept(false);
            }
        });
    }
    
    /**
     * Pre-cache blocks around a location (call this periodically or on movement).
     */
    public void preCacheArea(World world, int centerX, int centerY, int centerZ, int radius) {
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> {
            try {
                long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
                for (int dx = -radius; dx <= radius; dx++) {
                    for (int dy = -2; dy <= 1; dy++) {
                        for (int dz = -radius; dz <= radius; dz++) {
                            int bx = centerX + dx;
                            int by = centerY + dy;
                            int bz = centerZ + dz;
                            
                            String key = cacheKey(world.getUID(), bx, by, bz);
                            CachedBlock cached = blockCache.get(key);
                            
                            if (cached == null || cached.isExpired()) {
                                Block block = world.getBlockAt(bx, by, bz);
                                blockCache.put(key, new CachedBlock(block.getType(), now));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        });
    }
    
    /**
     * Cleanup expired cache entries.
     */
    public void cleanup() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        blockCache.entrySet().removeIf(e -> e.getValue().isExpiredAt(now));
    }
    
    public void shutdown() {
        executor.shutdown();
        try {
            if (!executor.awaitTermination(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS)) {
                executor.shutdownNow();
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            executor.shutdownNow();
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        blockCache.clear();
    }
    
    private String cacheKey(UUID worldId, int x, int y, int z) {
        return worldId.toString() + ":" + x + ":" + y + ":" + z;
    }
    
    private static class CachedBlock {
        final Material material;
        final long timestamp;
        
        CachedBlock(Material material, long timestamp) {
            this.material = material;
            this.timestamp = timestamp;
        }
        
        boolean isExpired() {
            return System.currentTimeMillis() - timestamp > CACHE_TTL_MS;
        }
        
        boolean isExpiredAt(long now) {
            return now - timestamp > CACHE_TTL_MS;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/DiscordService.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.util.MsgUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.URL;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public final class DiscordService {

    private final Configs configs;

    // Cache Crafatar availability to avoid repeated checks (milliseconds)
    private volatile boolean crafatarAvailable = true;
    private volatile long crafatarLastChecked = 0L;
    private static final long CRAFATAR_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000L; // 5 minutes

    public DiscordService(Configs configs) {
        this.configs = configs;
    }

    public void sendAlert(Player suspect, String check, int addVl, String info) {
        sendAlert(suspect, check, addVl, info, null);
    }

    /**
     * Send an alert and notify a callback with overall result code.
     * callback.accept(0) means at least one webhook succeeded (HTTP < 400).
     * Otherwise callback.accept(lastCode) with the last HTTP code or -1 on exception.
     */
    public void sendAlert(Player suspect, String check, int addVl, String info, java.util.function.IntConsumer callback) {
        if (!configs.plugin().getConfig().getBoolean("discord.enabled", false)) return;
        // Determine webhooks to post to (per-check override > list > legacy single URL)
        java.util.List<String> webhooks = getWebhooksFor(check);
        if (webhooks == null || webhooks.isEmpty()) {
            if (callback != null) callback.accept(-1);
            return;
        }

        String fmt = configs.messages().getString("discord.format",
                "**{player}** was detected by **{check}** (+{vl}) - {info}");

        Map<String, String> vars = new HashMap<>();
        vars.put("player", suspect.getName());
        vars.put("check", check);
        vars.put("vl", String.valueOf(addVl));
        vars.put("info", info == null ? "" : info);

        String content = MsgUtil.format(fmt, vars);
        // Remove color codes (Minecraft § codes) for Discord
        content = content.replaceAll("§.", "");

        final boolean embedEnabled = configs.plugin().getConfig().getBoolean("discord.embed.enabled", true);
        final String username = configs.plugin().getConfig().getString("discord.username", "VirgolaAC");
        final String avatar = configs.plugin().getConfig().getString("discord.avatar-url", "");

        final String payload = embedEnabled ? jsonEncodeEmbed(suspect, check, addVl, info, content, username, avatar)
                : jsonEncodeContent(content);

        // Send asynchronously to each configured webhook with retry/backoff for 429
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(configs.plugin(), () -> {
            int lastCode = -1;
            boolean anySuccess = false;
            for (String webhook : webhooks) {
                int code = sendWithRetries(webhook, payload);
                lastCode = code;
                if (code >= 0 && code < 400) anySuccess = true;
            }
            if (callback != null) {
                if (anySuccess) callback.accept(0);
                else callback.accept(lastCode);
            }
        });
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private int sendWithRetries(String webhook, String payload) {
        final int maxRetries = 3;
        int attempt = 0;
        int lastCode = -1;
        while (attempt < maxRetries) {
            HttpURLConnection conn = null;
            try {
                URL url = new URL(webhook);
                conn = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
                conn.setRequestMethod("POST");
                conn.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");
                conn.setDoOutput(true);

                byte[] out = payload.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                conn.setFixedLengthStreamingMode(out.length);
                conn.connect();
                try (OutputStream os = conn.getOutputStream()) {
                    os.write(out);
                }

                int code = conn.getResponseCode();
                lastCode = code;
                if (code == 429) {
                    // Rate limited: try to read Retry-After
                    String ra = conn.getHeaderField("Retry-After");
                    long waitMs = 2000L * (1L << attempt); // exponential backoff
                    if (ra != null) {
                        try {
                            waitMs = (long) (Double.parseDouble(ra) * 1000L);
                        } catch (NumberFormatException ignored) {}
                    }
                    try { Thread.sleep(waitMs); } catch (InterruptedException ignored) {}
                    attempt++;
                    continue;
                }
                if (code >= 400) {
                    Bukkit.getLogger().warning("VirgolaAC: Discord webhook returned HTTP " + code + " for " + webhook);
                }
                return code;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                Bukkit.getLogger().warning("VirgolaAC: Failed to post to Discord webhook: " + e.getMessage());
                lastCode = -1;
                try { Thread.sleep(1000L * (attempt + 1)); } catch (InterruptedException ignored) {}
                attempt++;
            } finally {
                if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
            }
        }
        return lastCode;
    }

    private java.util.List<String> getWebhooksFor(String check) {
        // Check-specific webhooks first
        try {
            Object mapObj = configs.plugin().getConfig().getConfigurationSection("discord.check-webhooks");
            if (mapObj != null) {
                java.util.List<String> list = configs.plugin().getConfig().getStringList("discord.check-webhooks." + check);
                if (list != null && !list.isEmpty()) return list;
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        // List of webhooks
        try {
            java.util.List<String> list = configs.plugin().getConfig().getStringList("discord.webhooks");
            if (list != null && !list.isEmpty()) return list;
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        // Legacy single webhook
        String legacy = configs.plugin().getConfig().getString("discord.webhook-url", "");
        if (legacy != null && !legacy.isEmpty()) return java.util.List.of(legacy);
        return java.util.List.of();
    }

    /**
     * Check (cached) whether Crafatar is reachable. Performs a lightweight HEAD request
     * no more often than CRAFATAR_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS.
     */
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private boolean isCrafatarAvailable() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now - crafatarLastChecked < CRAFATAR_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS) return crafatarAvailable;
        crafatarLastChecked = now;
        HttpURLConnection conn = null;
        try {
            URL url = new URL("https://crafatar.com/avatars/00000000000000000000000000000000?size=16");
            conn = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
            conn.setRequestMethod("HEAD");
            conn.setConnectTimeout(2000);
            conn.setReadTimeout(2000);
            int code = conn.getResponseCode();
            crafatarAvailable = (code >= 200 && code < 400);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            crafatarAvailable = false;
        } finally {
            if (conn != null) conn.disconnect();
        }
        return crafatarAvailable;
    }

    private String jsonEncodeEmbed(org.bukkit.entity.Player suspect, String check, int vl, String info, String content, String username, String avatarUrl) {
        // Read embed templates
        String titleT = configs.messages().getString("discord.embed.title", "{check} detected");
        String descT = configs.messages().getString("discord.embed.description", "{player} was flagged for {check} (+{vl}) {info}");

        java.util.Map<String, String> vars = new java.util.HashMap<>();
        vars.put("player", suspect.getName());
        vars.put("check", check);
        vars.put("vl", String.valueOf(vl));
        vars.put("info", info == null ? "" : info);
        vars.put("world", suspect.getWorld().getName());
        vars.put("x", String.valueOf(suspect.getLocation().getBlockX()));
        vars.put("y", String.valueOf(suspect.getLocation().getBlockY()));
        vars.put("z", String.valueOf(suspect.getLocation().getBlockZ()));
        vars.put("ping", String.valueOf(suspect.getPing()));

        String title = MsgUtil.format(titleT, vars).replaceAll("§.", "");
        String description = MsgUtil.format(descT, vars).replaceAll("§.", "");

        int color = configs.plugin().getConfig().getInt("discord.embed.color", 16711680); // default red

        // Thumbnail (player head) using Crafatar if available, otherwise fallback to Minotar by username
        String uuidNoDash = suspect.getUniqueId().toString().replace("-", "");
        String thumbnail;
        if (isCrafatarAvailable()) {
            thumbnail = "https://crafatar.com/avatars/" + uuidNoDash + "?size=128&overlay=true&default=steve";
        } else {
            // Fallback service (Minotar) uses username; slightly less robust but usable when Crafatar is down
            thumbnail = "https://minotar.net/avatar/" + suspect.getName() + "/128.png";
        }

        // Debug: log avatar URL and related info to help diagnose missing skins
        try {
            Bukkit.getLogger().info("VirgolaAC: Discord avatar for " + suspect.getName() + " (uuid=" + suspect.getUniqueId() + ") -> " + thumbnail + " (crafatar=" + crafatarAvailable + ")");
            if (avatarUrl != null && !avatarUrl.isEmpty()) {
                Bukkit.getLogger().info("VirgolaAC: Configured discord.avatar-url = " + avatarUrl);
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        String serverName = configs.plugin().getServer().getName();
        String footerText = "VirgolaAC • " + serverName;

        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append('{');
        if (username != null && !username.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append("\"username\":\"").append(escapeJson(username)).append("\",");
        }
        if (avatarUrl != null && !avatarUrl.isEmpty()) {
            sb.append("\"avatar_url\":\"").append(escapeJson(avatarUrl)).append("\",");
        }
        sb.append("\"embeds\":[{");
        sb.append("\"title\":\"").append(escapeJson(title)).append("\",");

        // Description: use the description template, then add the info block if present
        StringBuilder desc = new StringBuilder();
        if (description != null && !description.isEmpty()) {
            desc.append(description);
        }
        if (info != null && !info.isEmpty()) {
            if (desc.length() > 0) desc.append("\n\n");
            desc.append("**Details:**\n```").append(escapeJson(info)).append("```");
        }
        sb.append("\"description\":\"").append(escapeJson(desc.toString())).append("\",");

        sb.append("\"color\":").append(color).append(',');

        // Author = player with head thumbnail
        sb.append("\"author\":{\"name\":\"").append(escapeJson(suspect.getName())).append("\",\"icon_url\":\"")
          .append(escapeJson(thumbnail)).append("\"},");

        // Fields: compact and grouped for readability
        sb.append("\"fields\":[");
        // First row: Check and VL
        sb.append(fieldJson("Check", check, true)).append(',');
        sb.append(fieldJson("VL", String.valueOf(vl), true)).append(',');
        // Second row: World and Ping
        sb.append(fieldJson("World", suspect.getWorld().getName(), true)).append(',');
        sb.append(fieldJson("Ping (ms)", String.valueOf(suspect.getPing()), true)).append(',');
        // Location on its own row (not inline)
        String loc = suspect.getLocation().getBlockX() + ", " + suspect.getLocation().getBlockY() + ", " + suspect.getLocation().getBlockZ();
        sb.append(fieldJson("Location", loc, false));
        sb.append("]");

        // Timestamp and footer
        sb.append(",\"timestamp\":\"").append(java.time.Instant.now().toString()).append('\"');
        sb.append(",\"footer\":{\"text\":\"").append(escapeJson(footerText)).append("\"}");

        sb.append("}]}");
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private static String fieldJson(String name, String value, boolean inline) {
        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
        b.append("{\"name\":\"").append(escapeJson(name)).append("\",\"value\":\"")
         .append(escapeJson(value)).append("\",\"inline\":").append(inline ? "true" : "false").append('}');
        return b.toString();
    }

    private static String escapeJson(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
            char c = s.charAt(i);
            switch (c) {
                case '\\': out.append("\\\\"); break;
                case '"': out.append("\\\""); break;
                case '\n': out.append("\\n"); break;
                case '\r': out.append("\\r"); break;
                default: out.append(c); break;
            }
        }
        return out.toString();
    }

    private static String jsonEncodeContent(String content) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        sb.append('{');
        sb.append("\"content\":\"");
        for (int i = 0; i < content.length(); i++) {
            char c = content.charAt(i);
            switch (c) {
                case '\\': sb.append("\\\\"); break;
                case '"': sb.append("\\\""); break;
                case '\n': sb.append("\\n"); break;
                case '\r': sb.append("\\r"); break;
                default: sb.append(c); break;
            }
        }
        sb.append('\"');
        sb.append('}');
        return sb.toString();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/ExemptionService.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

/**
 * Centralized exemption windows (fail-open).
 * Listeners set short grace periods for legit mechanics (teleports, riptide, velocity, etc.).
 * Checks query this service and skip detection while exempt is active.
 */
public final class ExemptionService {

    // per-player exemptions (reason -> untilMs)
    private final Map<UUID, Map<String, Long>> untilByReason = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public void exempt(Player player, String reason, long durationMs) {
        if (player == null || reason == null) return;
        UUID uuid = player.getUniqueId();
        long until = System.currentTimeMillis() + Math.max(0L, durationMs);
        untilByReason
                .computeIfAbsent(uuid, k -> new ConcurrentHashMap<>())
                .merge(reason, until, (a, b) -> Math.max(a, b));
    }

    public boolean isExempt(Player player) {
        if (player == null) return false;
        return isExempt(player.getUniqueId());
    }

    public boolean isExempt(UUID uuid) {
        Map<String, Long> m = untilByReason.get(uuid);
        if (m == null || m.isEmpty()) return false;

        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        boolean any = false;

        // cleanup expired + check
        for (var it = m.entrySet().iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
            var e = it.next();
            if (e.getValue() <= now) it.remove();
            else any = true;
        }

        if (m.isEmpty()) untilByReason.remove(uuid);
        return any;
    }

    public void clear(UUID uuid) {
        if (uuid == null) return;
        untilByReason.remove(uuid);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/LagCompensation.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

public final class LagCompensation {

    private final TickService tickService;
    private final double pingBase;
    private final double pingPer100;
    private final double tpsBase;
    private final double tpsPerBelow20;

    public LagCompensation(TickService tickService,
                           double pingBase,
                           double pingPer100,
                           double tpsBase,
                           double tpsPerBelow20) {
        this.tickService = tickService;
        this.pingBase = pingBase;
        this.pingPer100 = pingPer100;
        this.tpsBase = tpsBase;
        this.tpsPerBelow20 = tpsPerBelow20;
    }

    public double timingMultiplier(Player p) {
        int ping = Math.max(0, p.getPing());
        double pingMul = pingBase + (ping / 100.0) * pingPer100;

        double tps = tickService.getTps();
        double below = Math.max(0.0, 20.0 - tps);
        double tpsMul = tpsBase + below * tpsPerBelow20;

        return Math.max(0.5, Math.min(3.0, pingMul * tpsMul));
    }
    /** Backwards-compatible alias for checks. */
    public double multiplier(Player p) {
        return timingMultiplier(p);
    }

}
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/PlayerData.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

public final class PlayerData {

    public record RotSample(long timeMs, float yaw, float pitch) {}

    private final Player player;

    // recent rotation samples (packet-based) for analysis
    private final Deque<RotSample> rots = new ArrayDeque<>();

    // packet-sampled horizontal speed using last server Location snapshots
    private volatile Location lastServerLoc;
    private volatile long lastServerLocMs;
    private volatile double lastHSpeed;

    // last packet rotation deltas
    private volatile float lastYaw;
    private volatile float lastPitch;
    private volatile float lastDYaw;
    private volatile float lastDPitch;
    private volatile long lastFlyingMs;

    // exemptions
    private volatile long exemptUntilMs;

    public PlayerData(Player player) {
        this.player = player;
        Location l = player.getLocation();
        this.lastServerLoc = l;
        this.lastServerLocMs = System.currentTimeMillis();
        this.lastYaw = l.getYaw();
        this.lastPitch = l.getPitch();
        this.lastFlyingMs = 0L;
    }

    public Player player() { return player; }

    public void addRotationSample(float yaw, float pitch) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        rots.addLast(new RotSample(now, yaw, pitch));
        while (rots.size() > 40) rots.pollFirst();

        float dy = angleDelta(yaw, lastYaw);
        float dp = pitch - lastPitch;
        lastDYaw = Math.abs(dy);
        lastDPitch = Math.abs(dp);
        lastYaw = yaw;
        lastPitch = pitch;
        lastFlyingMs = now;
    }

    public Deque<RotSample> rots() { return rots; }

    public void updateServerLocation(Location loc) {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        Location prev = lastServerLoc;
        long prevMs = lastServerLocMs;
        lastServerLoc = loc;
        lastServerLocMs = now;

        if (prev != null && prevMs > 0L) {
            double dx = loc.getX() - prev.getX();
            double dz = loc.getZ() - prev.getZ();
            double dist = Math.sqrt(dx*dx + dz*dz);
            double dt = Math.max(1, now - prevMs);
            // blocks per millisecond * 50 -> blocks per tick (approx)
            lastHSpeed = dist / dt * 50.0;
        }
    }

    public double lastHSpeed() { return lastHSpeed; }
    public float lastDYaw() { return lastDYaw; }
    public float lastDPitch() { return lastDPitch; }
    public long lastFlyingMs() { return lastFlyingMs; }

    public float lastYaw() { return lastYaw; }
    public float lastPitch() { return lastPitch; }

    /** Alias used by listeners. */
    public void pushServerLocation(Location loc) {
        updateServerLocation(loc);
    }

    /** Alias used by packet pipeline. */
    public void pushRotation(float yaw, float pitch) {
        addRotationSample(yaw, pitch);
    }

    public void setLastFlyingPacketTimeMs(long timeMs) {
        this.lastFlyingMs = timeMs;
    }


    public void exemptFor(long ms) {
        exemptUntilMs = Math.max(exemptUntilMs, System.currentTimeMillis() + ms);
    }

    public boolean isExempt() {
        return System.currentTimeMillis() < exemptUntilMs;
    }

    private static float angleDelta(float a, float b) {
        float d = a - b;
        while (d <= -180f) d += 360f;
        while (d > 180f) d -= 360f;
        return d;
    }
}
```

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class PlayerDataManager {

    private final Map<UUID, PlayerData> data = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public PlayerData get(Player p) {
        return data.computeIfAbsent(p.getUniqueId(), id -> new PlayerData(p));
    }

    public void remove(UUID uuid) {
        data.remove(uuid);
    }

    public void remove(Player p) {
        if (p != null) remove(p.getUniqueId());
    }

}
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/TickService.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.plugin.java.JavaPlugin;

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

public final class TickService {

    private final Deque<Long> samples = new ArrayDeque<>();
    private volatile double tps = 20.0;

    public void start(JavaPlugin plugin) {
        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(plugin, () -> {
            long now = System.nanoTime();
            samples.addLast(now);
            while (samples.size() > 100) samples.pollFirst();
            if (samples.size() >= 2) {
                long first = samples.peekFirst();
                long last = samples.peekLast();
                long dt = last - first;
                if (dt > 0) {
                    double seconds = dt / 1_000_000_000.0;
                    double ticks = samples.size() - 1;
                    double calc = ticks / seconds;
                    tps = Math.min(20.0, Math.max(0.0, calc));
                }
            }
        }, 1L, 1L);
    }

    public double getTps() {
        return tps;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/core/ViolationService.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.core;

import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;

import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class ViolationService {

    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
	private final Configs configs;
    private final AlertService alertService;

    // uuid -> (checkId -> vl)
    private final Map<UUID, Map<String, Integer>> vls = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    public ViolationService(Configs configs, AlertService alertService) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.alertService = alertService;
    }

    public int addVl(PlayerData data, String checkId, int add, String info) {
        Map<String, Integer> m = vls.computeIfAbsent(data.player().getUniqueId(), u -> new ConcurrentHashMap<>());
        int newVl = m.merge(checkId, add, (a, b) -> a + b);

        // alert always (you can gate by config later)
        alertService.alert(data.player(), checkId, add, info);

        return newVl;
    }

    /** Backwards-compatible alias for checks. */
    public int flag(PlayerData data, String checkId, int add, String info) {
        return addVl(data, checkId, add, info);
    }

    /** Backwards-compatible alias for checks that only have a Player reference. */
    public int flag(org.bukkit.entity.Player player, String checkId, int add, String info) {
        Map<String, Integer> m = vls.computeIfAbsent(player.getUniqueId(), u -> new ConcurrentHashMap<>());
        int newVl = m.merge(checkId, add, (a, b) -> a + b);
        alertService.alert(player, checkId, add, info);
        return newVl;
    }

    public int getVl(UUID uuid, String checkId) {
        Map<String, Integer> m = vls.get(uuid);
        if (m == null) return 0;
        return m.getOrDefault(checkId, 0);
    }
    
    /** Get all violations for a player */
    public Map<String, Integer> getViolations(UUID uuid) {
        Map<String, Integer> m = vls.get(uuid);
        if (m == null) return Collections.emptyMap();
        return Collections.unmodifiableMap(m);
    }
    
    /** Get total VL for a player across all checks */
    public int getTotalVl(UUID uuid) {
        Map<String, Integer> m = vls.get(uuid);
        if (m == null) return 0;
        return m.values().stream().mapToInt(Integer::intValue).sum();
    }

    public void reset(UUID uuid) {
        vls.remove(uuid);
    }
    
    /** Reset all violations for all players */
    public void resetAll() {
        vls.clear();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/gui/AcGui.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.gui;

import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.Check;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckManager;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.TickService;
import it.virgola.anticheat.util.ColorUtil;
import org.bukkit.Bukkit;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryCloseEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.AsyncPlayerChatEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.Inventory;
import org.bukkit.inventory.InventoryHolder;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemFlag;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;
import org.bukkit.inventory.meta.ItemMeta;
import org.bukkit.plugin.Plugin;
import org.bukkit.scheduler.BukkitTask;

import java.util.*;

public final class AcGui implements Listener {

    private final Configs configs;
    private final CheckManager checkManager;
    private final TickService tickService;
    private final Plugin plugin;
    
    // Track open inventories and their update tasks
    private final Map<UUID, BukkitTask> updateTasks = new HashMap<>();
    private final Set<UUID> openGuis = new HashSet<>();
    // Settings view contexts keyed by Inventory to avoid timing races when switching inventories
    private final Map<Inventory, SettingsContext> invSettings = new WeakHashMap<>();
    // Pending chat-input contexts for editing config values (player -> chat context)
    private final Map<UUID, ChatInputContext> chatInputContexts = new HashMap<>();
    
    // Slot for server status (bottom row, center)
    private static final int STATUS_SLOT = 49;
    // Slots for decorative glass panes (bottom row except center)
    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private static final int[] GLASS_SLOTS = {45, 46, 47, 48, 50, 51, 52, 53};

    public AcGui(Configs configs, CheckManager checkManager, TickService tickService, Plugin plugin) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.checkManager = checkManager;
        this.tickService = tickService;
        this.plugin = plugin;
    }
    
    // Backwards compatibility constructor
    public AcGui(Configs configs, CheckManager checkManager) {
        this.configs = configs;
        this.checkManager = checkManager;
        this.tickService = null;
        this.plugin = null;
    }

    private record Holder(CheckId id) implements InventoryHolder {
        @Override public Inventory getInventory() { return null; }
    }
    
    private void hideItemAttributes(ItemMeta meta) {
        if (meta == null) return;
        meta.addItemFlags(
            ItemFlag.HIDE_ATTRIBUTES,
            ItemFlag.HIDE_ENCHANTS,
            ItemFlag.HIDE_UNBREAKABLE
        );
    }
    
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    public void open(Player p) {
        int size = 54;
        String title = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.title", "&d&lVirgolaAC");
        Inventory inv = Bukkit.createInventory(new Holder(null), size, ColorUtil.color(title));

        // Fill decoration (top and bottom rows with glass panes)
        ItemStack glass = makeGlassPane();
        for (int i = 0; i < 9; i++) inv.setItem(i, glass);
        for (int i = 45; i < 54; i++) {
            if (i == STATUS_SLOT) continue;
            inv.setItem(i, glass);
        }

        // Place check items in the main area (first 45 slots)
        CheckId[] ids = CheckId.values();
        for (int i = 0; i < ids.length && i < 45; i++) {
            CheckId id = ids[i];
            boolean enabled = checkManager.isEnabled(id);
            inv.setItem(i, makeCheckItem(p, id, enabled));
        }

        // Server status item
        inv.setItem(STATUS_SLOT, makeStatusItem(p));

        p.openInventory(inv);
        openGuis.add(p.getUniqueId());

        // Start dynamic update task (every 20 ticks = 1 second)
        if (plugin != null && tickService != null) {
            BukkitTask task = Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskTimer(plugin, () -> {
                if (!openGuis.contains(p.getUniqueId())) {
                    return;
                }
                Player player = Bukkit.getPlayer(p.getUniqueId());
                if (player == null || player.getOpenInventory() == null || player.getOpenInventory().getTopInventory() == null
                        || player.getOpenInventory().getTopInventory().getHolder() == null
                        || !(player.getOpenInventory().getTopInventory().getHolder() instanceof Holder)) {
                    return;
                }
                // Update only the status item
                player.getOpenInventory().getTopInventory().setItem(STATUS_SLOT, makeStatusItem(player));
            }, 20L, 20L);

            updateTasks.put(p.getUniqueId(), task);
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    public void openDetail(Player p, CheckId id) {
        Check c = checkManager.get(id);
        if (c == null) return;

        String title = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.title", "&d&lVirgolaAC &8- &f{check}")
                .replace("{check}", id.name());
        Inventory inv = Bukkit.createInventory(new Holder(id), 27, ColorUtil.color(title));

        // Info item
        inv.setItem(11, makeDetailInfoItem(p, id, c));

        // Toggle item
        inv.setItem(13, makeDetailToggleItem(p, id, c.enabled()));

        // Placeholder: settings / violations
        inv.setItem(15, makeDetailSettingsItem(p, id));

        // Back button
        inv.setItem(22, makeBackItem());

        p.openInventory(inv);
    }

    private static final class SettingsContext {
        final CheckId id;
        final Map<Integer, String> slotToPath;

        SettingsContext(CheckId id, Map<Integer, String> slotToPath) {
            this.id = id;
            this.slotToPath = slotToPath;
        }
    }

    private static final class ChatInputContext {
        final CheckId id;
        final String path;
        final Class<?> type;

        ChatInputContext(CheckId id, String path, Class<?> type) {
            this.id = id;
            this.path = path;
            this.type = type;
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings({ "deprecation", "unused" })
    private ItemStack makeCheckItem(Player p, CheckId id, boolean enabled) {
        // Determine wrap length
        int wrap = configs.messages().getInt("gui.panel.wrap-length", 35);

        // Prefer per-check material, then role material, then colored wool
        String perMat = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.checks." + id.name() + ".material", null);
        String role = getRole(p);
        String roleMat = configs.messages().getString("gui.roles." + role + ".material", null);

        Material mat = null;
        if (perMat != null) {
            try { mat = Material.valueOf(perMat); } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) { mat = null; }
        }
        if (mat == null && roleMat != null) {
            try { mat = Material.valueOf(roleMat); } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) { mat = null; }
        }
        if (mat == null) mat = enabled ? Material.LIME_WOOL : Material.RED_WOOL;

        // If disabled, prefer disabled-material replacement
        if (!enabled) {
            String disMat = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.checks." + id.name() + ".disabled-material", null);
            if (disMat != null) {
                try { mat = Material.valueOf(disMat); } catch (IllegalArgumentException ignored) { }
            }
        }

        ItemStack it = new ItemStack(mat);
        ItemMeta meta = it.getItemMeta();
        if (meta != null) {
            // Display name
            String namePath = enabled ? "gui.panel.check-item.name-enabled" : "gui.panel.check-item.name-disabled";
            String displayName = configs.messages().getString(namePath, (enabled ? "&a&l" : "&c&l") + id.name());
            displayName = displayName.replace("{check}", id.name());
            meta.setDisplayName(ColorUtil.color(displayName));

            // Only short lore in main panel — full description lives in the detail Info.
            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();
            // If disabled and a dedicated disabled-item config exists, use it (but do not inject full descriptions)
            List<String> disabledCfg = configs.messages().getStringList("gui.panel.checks." + id.name() + ".disabled-item.lore");
            String disabledName = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.checks." + id.name() + ".disabled-item.name", null);
            if (!enabled && ((disabledCfg != null && !disabledCfg.isEmpty()) || disabledName != null)) {
                if (disabledName != null) meta.setDisplayName(ColorUtil.color(disabledName.replace("{check}", id.name())));
                if (disabledCfg != null) {
                    for (String s : disabledCfg) {
                        if (s == null) continue;
                        // avoid inserting long descriptions here — replace {description} with a hint
                        String line = s.replace("{check}", id.name()).replace("{description}", configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.check-item.description-hint", "(see details)"));
                        // use configured line as-is (messages.yml controls color/format)
                        lore.add(ColorUtil.color(line));
                    }
                }
            } else {
                List<String> shortCfg = configs.messages().getStringList("gui.panel.check-item.short-lore");
                if (shortCfg != null && !shortCfg.isEmpty()) {
                    for (String s : shortCfg) {
                        if (s == null) continue;
                        String line = s.replace("{check}", id.name()).replace("{type}", id.type()).replace("{state}", enabled ? "&a✔ Attivo" : "&c✘ Disattivo");
                        // use configured line as-is (messages.yml controls color/format)
                        lore.add(ColorUtil.color(line));
                    }
                } else {
                    lore.add(ColorUtil.color("&e&lClick per maggiori informazioni"));
                }
            }

            meta.setLore(lore);
            hideItemAttributes(meta);
            it.setItemMeta(meta);
        }
        return it;
    }
    
        /** Format a long description into a list of lore lines with bullets and continuation prefix. */
        private List<String> formatDescription(String desc, int wrap) {
            List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
            if (desc == null || desc.isEmpty()) return out;

            String bullet = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.bullet", "&e- ");
            String cont = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.continuation", "  ");

            String[] paragraphs = desc.split("\\n");
            for (String para : paragraphs) {
                List<String> lines = wrap(para.trim(), wrap);
                if (lines.isEmpty()) continue;
                // first line with bullet
                out.add(ColorUtil.color(bullet + lines.get(0)));
                // continuation lines
                for (int i = 1; i < lines.size(); i++) {
                    out.add(ColorUtil.color(cont + lines.get(i)));
                }
            }

            return out;
        }

    /** Simple word-wrap returning lines not longer than max (tries to respect word boundaries). */
    private List<String> wrap(String text, int max) {
        if (text == null) return Collections.emptyList();
        List<String> out = new ArrayList<>();
        String[] words = text.split(" ");
        StringBuilder cur = new StringBuilder();
        for (String w : words) {
            if (cur.length() == 0) {
                cur.append(w);
            } else if (cur.length() + 1 + w.length() <= max) {
                cur.append(' ').append(w);
            } else {
                out.add(cur.toString());
                cur = new StringBuilder(w);
            }
        }
        if (cur.length() > 0) out.add(cur.toString());
        return out;
    }
    
    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private ItemStack makeStatusItem(Player p) {
        ItemStack it = new ItemStack(Material.BEACON);
        ItemMeta meta = it.getItemMeta();
        if (meta != null) {
            String name = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.status-item.name", "&b&lStato Server");
            meta.setDisplayName(ColorUtil.color(name));

            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();

            // TPS
            double tps = tickService != null ? tickService.getTps() : 20.0;
            String tpsColor = tps >= 19 ? "&a" : (tps >= 15 ? "&e" : "&c");
            String tpsTpl = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.status-item.tps", "&7TPS: {color}{tps}");
            lore.add(ColorUtil.color(tpsTpl.replace("{color}", tpsColor).replace("{tps}", String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", tps))));

            // Online players
            int online = Bukkit.getOnlinePlayers().size();
            int max = Bukkit.getMaxPlayers();
            String playersTpl = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.status-item.players", "&7Players: &f{online}&7/&f{max}");
            lore.add(ColorUtil.color(playersTpl.replace("{online}", String.valueOf(online)).replace("{max}", String.valueOf(max))));

            // Active checks
            int activeChecks = checkManager.enabledCount();
            int totalChecks = CheckId.values().length;
            String checksTpl = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.status-item.active-checks", "&7Checks attivi: &a{active}&7/&f{total}");
            lore.add(ColorUtil.color(checksTpl.replace("{active}", String.valueOf(activeChecks)).replace("{total}", String.valueOf(totalChecks))));

            // Memory usage
            Runtime runtime = Runtime.getRuntime();
            long usedMem = (runtime.totalMemory() - runtime.freeMemory()) / 1024 / 1024;
            long maxMem = runtime.maxMemory() / 1024 / 1024;
            double memPercent = (double) usedMem / maxMem * 100;
            String memColor = memPercent < 70 ? "&a" : (memPercent < 85 ? "&e" : "&c");
            String memTpl = configs.messages().getString("gui.panel.status-item.memory", "&7RAM: {color}{used}MB&7/&f{max}MB");
            lore.add(ColorUtil.color(memTpl.replace("{color}", memColor).replace("{used}", String.valueOf(usedMem)).replace("{max}", String.valueOf(maxMem))));

            meta.setLore(lore);
            hideItemAttributes(meta);
            it.setItemMeta(meta);
        }
        return it;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private ItemStack makeDetailInfoItem(Player p, CheckId id, Check c) {
        ItemStack it = new ItemStack(Material.PAPER);
        ItemMeta meta = it.getItemMeta();
        if (meta != null) {
            String name = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.info-item.name", "&b&lInfo &8- &f{check}")
                    .replace("{check}", id.name());
            meta.setDisplayName(ColorUtil.color(name));

            List<String> cfg = configs.messages().getStringList("gui.detail.info-item.lore");
            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();
            int wrap = configs.messages().getInt("gui.panel.wrap-length", 35);

            if (cfg != null && !cfg.isEmpty()) {
                for (String s : cfg) {
                    if (s == null) continue;
                    if (s.contains("{description}")) {
                        String extrasHeader = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.extras-header", "&6&lDettagli:");
                        lore.add(ColorUtil.color(extrasHeader));
                        String customDesc = configs.messages().getString("gui.templates." + id.name() + ".description", id.description());
                        List<String> descLines = formatDescription(customDesc, wrap);
                        lore.addAll(descLines);
                    } else {
                        String replaced = s.replace("{type}", id.type())
                                           .replace("{check}", id.name())
                                           .replace("{state}", c.enabled() ? "&a✔ Attivo" : "&c✘ Disattivo");
                        lore.add(ColorUtil.color(replaced));
                    }
                }
            } else {
                String sep = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.info-item.separator", "&7──────────────────");
                String typeLine = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.info-item.type", "&eTipo: &f{type}").replace("{type}", id.type());
                String stateLine = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.info-item.state", "&eStato: &f{state}").replace("{state}", (c.enabled() ? "&a✔ Attivo" : "&c✘ Disattivo"));
                String extrasHeader = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.extras-header", "&6&lDettagli:");
                lore.add(ColorUtil.color(sep));
                lore.add(ColorUtil.color(typeLine));
                lore.add("");
                lore.add(ColorUtil.color(extrasHeader));
                String customDesc = configs.messages().getString("gui.templates." + id.name() + ".description", id.description());
                lore.addAll(formatDescription(customDesc, wrap));
                lore.add("");
                lore.add(ColorUtil.color(stateLine));
            }

            // Remove duplicate header if messages.yml placed the name as first lore line
            String disp = ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.info-item.name", "&d&l{check}").replace("{check}", id.name()));
            if (!lore.isEmpty() && lore.get(0).equals(disp)) {
                lore.remove(0);
            }

            // Append optional template extras for this check
            List<String> extras = configs.messages().getStringList("gui.templates." + id.name() + ".extra");
            if (extras != null && !extras.isEmpty()) {
                lore.add("");
                for (String ex : extras) lore.add(ColorUtil.color(ex.replace("{check}", id.name())));
            }

            meta.setLore(lore);
            hideItemAttributes(meta);
            it.setItemMeta(meta);
        }
        return it;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private ItemStack makeDetailToggleItem(Player p, CheckId id, boolean enabled) {
        String namePath = enabled ? "gui.detail.toggle-item.name-enabled" : "gui.detail.toggle-item.name-disabled";
        String name = configs.messages().getString(namePath, (enabled ? "&a&lDisattiva {check}" : "&c&lAttiva {check}"))
                .replace("{check}", id.name());

        ItemStack it = new ItemStack(mat);
        ItemMeta meta = it.getItemMeta();
        if (meta != null) {
            meta.setDisplayName(ColorUtil.color(name));
            List<String> cfg = configs.messages().getStringList("gui.detail.toggle-item.lore");
            List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();
            if (cfg != null && !cfg.isEmpty()) {
                for (String s : cfg) lore.add(ColorUtil.color(s.replace("{action}", enabled ? "disattivare" : "attivare").replace("{check}", id.name())));
            }
            meta.setLore(lore);
            hideItemAttributes(meta);
            it.setItemMeta(meta);
        }
        return it;
    }

    private String getRole(Player p) {
        if (p.hasPermission("virgolaac.admin")) return "admin";
        if (p.hasPermission("virgolaac.mod")) return "mod";
        return "default";
    }

    @EventHandler
    public void onClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player p)) return;
        if (event.getInventory() == null || !(event.getInventory().getHolder() instanceof Holder)) return;

        event.setCancelled(true);



        Holder holder = (Holder) event.getInventory().getHolder();
        CheckId holderId = holder.id();

        int slot = event.getRawSlot();
        if (slot < 0 || slot >= event.getInventory().getSize()) return;

        // If this inventory has a settings context, handle settings interactions
        SettingsContext sctx = invSettings.get(event.getInventory());
        if (sctx != null) {
            handleSettingsClick(p, event, sctx);
            return;
        }

        // If main panel
        if (holderId == null) {
            // Ignore clicks on glass panes and status item
            if (slot >= 45) return;

            CheckId[] ids = CheckId.values();
            if (slot >= ids.length) return;

            CheckId id = ids[slot];
            // Open detail view
            openDetail(p, id);
            return;
        }

        // Detail inventory interactions
        CheckId id = holderId;
        // Toggle slot (13)
        if (slot == 13) {
            boolean newState = checkManager.toggle(id);
            // update toggle and info
            event.getInventory().setItem(13, makeDetailToggleItem(p, id, newState));
            event.getInventory().setItem(11, makeDetailInfoItem(p, id, checkManager.get(id)));
            p.updateInventory();
            return;
        }

        // Settings / violations slot (15) -> open settings viewer
        if (slot == 15) {
            openSettings(p, id);
            return;
        }

        // Back button (22)
        if (slot == 22) {
            p.closeInventory();
            open(p);
            return;
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    @EventHandler
    public void onPlayerChat(AsyncPlayerChatEvent event) {
        UUID uuid = event.getPlayer().getUniqueId();
        if (!chatInputContexts.containsKey(uuid)) return;

        // We expect this chat message as the next value for a config param
        event.setCancelled(true);
        ChatInputContext ctx = chatInputContexts.remove(uuid);
        if (ctx == null) return;

        String msg = event.getMessage().trim();
        Player p = event.getPlayer();

        if (msg.equalsIgnoreCase("cancel") || msg.equalsIgnoreCase("annulla")) {
            p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.cancelled", "&cModifica annullata.")));
            // Reopen settings inventory
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> openSettings(p, ctx.id));
            return;
        }

        // Parse based on expected type
        Object parsed = null;
        try {
            if (ctx.type == Boolean.class || ctx.type == boolean.class) {
                String v = msg.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
                if (v.equals("true") || v.equals("on") || v.equals("yes") || v.equals("si")) parsed = true;
                else if (v.equals("false") || v.equals("off") || v.equals("no")) parsed = false;
                else {
                    p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.invalid-boolean", "&cValore booleano non valido. Usa true/false o on/off.")));
                    Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> openSettings(p, ctx.id));
                    return;
                }
            } else if (Number.class.isAssignableFrom(ctx.type) || ctx.type == int.class || ctx.type == double.class || ctx.type == float.class || ctx.type == long.class) {
                // Try parse double; we'll store as integer if original was integer
                double d = Double.parseDouble(msg.replace(',', '.'));
                // Determine original type by inspecting current config value
                Object cur = configs.checks().cfg().get(ctx.path);
                if (cur instanceof Integer) parsed = (int) Math.round(d);
                else if (cur instanceof Long) parsed = (long) Math.round(d);
                else if (cur instanceof Float) parsed = (float) d;
                else parsed = d;
            } else if (List.class.isAssignableFrom(ctx.type) || configs.checks().cfg().get(ctx.path) instanceof java.util.List) {
                // parse comma-separated list of strings
                String[] parts = msg.split(",");
                List<String> list = new ArrayList<>();
                for (String s : parts) {
                    String t = s.trim();
                    if (!t.isEmpty()) list.add(t);
                }
                parsed = list;
            } else {
                // treat as string
                parsed = msg;
            }
        } catch (NumberFormatException ex) {
            p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.invalid-number", "&cValore numerico non valido.")));
            Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> openSettings(p, ctx.id));
            return;
        }

        // Save value
        configs.checks().set(ctx.path, parsed);
        if (plugin != null) Bukkit.getScheduler().runTaskAsynchronously(plugin, () -> configs.checks().save());
        else configs.checks().save();

        p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.saved", "&aValore salvato.")));
        // Reopen settings inventory on main thread
        // If this was the enabled flag for the check, ensure CheckManager reflects it
        String enabledPath = "checks." + ctx.id.name() + ".enabled";
        if (enabledPath.equals(ctx.path) && parsed instanceof Boolean) {
            Check ch = checkManager.get(ctx.id);
            if (ch != null) ch.setEnabled((Boolean) parsed);
            // persist already done via configs
            refreshMainPanelForCheck(ctx.id, (Boolean) parsed);
        }

        Bukkit.getScheduler().runTask(plugin, () -> openSettings(p, ctx.id));
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("deprecation")
    private void openSettings(Player p, CheckId id) {
        // Build list of parameters for this check
        String base = "checks." + id.name();
        org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration cfg = configs.checks().cfg();

        // First look for params section
        org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection params = cfg.getConfigurationSection(base + ".params");
        Map<String, Object> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        if (params != null) {
            for (String key : params.getKeys(false)) {
                map.put(key, params.get(key));
            }
        } else {
            // fallback: include top-level keys and sub_checks if present
            org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection root = cfg.getConfigurationSection(base);
            if (root != null) {
                for (String key : root.getKeys(false)) {
                    if ("sub_checks".equals(key)) continue;
                    map.put(key, root.get(key));
                }
                org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection subs = root.getConfigurationSection("sub_checks");
                if (subs != null) {
                    for (String sub : subs.getKeys(false)) {
                        org.bukkit.configuration.ConfigurationSection sc = subs.getConfigurationSection(sub);
                        if (sc == null) continue;
                        for (String k : sc.getKeys(false)) {
                            map.put("sub_checks." + sub + "." + k, sc.get(k));
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (map.isEmpty()) {
            p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.not-implemented", "&eNessun parametro configurabile.")));
            return;
        }

        int count = map.size();
        int rows = Math.max(1, (int) Math.ceil(count / 9.0));
        int size = rows * 9;
        Inventory inv = Bukkit.createInventory(new Holder(id), size, ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-inventory-title", "&e&lSettings &8- &f{check}").replace("{check}", id.name())));

        int slot = 0;
        Map<Integer, String> slotToPath = new HashMap<>();
        boolean hasParamsSection = cfg.getConfigurationSection(base + ".params") != null;
        for (Map.Entry<String, Object> e : map.entrySet()) {
            String key = e.getKey();
            String fullPath;
            // If this entry came from sub_checks we kept the prefix
            if (key.startsWith("sub_checks.")) {
                fullPath = base + "." + key;
            } else if (hasParamsSection) {
                // Most checks store values under checks.<CHECK>.params.<key>
                fullPath = base + ".params." + key;
            } else {
                fullPath = base + "." + key;
            }

            ItemStack it = new ItemStack(Material.PAPER);
            ItemMeta meta = it.getItemMeta();
            if (meta != null) {
                String paramNameFmt = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.param-name-format", "&6{param}");
                meta.setDisplayName(ColorUtil.color(paramNameFmt.replace("{param}", key)));
                List<String> lore = new ArrayList<>();
                Object val = cfg.get(fullPath);

                // Add a user-friendly description if available in messages.yml
                // fallback attempts
                String desc = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.descriptions." + id.name() + "." + key, null);
                if (desc == null) desc = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.descriptions." + id.name() + "." + key.replace('.', '_'), null);
                if (desc == null) desc = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.descriptions." + key, null);
                int wrapLen = configs.messages().getInt("gui.panel.wrap-length", 35);
                if (desc != null) {
                    List<String> dlines = wrap(desc, wrapLen);
                    for (String dl : dlines) lore.add(ColorUtil.color(dl));
                    lore.add("");
                }

                String valueTpl = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.value", "&7Value: &f{value}");
                lore.add(ColorUtil.color(valueTpl.replace("{value}", String.valueOf(val))));
                lore.add("");
                if (val instanceof Boolean) {
                    lore.add(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.click-toggle", "&eModifica in chat (true/false)")));
                } else if (val instanceof Number) {
                    lore.add(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.left-right", "&eInserisci un numero in chat")));
                } else if (val instanceof java.util.List) {
                    lore.add(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.list-hint", "&eInserisci valori separati da virgola")));
                } else {
                    String typeTpl = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.type", "&7Type: &f{type}");
                    lore.add(ColorUtil.color(typeTpl.replace("{type}", (val == null ? "null" : val.getClass().getSimpleName()))));
                }
                meta.setLore(lore);
                hideItemAttributes(meta);
                it.setItemMeta(meta);
            }

            inv.setItem(slot, it);
            slotToPath.put(slot, fullPath);
            slot++;
        }

        // Register the settings context for this inventory and open it.
        invSettings.put(inv, new SettingsContext(id, slotToPath));
        p.openInventory(inv);
    }

    private void handleSettingsClick(Player p, InventoryClickEvent event, SettingsContext ctx) {
        int slot = event.getRawSlot();
        String path = ctx.slotToPath.get(slot);
        if (path == null) return;
        org.bukkit.configuration.file.YamlConfiguration cfg = configs.checks().cfg();
        Object val = cfg.get(path);

        // Permission check for editing settings
        if (!p.hasPermission("virgolaac.panel.editsettings")) {
            p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color(configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.no-permission", "&cNon hai il permesso per modificare le impostazioni.")));
            return;
        }



        // Always prompt via chat for an explicit new value (provides consistent UX and validation)
        p.closeInventory();
        Class<?> type = val == null ? String.class : val.getClass();
        chatInputContexts.put(p.getUniqueId(), new ChatInputContext(ctx.id, path, type));
        String prompt = configs.messages().getString("gui.detail.settings-item.prompt", "&eInserisci il nuovo valore per {param} in chat. Scrivi 'cancel' per annullare.");
        String human = path.substring(path.lastIndexOf('.') + 1);
        p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color(prompt.replace("{param}", human).replace("{value}", String.valueOf(val == null ? "null" : val))));
        return;
    }

    private void refreshMainPanelForCheck(CheckId id, boolean newState) {
        // Update any open main panel inventories for all tracked players
        CheckId[] ids = CheckId.values();
        int idx = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) if (ids[i] == id) { idx = i; break; }
        if (idx < 0) return;

        for (UUID u : new HashSet<>(openGuis)) {
            Player pl = Bukkit.getPlayer(u);
            if (pl == null) continue;
            if (pl.getOpenInventory() == null) continue;
            Inventory top = pl.getOpenInventory().getTopInventory();
            if (top == null || !(top.getHolder() instanceof Holder) || ((Holder) top.getHolder()).id() != null) continue;
            // Recreate item for this player role
            Player player = pl;
            ItemStack item = makeCheckItem(player, id, newState);
            int slot = idx;
            if (slot < top.getSize()) top.setItem(slot, item);
        }
    }

    @EventHandler
    public void onClose(InventoryCloseEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getInventory().getHolder() instanceof Holder)) return;

        Holder holder = (Holder) event.getInventory().getHolder();
        UUID uuid = event.getPlayer().getUniqueId();
        openGuis.remove(uuid);

        // Cancel update task only if closing main panel
        if (holder.id() == null) {
            BukkitTask task = updateTasks.remove(uuid);
            if (task != null) task.cancel();
        }

        // Remove any settings context associated with this inventory
        invSettings.remove(event.getInventory());
    }
}
```

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.listeners;

import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerDataManager;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerJoinEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerTeleportEvent;

public final class CoreListener implements Listener {

    private final PlayerDataManager dataManager;

    public CoreListener(PlayerDataManager dataManager) {
        this.dataManager = dataManager;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent event) {
        Player p = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData d = dataManager.get(p);
        d.exemptFor(2000);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        dataManager.remove(event.getPlayer());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent event) {
        Player p = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData d = dataManager.get(p);
        d.exemptFor(1500);
        d.pushServerLocation(event.getTo());
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player p = event.getPlayer();
        PlayerData d = dataManager.get(p);
        d.pushServerLocation(event.getTo());
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/listeners/DamageListener.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.listeners;

import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckManager;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerDataManager;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack;

public final class DamageListener implements Listener {

    private final PlayerDataManager dataManager;
    private final CheckManager checkManager;

    public DamageListener(PlayerDataManager dataManager, CheckManager checkManager) {
        this.dataManager = dataManager;
        this.checkManager = checkManager;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = false)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof Player player)) return;
        
        PlayerData data = dataManager.get(player);
        checkManager.handleDamage(data, event);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onEntityDamageByEntity(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player damager)) return;

        // If damager holds a MACE, notify NoFall check so it can ignore fall damage shortly after.
        ItemStack main = damager.getInventory().getItemInMainHand();
        if (main != null && "MACE".equals(main.getType().name())) {
            var check = checkManager.get(it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckId.NOFALL);
            if (check instanceof it.virgola.anticheat.checks.impl.NoFallCheck noFall) {
                noFall.markMaceHit(damager.getUniqueId());
            }
        }
    }
}
```
