`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/listeners/FreezeListener.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.listeners;

import it.virgola.anticheat.commands.AcCommand;
import it.virgola.anticheat.util.ColorUtil;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerMoveEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerQuitEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.inventory.InventoryClickEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerDropItemEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.PlayerInteractEvent;

/**
 * Handles freeze functionality - prevents frozen players from moving/interacting
 */
public final class FreezeListener implements Listener {

    private final AcCommand acCommand;

    public FreezeListener(AcCommand acCommand) {
        this.acCommand = acCommand;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        if (!acCommand.isFrozen(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId())) return;
        
        // Allow head rotation, block position change
        if (event.getFrom().getBlockX() != event.getTo().getBlockX() ||
            event.getFrom().getBlockY() != event.getTo().getBlockY() ||
            event.getFrom().getBlockZ() != event.getTo().getBlockZ()) {
            event.setTo(event.getFrom());
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public void onCommand(PlayerCommandPreprocessEvent event) {
        if (!acCommand.isFrozen(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId())) return;
        
        // Allow only basic commands
        String cmd = event.getMessage().toLowerCase();
        if (!cmd.startsWith("/msg") && !cmd.startsWith("/r") && !cmd.startsWith("/reply")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
            event.getPlayer().sendMessage(ColorUtil.color("&c&lSei freezato! Non puoi usare comandi."));
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public void onQuit(PlayerQuitEvent event) {
        if (!acCommand.isFrozen(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId())) return;
        
        // Player quit while frozen - this is suspicious
        String msg = ColorUtil.color("&c[AC] &f" + event.getPlayer().getName() + " &7si è disconnesso mentre era &cfreezato&7!");
        for (Player p : event.getPlayer().getServer().getOnlinePlayers()) {
            if (p.hasPermission("virgolaac.staff")) {
                p.sendMessage(msg);
            }
        }
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageByEntityEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getDamager() instanceof Player p)) return;
        if (!acCommand.isFrozen(p.getUniqueId())) return;
        
        event.setCancelled(true);
        p.sendMessage(ColorUtil.color("&c&lSei freezato! Non puoi attaccare."));
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public void onInventoryClick(InventoryClickEvent event) {
        if (!(event.getWhoClicked() instanceof Player p)) return;
        if (!acCommand.isFrozen(p.getUniqueId())) return;
        
        event.setCancelled(true);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public void onDrop(PlayerDropItemEvent event) {
        if (!acCommand.isFrozen(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId())) return;
        
        event.setCancelled(true);
        event.getPlayer().sendMessage(ColorUtil.color("&c&lSei freezato! Non puoi droppare oggetti."));
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public void onInteract(PlayerInteractEvent event) {
        if (!acCommand.isFrozen(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId())) return;
        
        event.setCancelled(true);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/listeners/MovementExemptionListener.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.listeners;

import it.virgola.anticheat.core.ExemptionService;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.entity.Projectile;
import org.bukkit.event.EventHandler;
import org.bukkit.event.EventPriority;
import org.bukkit.event.Listener;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageByEntityEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityDamageEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.EntityToggleGlideEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.entity.PlayerDeathEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.player.*;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleEnterEvent;
import org.bukkit.event.vehicle.VehicleExitEvent;

public final class MovementExemptionListener implements Listener {

    private final ExemptionService exemptions;

    public MovementExemptionListener(ExemptionService exemptions) {
        this.exemptions = exemptions;
    }

    // Teleport / change world / respawn -> big desync windows
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onTeleport(PlayerTeleportEvent e) {
        long ms = 1800;
        // Ender pearls give extra velocity
        if (e.getCause() == PlayerTeleportEvent.TeleportCause.ENDER_PEARL) {
            ms = 2200;
        }
        // Chorus fruit has weird landing (using string comparison for compatibility)
        String causeName = e.getCause().name();
        if (causeName.contains("CHORUS")) {
            ms = 1500;
        }
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "teleport", ms);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onWorld(PlayerChangedWorldEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "worldchange", 2500);
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onRespawn(PlayerRespawnEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "respawn", 2500);
    }
    
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onDeath(PlayerDeathEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getEntity(), "death", 2000);
    }

    // Elytra toggle glide (can look like fly/hover)
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onGlide(EntityToggleGlideEvent e) {
        if (e.getEntity() instanceof Player p) {
            // Longer grace for glide stop (can have weird landing momentum)
            long ms = e.isGliding() ? 1500 : 2000;
            exemptions.exempt(p, "elytra", ms);
        }
    }

    // Riptide (trident) gives massive movement
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onRiptide(PlayerRiptideEvent e) {
        // Riptide can give very high velocity for a while
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "riptide", 2000);
    }

    // Velocity changes (knockback, explosions, plugin velocity)
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamageByEntity(EntityDamageByEntityEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getEntity() instanceof Player p)) return;

        long ms = 900;

        switch (e.getCause()) {
            case ENTITY_EXPLOSION, BLOCK_EXPLOSION -> ms = 2000;
            case ENTITY_ATTACK -> ms = 1100;
            case ENTITY_SWEEP_ATTACK -> ms = 1200;
            default -> ms = 900;
        }

        // Projectiles can add extra weird velocity
        if (e.getDamager() instanceof Projectile) {
            ms = Math.max(ms, 1200);
        }

        exemptions.exempt(p, "damagebyentity", ms);
    }

    // Damage (especially explosion / entity attack) often correlates with knockback & weird air ticks
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onDamage(EntityDamageEvent e) {
        if (!(e.getEntity() instanceof Player p)) return;
        
        long ms = switch (e.getCause()) {
            case ENTITY_EXPLOSION, BLOCK_EXPLOSION -> 2000;
            case FALL -> 600; // Fall damage can have weird landing
            case FLY_INTO_WALL -> 800;
            case ENTITY_ATTACK, ENTITY_SWEEP_ATTACK -> 1100;
            default -> 900;
        };
        
        exemptions.exempt(p, "damage", ms);
    }

    // Vehicle enter/exit can also cause desync
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVehicleEnter(VehicleEnterEvent e) {
        if (e.getEntered() instanceof Player p) {
            exemptions.exempt(p, "vehicle_enter", 1200);
        }
    }
    
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onVehicleExit(VehicleExitEvent e) {
        if (e.getExited() instanceof Player p) {
            // Exit can have weird ejection velocity
            exemptions.exempt(p, "vehicle_exit", 1500);
        }
    }
    
    // Sneak toggle (for dismount and other mechanics)
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onSneak(PlayerToggleSneakEvent e) {
        // Very short grace for sneak state changes
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "sneak_toggle", 150);
    }
    
    // Sprint toggle
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onSprint(PlayerToggleSprintEvent e) {
        // Short grace for sprint state changes
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "sprint_toggle", 200);
    }
    
    // Flight toggle
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onFlight(PlayerToggleFlightEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "flight_toggle", 1800);
    }
    
    // Join grace (client is syncing)
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onJoin(PlayerJoinEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "join", 3000);
    }
    
    // Item consume (potions can give instant movement effects)
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onItemConsume(PlayerItemConsumeEvent e) {
        Player p = e.getPlayer();
        Material type = e.getItem().getType();
        
        // Milk bucket removes effects instantly
        if (type == Material.MILK_BUCKET) {
            exemptions.exempt(p, "milk", 600);
            return;
        }
        
        // Potions can give instant speed/slow changes
        if (type == Material.POTION || type == Material.SPLASH_POTION || type == Material.LINGERING_POTION) {
            exemptions.exempt(p, "potion_consume", 800);
        }
        
        // Chorus fruit teleports
        if (type == Material.CHORUS_FRUIT) {
            exemptions.exempt(p, "chorus_fruit", 1500);
        }
        
        // Golden apple effects
        if (type == Material.GOLDEN_APPLE || type == Material.ENCHANTED_GOLDEN_APPLE) {
            exemptions.exempt(p, "golden_apple", 500);
        }
    }
    
    // Bed enter/leave
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onBedEnter(PlayerBedEnterEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "bed_enter", 1000);
    }
    
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onBedLeave(PlayerBedLeaveEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "bed_leave", 1200);
    }
    
    // Game mode change
    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)
    public void onGameModeChange(PlayerGameModeChangeEvent e) {
        exemptions.exempt(e.getPlayer(), "gamemode_change", 1500);
    }
}
```

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.listeners;

import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckManager;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;

        this.dataManager = dataManager;
        this.checkManager = checkManager;
    }

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR, ignoreCancelled = true)
    public void onMove(PlayerMoveEvent event) {
        Player player = event.getPlayer();

    @EventHandler(priority = EventPriority.MONITOR)

        checkManager.handleQuit(event.getPlayer().getUniqueId());
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/packets/PacketIngestor.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.packets;

import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketListenerAbstract;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketListenerPriority;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.event.PacketReceiveEvent;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.protocol.packettype.PacketTypeCommon;
import com.github.retrooper.packetevents.wrapper.play.client.WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying;
import it.virgola.anticheat.checks.CheckManager;
import it.virgola.anticheat.config.Configs;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerData;
import it.virgola.anticheat.core.PlayerDataManager;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;

public final class PacketIngestor extends PacketListenerAbstract {

    @SuppressWarnings("unused")
    private final Configs configs;

    private final PlayerDataManager dataManager;
    private final CheckManager checkManager;

    public PacketIngestor(Configs configs, PlayerDataManager dataManager, CheckManager checkManager) {
        super(PacketListenerPriority.NORMAL);
        this.configs = configs;
        this.dataManager = dataManager;
        this.checkManager = checkManager;
    }

    @Override
    public void onPacketReceive(PacketReceiveEvent event) {
        Object obj = event.getPlayer();
        if (!(obj instanceof Player player)) return;

        PlayerData data = dataManager.get(player);

        PacketTypeCommon type = event.getPacketType();

        // Track movement/rotation only on Flying-related packets.
        // This is crucial: creating/reading the wrapper on non-flying packets causes IndexOutOfBounds.
        if (WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying.isFlying(type)) {
            try {
                WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying w = new WrapperPlayClientPlayerFlying(event);

                // IMPORTANT:
                // In PacketEvents 2.11.x the wrapper reads from the buffer lazily.
                // Calling w.read() manually is not needed and can break if the buffer isn't in the expected state.
                // So: do NOT call w.read().

                if (w.hasRotationChanged()) {
                    // Use location accessors (safe for the flying wrapper).
                    float yaw = w.getLocation().getYaw();
                    float pitch = w.getLocation().getPitch();
                    data.pushRotation(yaw, pitch);
                }

                data.setLastFlyingPacketTimeMs(System.currentTimeMillis());
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {
                // Fail-safe: never allow a packet decode issue to break PacketEvents pipeline.
                // Optional: log only if you have a debug switch.
            }
        }

        // Dispatch to checks (always last)
        checkManager.handlePacket(data, event);
    }
}
```

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.util;

import net.md_5.bungee.api.ChatColor;

public final class ColorUtil {
    private ColorUtil() {}

    public static String color(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        return ChatColor.translateAlternateColorCodes('&', s);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/util/MovementUtil.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.util;

import org.bukkit.Location;
import org.bukkit.Material;
import org.bukkit.World;
import org.bukkit.block.Block;
import org.bukkit.block.data.Levelled;
import org.bukkit.block.data.type.Slab;
import org.bukkit.block.data.type.TrapDoor;
import org.bukkit.entity.Entity;
import org.bukkit.entity.Player;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffect;
import org.bukkit.potion.PotionEffectType;
import org.bukkit.util.BoundingBox;

import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Small, shared movement helpers used across multiple checks.
 * Goal: reduce duplicated "material gates" and keep FP-prone rules consistent.
 * Updated for Paper 1.21.x compatibility and improved false-positive prevention.
 */
public final class MovementUtil {

    private MovementUtil() {}
    
    // ------------------------ constants ------------------------
    
    /** Player hitbox width (0.6 blocks) */
    public static final double PLAYER_WIDTH = 0.6;
    /** Player hitbox height standing (1.8 blocks) */
    public static final double PLAYER_HEIGHT = 1.8;
    /** Player hitbox height sneaking (1.5 blocks) */
    public static final double PLAYER_HEIGHT_SNEAKING = 1.5;
    /** Player hitbox height swimming/crawling (0.6 blocks) */
    public static final double PLAYER_HEIGHT_SWIMMING = 0.6;
    
    /** Vanilla gravity acceleration (blocks/tick²) */
    public static final double GRAVITY = 0.08;
    /** Vanilla air drag (multiplier per tick) */
    public static final double AIR_DRAG = 0.98;
    /** Vanilla jump velocity (blocks/tick) */
    public static final double JUMP_VELOCITY = 0.42;
    /** Max vanilla fall speed (terminal velocity) */
    public static final double TERMINAL_VELOCITY = 3.92;

    // ------------------------ safe access ------------------------

    public static Material safeType(Block b) {
        try { return b.getType(); } catch (Throwable t) { return Material.AIR; }
    }

    // ------------------------ material tags ------------------------

    public static boolean isLiquid(Material m) {
        return m == Material.WATER || m == Material.LAVA || m == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN;
    }

    public static boolean isWater(Material m) {
        return m == Material.WATER || m == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN;
    }

    public static boolean isClimbable(Material m) {
        if (m == null) return false;
        return m == Material.LADDER
                || m == Material.VINE
                || m == Material.SCAFFOLDING
                || m.name().contains("TWISTING_VINES")
                || m.name().contains("WEEPING_VINES");
    }

    /**
     * Blocks that can heavily affect movement/physics and commonly cause FPs.
     * NOTE: ICE is handled elsewhere for Speed, but we still treat it as "weird" for Fly/Jesus.
     */
    public static boolean isWeirdMovementBlock(Material m) {
        if (m == null) return false;
        return m == Material.COBWEB
                || m == Material.SLIME_BLOCK
                || m == Material.HONEY_BLOCK
                || m.name().contains("ICE")
                || m == Material.SOUL_SAND
                || m == Material.SOUL_SOIL;
    }

    /** Very thin collision supports (player can be "not onGround" for a tick in some edge cases). */
    public static boolean isThinSupport(Material m) {
        if (m == null) return false;
        String n = m.name();
        if (n.endsWith("_CARPET")) return true;
        if (m == Material.MOSS_CARPET) return true;
        if (m == Material.LILY_PAD) return true;
        if (m == Material.SNOW) return true; // snow layer
        if (n.endsWith("_PRESSURE_PLATE")) return true;
        if (n.endsWith("_BUTTON")) return true;
        if (n.endsWith("_RAIL") || n.contains("RAIL")) return true;
        if (m == Material.TRIPWIRE || m == Material.TRIPWIRE_HOOK) return true;
        if (m == Material.REDSTONE_WIRE) return true;
        if (m == Material.STRING) return true;
        if (m == Material.POINTED_DRIPSTONE) return true;
        if (m == Material.PINK_PETALS) return true;
        return false;
    }

    /** Surface-only supports that are commonly on water (so Fly/Jesus must not treat as "floating"). */
    public static boolean isLiquidSurfaceSupport(Material m) {
        return m == Material.LILY_PAD;
    }

    // ------------------------ liquid proximity ------------------------

    /** Checks liquid presence by sampling feet and a few depths below. */
    public static boolean isInLiquidRobust(Location to, double... ySamples) {
        if (to == null) return false;
        for (double ys : ySamples) {
            Material t = safeType(to.clone().add(0, ys, 0).getBlock());
            if (isLiquid(t)) return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Returns how far player feet are below the top surface of the nearest sampled liquid block.
     * 0 = exactly at surface; + = below surface.
     * If no liquid is found in sample, returns +Inf.
     */
    public static double surfaceDistanceToLiquid(Location to, double... ySamples) {
        if (to == null) return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
        Block liquid = null;
        for (double ys : ySamples) {
            Block b = to.clone().add(0, ys, 0).getBlock();
            if (isLiquid(safeType(b))) { liquid = b; break; }
        }
        if (liquid == null) return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

        double surfaceY = liquid.getY() + 1.0;
        double dist = surfaceY - to.getY();
        if (dist < 0) dist = 0;
        return dist;
    }

    /** Head-air + liquid nearby (more robust than a single-depth sample). */
    public static boolean isFloatingAtSurface(Player p, Location to, double headYOffset, double... liquidSamples) {
        if (p == null || to == null) return false;

        Location head = to.clone().add(0, headYOffset, 0);
        Material headType = safeType(head.getBlock());
        if (headType != Material.AIR && !headType.isAir()) return false;

        return isInLiquidRobust(to, liquidSamples);
    }

    // ------------------------ solid support scanning ------------------------

    public static double supportDistanceToSolid(Location to, double maxEps, int maxDepth) {
        if (to == null) return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
        World w = to.getWorld();
        if (w == null) return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

        double x = to.getX();
        double z = to.getZ();

        double[] xs = { x - 0.30, x + 0.30 };
        double[] zs = { z - 0.30, z + 0.30 };

        double best = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

        for (double sx : xs) {
            for (double sz : zs) {
                int bx = (int) Math.floor(sx);
                int bz = (int) Math.floor(sz);

                double feetY = to.getY();
                int startY = (int) Math.floor(feetY);

                for (int dy = 0; dy <= maxDepth; dy++) {
                    int by = startY - dy;

                    Material m;
                    try {
                        m = w.getBlockAt(bx, by, bz).getType();
                    } catch (Throwable t) {
                        m = Material.AIR;
                    }

                    if (m == Material.AIR) continue;
                    if (isLiquid(m)) continue;
                    if (isPassableSupport(m)) continue;

                    double top = by + 1.0;
                    double dist = feetY - top;
                    if (dist < 0) dist = 0;

                    if (dist < best) best = dist;
                    if (best <= maxEps) return best;

                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        return best;
    }

    public static boolean isPassableSupport(Material m) {
        return m == Material.SEAGRASS
                || m == Material.TALL_SEAGRASS
                || m == Material.KELP
                || m == Material.KELP_PLANT
                || m == Material.WATER
                || m == Material.LAVA
                || m == Material.BUBBLE_COLUMN;
    }

    // ------------------------ entity support ------------------------

    /**
     * Detects if the player is standing on top of another entity (e.g., boat) without being inside it.
     * Uses bounding boxes when available.
     */
    public static boolean isStandingOnEntitySupport(Player p, Location to, double yEps, double xzRadius) {
        if (p == null || to == null) return false;
        World w = to.getWorld();
        if (w == null) return false;

        // Player AABB approximation at "to"
        BoundingBox playerBox = new BoundingBox(
                to.getX() - 0.30, to.getY(), to.getZ() - 0.30,
                to.getX() + 0.30, to.getY() + 1.80, to.getZ() + 0.30
        );

        for (Entity e : w.getNearbyEntities(to, xzRadius, 1.8, xzRadius)) {
            if (e == null) continue;
            if (e == p) continue;
            if (e.isDead()) continue;

            BoundingBox eb;
            try {
                eb = e.getBoundingBox();
            } catch (Throwable t) {
                // fallback rough box
                Location el = e.getLocation();
                eb = new BoundingBox(el.getX() - 0.35, el.getY(), el.getZ() - 0.35, el.getX() + 0.35, el.getY() + 1.2, el.getZ() + 0.35);
            }

            // horizontal overlap
            boolean xzOverlap = eb.getMaxX() > playerBox.getMinX()
                    && eb.getMinX() < playerBox.getMaxX()
                    && eb.getMaxZ() > playerBox.getMinZ()
                    && eb.getMinZ() < playerBox.getMaxZ();

            if (!xzOverlap) continue;

            double feetY = to.getY();
            double topY = eb.getMaxY();

            // feet close to entity top (standing on it)
            if (feetY >= (topY - yEps) && feetY <= (topY + 0.20)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    // ------------------------ advanced block checks ------------------------
    
    /**
     * Checks if player is near a block edge (can cause 1-tick airborne glitches).
     * Returns true if player is within edgeThreshold of any block edge.
     */
    public static boolean isNearBlockEdge(Location loc, double edgeThreshold) {
        if (loc == null) return false;
        
        double x = loc.getX();
        double z = loc.getZ();
        
        double fracX = x - Math.floor(x);
        double fracZ = z - Math.floor(z);
        
        // Near any block edge (0 or 1 boundaries)
        return fracX < edgeThreshold || fracX > (1.0 - edgeThreshold)
            || fracZ < edgeThreshold || fracZ > (1.0 - edgeThreshold);
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if there's a trapdoor nearby that could affect movement.
     * Open trapdoors can be climbed like ladders in certain conditions.
     */
    public static boolean hasNearbyOpenTrapdoor(Location loc, int radius) {
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) return false;
        
        int cx = loc.getBlockX();
        int cy = loc.getBlockY();
        int cz = loc.getBlockZ();
        
        for (int x = -radius; x <= radius; x++) {
            for (int y = -1; y <= 1; y++) {
                for (int z = -radius; z <= radius; z++) {
                    Block b = loc.getWorld().getBlockAt(cx + x, cy + y, cz + z);
                    Material type = safeType(b);
                    if (type.name().contains("TRAPDOOR")) {
                        try {
                            TrapDoor td = (TrapDoor) b.getBlockData();
                            if (td.isOpen()) return true;
                        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if the player is on a slab or stairs (half blocks that affect step/jump).
     */
    public static boolean isOnHalfBlock(Location loc) {
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) return false;
        
        Block under = loc.clone().add(0, -0.1, 0).getBlock();
        Material type = safeType(under);
        
        if (type.name().endsWith("_SLAB")) {
            try {
                Slab slab = (Slab) under.getBlockData();
                return slab.getType() == Slab.Type.BOTTOM || slab.getType() == Slab.Type.TOP;
            } catch (Throwable ignored) {}
        }
        
        return type.name().endsWith("_STAIRS");
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if player is inside a non-full collision block (fences, walls, etc.).
     * These can cause weird collision/movement behavior.
     */
    public static boolean isInNonFullBlock(Location loc) {
        if (loc == null) return false;
        
        Material feet = safeType(loc.getBlock());
        String name = feet.name();
        
        return name.contains("FENCE") 
            || name.contains("WALL") 
            || name.contains("PANE")
            || name.contains("BARS")
            || name.contains("CHAIN")
            || feet == Material.END_ROD
            || feet == Material.LIGHTNING_ROD;
    }
    
    /**
     * Detects bounce blocks (slime, bed) that can give extra vertical velocity.
     */
    public static boolean isBounceBlock(Material m) {
        if (m == null) return false;
        return m == Material.SLIME_BLOCK 
            || m.name().contains("_BED");
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if block is a powder snow (causes slow sinking).
     */
    public static boolean isPowderSnow(Material m) {
        return m == Material.POWDER_SNOW;
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if player is standing on or near a bounce block.
     */
    public static boolean isNearBounceBlock(Location loc) {
        if (loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) return false;
        
        Material under = safeType(loc.clone().add(0, -0.5, 0).getBlock());
        Material below = safeType(loc.clone().add(0, -1.0, 0).getBlock());
        
        return isBounceBlock(under) || isBounceBlock(below);
    }
    
    /**
     * Returns the water level at a location (0-8, 8 being full block).
     * Returns -1 if not water.
     */
    public static int getWaterLevel(Block block) {
        if (block == null) return -1;
        Material type = safeType(block);
        if (type != Material.WATER) return -1;
        
        try {
            Levelled level = (Levelled) block.getBlockData();
            return level.getMaximumLevel() - level.getLevel();
        } catch (Throwable t) {
            return 8; // assume full if can't read
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if player is in flowing water (can push player).
     */
    public static boolean isInFlowingWater(Location loc) {
        if (loc == null) return false;
        Block b = loc.getBlock();
        int level = getWaterLevel(b);
        return level > 0 && level < 8;
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if player has frost walker boots (can walk on water).
     */
    public static boolean hasFrostWalker(Player p) {
        if (p == null) return false;
        try {
            var boots = p.getInventory().getBoots();
            if (boots == null) return false;
            return boots.getEnchantmentLevel(org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.FROST_WALKER) > 0;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Gets depth strider level (0-3).
     */
    public static int getDepthStriderLevel(Player p) {
        if (p == null) return 0;
        try {
            var boots = p.getInventory().getBoots();
            if (boots == null) return 0;
            int level = boots.getEnchantmentLevel(org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.DEPTH_STRIDER);
            return Math.min(3, Math.max(0, level));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Gets soul speed level (0-3).
     */
    public static int getSoulSpeedLevel(Player p) {
        if (p == null) return 0;
        try {
            var boots = p.getInventory().getBoots();
            if (boots == null) return 0;
            int level = boots.getEnchantmentLevel(org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.SOUL_SPEED);
            return Math.min(3, Math.max(0, level));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Gets swift sneak level (0-3).
     */
    public static int getSwiftSneakLevel(Player p) {
        if (p == null) return 0;
        try {
            var leggings = p.getInventory().getLeggings();
            if (leggings == null) return 0;
            int level = leggings.getEnchantmentLevel(org.bukkit.enchantments.Enchantment.SWIFT_SNEAK);
            return Math.min(3, Math.max(0, level));
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Returns potion amplifier + 1 (so Speed I = 1, Speed II = 2, etc.).
     * Returns 0 if player doesn't have the effect.
     */
    public static int getPotionLevel(Player p, PotionEffectType type) {
        if (p == null || type == null) return 0;
        try {
            PotionEffect eff = p.getPotionEffect(type);
            if (eff == null) return 0;
            return eff.getAmplifier() + 1;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Calculates expected max horizontal speed for a player based on their state.
     * This is a helper for Speed checks.
     */
    public static double calculateMaxHorizontalSpeed(Player p, boolean onGround, boolean sprinting, boolean sneaking) {
        if (p == null) return 10.0; // fail-open
        
        // Base speeds (blocks/sec)
        double base;
        if (sneaking) {
            base = 1.31; // vanilla sneak speed
            int swiftSneak = getSwiftSneakLevel(p);
            if (swiftSneak > 0) {
                base *= (1.0 + 0.15 * swiftSneak); // swift sneak bonus
            }
        } else if (sprinting) {
            base = 5.61; // vanilla sprint speed
        } else {
            base = 4.32; // vanilla walk speed
        }
        
        // Speed potion
        int speedLevel = getPotionLevel(p, PotionEffectType.SPEED);
        if (speedLevel > 0) {
            base *= (1.0 + 0.20 * speedLevel);
        }
        
        // Slowness potion
        int slowLevel = getPotionLevel(p, PotionEffectType.SLOWNESS);
        if (slowLevel > 0) {
            base *= Math.max(0.0, 1.0 - 0.15 * slowLevel);
        }
        
        // Air bonus (strafing is slightly faster)
        if (!onGround) {
            base *= 1.05;
        }
        
        return base;
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if the player is in a crawling pose (swimming on land, or in 1-block gaps).
     */
    public static boolean isCrawling(Player p) {
        if (p == null) return false;
        try {
            // In 1.21+, there's a SWIMMING pose that can happen on land
            return p.getPose().name().equals("SWIMMING") && !p.isInWater();
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if player is in a 1.5-block ceiling (forces sneak height).
     */
    public static boolean isInLowCeiling(Player p, Location loc) {
        if (p == null || loc == null || loc.getWorld() == null) return false;
        
        // Check blocks at head height for standing
        Location head = loc.clone().add(0, 1.8, 0);
        if (safeType(head.getBlock()) != Material.AIR) return true;
        
        // Check slightly above (2.0)
        Location above = loc.clone().add(0, 2.0, 0);
        return safeType(above.getBlock()) != Material.AIR;
    }
    
    /**
     * Returns true if the player recently took damage (for knockback grace).
     */
    public static boolean isRecentlyDamaged(Player p, long maxAgeMs) {
        if (p == null) return false;
        try {
            // NoDamageTicks is in game ticks (20/sec)
            int ndt = p.getNoDamageTicks();
            // Default invulnerability is 10 ticks (500ms)
            return ndt > 0;
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks for berry bushes (slow movement).
     */
    public static boolean isBerryBush(Material m) {
        return m == Material.SWEET_BERRY_BUSH || m == Material.CAVE_VINES || m == Material.CAVE_VINES_PLANT;
    }
    
    /**
     * Checks if player is affected by any movement-slowing block.
     */
    public static boolean isInSlowingBlock(Location loc) {
        if (loc == null) return false;
        Material feet = safeType(loc.getBlock());
        return feet == Material.COBWEB 
            || feet == Material.HONEY_BLOCK 
            || isBerryBush(feet)
            || isPowderSnow(feet);
    }

    public static String fmt2(double d) { return String.format(Locale.US, "%.2f", d); }
    public static String fmt3(double d) { return String.format(Locale.US, "%.3f", d); }
}
```

`src/main/java/it/virgola/anticheat/util/MsgUtil.java`:

```java
package it.virgola.anticheat.util;

import java.util.Map;

public final class MsgUtil {
    private MsgUtil() {}

    public static String format(String template, Map<String, String> vars) {
        if (template == null) return "";
        String out = template;
        if (vars != null) {
            for (Map.Entry<String, String> e : vars.entrySet()) {
                out = out.replace("{" + e.getKey() + "}", e.getValue() == null ? "" : e.getValue());
            }
        }
        return ColorUtil.color(out);
    }
}
```

```yml
      # (e.g. 0.8 means received >= expected * 0.8 satisfies expectation)
      damage_tolerance: 0.8

      # This helps account for packet rounding and measurement differences (default 0.15)

  # Each sub-check has its own threshold and can be toggled
  KILLAURA:
    enabled: true

    alert_cooldown_ms: 1000
    post_flag_reduce: 2.0
    
    sub_checks:

        # Modes to ignore this sub-check for (optional list of GameMode names)
        # ignore_in_modes: [SPECTATOR]

        decay: 0.5
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────

        lag_buffer: 10.0
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 1.5
        threshold: 4.0
        decay: 0.4
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # NOSWING - Attack without swing animation
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      noswing:
        enabled: true
        # Max ms between swing and hit
        max_delay_ms: 100
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 2.0
        threshold: 4.0
        decay: 1.0
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # GCD - Invalid rotation sensitivity
      # Only analyzed during active combat (3s after hit)
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      gcd:
        enabled: true
        # Sample analysis (more samples = fewer false positives)
        sample_size: 30
        min_samples: 20
        # GCD thresholds - very low GCD + low stdDev = aimbot
        min_valid_gcd: 0.005
        max_std_dev: 0.0005
        # Buffer settings (conservative to avoid false positives)
        weight: 1.0
        threshold: 6.0
        decay: 0.5
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # MULTIAURA - Multiple targets too fast
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      multiaura:
        enabled: true
        # Time window to count targets (ms)
        window_ms: 500
        # Max different targets in window (sword cleave = 2)
        max_targets: 2
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 2.0
        threshold: 3.0
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # PATTERN - Robotic rotation consistency
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      pattern:
        enabled: true
        # Minimum samples for analysis
        min_samples: 10
        # Minimum variance (humans have natural jitter)
        min_variance: 0.5
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 1.0
        threshold: 5.0
        decay: 0.2

    # Mode-specific overrides for Killaura behavior.
    # Example: CREATIVE can relax some thresholds or increase reach.
    # Format: checks.KILLAURA.mode_overrides.<GAMEMODE>.sub_checks.<subcheck>.<key>
    mode_overrides:
      CREATIVE:
        sub_checks:
          reach:
            # Keep normal base, but increase creative reach and lag buffer
            creative_max_distance: 5.0
            lag_buffer: 0.35
            threshold: 4.0
          angle:
            max_angle: 65.0
            lag_buffer: 12.0
          noswing:
            max_delay_ms: 150
          gcd:
            min_valid_gcd: 0.006
            max_std_dev: 0.001
          multiaura:
            max_targets: 3
          pattern:
            min_variance: 0.6

```

`src/main/resources/config.yml`:

```yml
# VirgolaAntiCheat - main configuration

prefix: "&d&lVirgolaAC &8» &7"

debug:
  console: true

alerts:
  # Permission that receives alerts in-game
  permission: "virgolaac.alerts"
  # Also log alerts to console
  console: true

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         DISCORD WEBHOOK
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
discord:
  # Enable Discord notifications
  enabled: false
  # The full Discord webhook URL to post messages to
  webhook-url: ""
  # Optional: list of webhook URLs (used if present). Example:
  # webhooks:
  #   - "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/ID/TOKEN"
  webhooks: []
  # Optional: per-check webhooks mapping
  # check-webhooks:
  #   Speed:
  #     - "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/..."
  check-webhooks: {}
  # Username override for the webhook (optional)
  username: "VirgolaAC"
  # Avatar URL for the webhook (optional)
  avatar-url: ""
  # Embed (rich message) settings
  embed:
    enabled: true
    # Decimal color for the embed (e.g., 16711680 = red)
    color: 16711680

lag-compensation:
  # Ping is used as a multiplier for timings.
  # 1.0 means neutral.
  ping_multiplier:
    base: 1.0
    per_100ms: 0.15
  # TPS is used as a multiplier for timings.
  tps_multiplier:
    base: 1.0
    per_tps_below_20: 0.20

mitigation:
  enabled: false
  # Future: soft mitigations like hitbox clamp, place delay, etc.

gui:
  title: "&dVirgolaAntiCheat"

```

`src/main/resources/messages.yml`:

```yml
# ================================================================
#                      VIRGOLA ANTICHEAT - MESSAGES
#                  All messages are customizable below
# ================================================================
# Available placeholders:
#   {prefix}   - Plugin prefix (from config.yml)
#   {player}   - Player name
#   {staff}    - Staff member name
#   {check}    - Check name
#   {vl}       - Violation level
#   {reason}   - Kick/ban reason
#   {message}  - Broadcast message
#   {state}    - ON/OFF state
#   {ping}     - Player ping
#   {gamemode} - Player gamemode

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         GENERAL MESSAGES
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
general:
  no-permission: "{prefix}&cYou do not have permission."
  only-ingame: "{prefix}&cThis command can only be used in-game."
  unknown-command: "{prefix}&7Unknown command. Use &d/ac help"
  player-not-found: "{prefix}&cPlayer &f{player} &cnot found."
  reloaded: "{prefix}&aConfiguration reloaded successfully."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         COMMAND USAGE MESSAGES
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
usage:
  info: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac info &8<&fplayer&8>"
  reset: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac reset &8<&fplayer&7|&fall&8>"
  kick: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac kick &8<&fplayer&8> &7[reason]"
  ban: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac ban &8<&fplayer&8> &7[reason]"
  freeze: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac freeze &8<&fplayer&8>"
  unfreeze: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac unfreeze &8<&fplayer&8>"
  spectate: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac spectate &8<&fplayer&8>"
  tp: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac tp &8<&fplayer&8>"
  broadcast: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac broadcast &8<&fmessage&8>"
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         TOGGLE COMMANDS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
toggle:
  alerts: "{prefix}Alerts &8» {state}"
  verbose: "{prefix}Verbose &8» {state}"
  check: "{prefix}&f{check} &8» {state}"
  state-on: "&aON"
  state-off: "&cOFF"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         VIOLATIONS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
violations:
  reset-player: "{prefix}&aVL reset &7for &f{player}"
  reset-all: "{prefix}&aVL reset &7for &fall players"
  unknown-check: "{prefix}&cCheck &f{check} &cnot found."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         KICK & BAN

punishment:

  ban-broadcast: "{prefix}&f{staff} &8➤ &7banned &f{player} &8• &c{reason}"
  default-reason: "Cheating"

  # Kick screen (multiline using |)
  kick-screen: |

    &c&l⚠ DISCONNECTED

    &7You have been &ckicked &7from the server.
    &7Reason: &f{reason}


  # Ban screen (multiline using |)
  ban-screen: |

    &4&l✖ BANNED

    &7You have been &4permanently banned&7.
    &7Reason: &f{reason}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         FREEZE SYSTEM
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
freeze:
  frozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &8» &efrozen"
  unfrozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &8» &aunfrozen"
  broadcast: "{prefix}&f{staff} &7has frozen &f{player}"
  already-frozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &7is already &efrozen&7."
  not-frozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &7is not frozen."

  # Message shown to frozen player
  frozen-screen: |

    &c&l❄ FROZEN

    &7You have been stopped by &fstaff&7.
    &7Please wait for &instrunctions&7 and do not move.

    &8&oDisconnecting = automatic ban


  # Message shown when player is unfrozen
  unfrozen-screen: |

    &a&l✓ UNFROZEN

    &7You can now resume playing &anormally&7.


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         SPECTATE SYSTEM
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
spectate:
  start: "{prefix}&7Spectating &f{player} &8• &7use &d/ac unspec &7to exit"
  stop: "{prefix}&aSpectate ended &7• position restored"
  cannot-self: "{prefix}&7You cannot spectate &fyourself&7."
  not-spectating: "{prefix}&7You are not spectating anyone."


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         TELEPORT
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
teleport:
  success: "{prefix}&aTeleported &7to &f{player}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         STAFF BROADCAST
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
staff:
  broadcast-format: "&8[&d&lSTAFF&8] &f{staff}&8: &7{message}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         PLAYER INFO (/ac info)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------

  header: "\n&d&l─── &f&l{player} &d&l───"
  status-line: "&7Ping: {ping_color}{ping}ms &8• &7Mode: &f{gamemode}"

  # Dynamic colors for ping

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                       SETTINGS ITEM DESCRIPTIONS
#  Descriptions displayed in the settings GUI for each parameter.
#  Keys are: gui.detail.settings-item.descriptions.<CHECK>.<param>
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
gui:
  detail:
    settings-item:
      descriptions:
        SPEED:
          base_walk_bps: "Base walking speed (blocks/sec). Increasing this value raises the allowed baseline movement speed — players can move faster before the check flags them. Decreasing makes the check stricter and may increase false positives for legitimate players."
          min_bps: "Minimum considered bps for movement measurements. Raising will ignore very small movements; lowering makes the detector more sensitive to tiny motion."
          threshold: "Detection threshold for accumulated speed buffer. Higher threshold reduces false positives but delays flagging; lower threshold makes the system stricter and may increase alerts."
          weight_high: "High severity weight applied to strong speed violations. Increasing this increases the VL per violation; decreasing reduces punishment severity."
          decay_ok: "How quickly the speed buffer decays when behavior is normal. Higher decay clears violations faster; lower decay keeps historical violations longer."

        FLY:
          air_weight: "Relative weight for air-fly evidence. Increasing makes air detections contribute more to the buffer; lowering reduces their influence."
          air_threshold: "Threshold for air fly detection buffer. Raising requires stronger proof to flag; lowering is more sensitive."

        JESUS:
          min_flat_ticks: "Minimum ticks considered 'flat on water' before triggering detection. Increasing makes detector more patient; lowering detects faster but may false-trigger on brief contacts."
          threshold: "Detection threshold for Jesus buffer. Higher = stricter to trigger; lower = more sensitive."

        SCAFFOLD:
          max_angle: "Maximum allowed angle for scaffold-like placement behavior. Increasing allows steeper placements without flags; decreasing tightens detection for straighter builds."
          min_violations: "Number of violations required before an alert is issued. Increasing reduces noisy alerts; lowering makes detection quicker."

        NOFALL:
          min_fall_blocks: "Minimum fall distance (blocks) that should normally cause fall damage. Increasing raises the bar (fewer falls trigger damage expectations); decreasing makes the check consider smaller falls."
          damage_grace_ms: "Grace period (ms) to wait for the damage event after landing. Increase to tolerate lag (avoids false positives), decrease to be stricter."

        BADPACKETS:
          threshold: "Buffer threshold for invalid packets. Higher = fewer reports (more tolerant); lower = more sensitive to malformed or unexpected packets."

        KILLAURA:
          sub_checks.reach.max_distance: "Maximum allowed attack reach (blocks). Increase to allow longer legitimate hits (creative/laggy); decrease to detect extended reach."
          sub_checks.angle.max_angle: "Maximum angle difference allowed between look direction and target for a valid hit. Increasing allows wider aim; decreasing makes the check stricter (flags when not looking)."
          sub_checks.noswing.max_delay_ms: "Maximum allowed ms between swing and hit. Increase to tolerate slower swings/lag; decrease to detect hits without proper swing timing."
          sub_checks.gcd.sample_size: "Number of rotation samples used for GCD analysis. Increasing makes analysis more stable but slower; decreasing makes detection quicker but possibly noisier."
          sub_checks.multiaura.max_targets: "Maximum different targets allowed within the time window. Increasing allows more simultaneous hits; decreasing detects multi-target aimbot behavior sooner."
          sub_checks.pattern.min_variance: "Minimum rotational variance expected from a human. Increasing this requires more jitter (harder to flag); decreasing makes the detector sensitive to robotic consistency."
    discord:
      usage: "/ac discord <subcommand>"
      description: "Manage Discord webhook integration."
      details:
        - "set <webhook>: save the webhook URL and enable Discord alerts (legacy single)"
        - "add <webhook>: add a webhook to the global list"
        - "remove <webhook>: remove a webhook from the global list"
        - "list: show configured global webhooks"
        - "setcheck <check> <webhook>: set a webhook for a specific check"
        - "unsetcheck <check>: remove per-check webhook"
        - "test: send a test alert to the configured webhook(s)"
        - "disable: disable Discord alerts"
      examples:
        - "/ac discord add https://discord.com/api/webhooks/..."
        - "/ac discord test"
        - "/ac discord disable"

  usage: |
    {prefix}&7Discord webhook commands:

    &f/ac discord setcheck <check> <webhook> &8- &7Set a webhook for a specific check
    &f/ac discord unsetcheck <check> &8- &7Remove per-check webhook

    &f/ac discord disable &8- &7Disable Discord alerts

  format: "**{player}** was detected by **{check}** (+{vl}) - {info}"

  embed:
    title: "{check} detected"
    description: "{player} was flagged for {check} (+{vl}) {info}"

      value: "&7Value: &f{value}"
      click-toggle: "&eClick to toggle"
      left-right: "&eLeft click: +  &eRight click: -"
      type: "&7Type: &f{type}"
      no-permission: "&cNon hai il permesso per modificare le impostazioni."
      prompt: "&eInserisci il nuovo valore per {param} in chat. Scrivi 'cancel' per annullare."
      cancelled: "&cModifica annullata."
      saved: "&aValore salvato."
      invalid-number: "&cValore numerico non valido."
      invalid-boolean: "&cValore booleano non valido. Usa true/false o on/off."
  templates:
    SCAFFOLD:
      description: "&fAnalizza l'angolo e la velocità di piazzamento dei blocchi per rilevare &dScaffold &fe &dBridge."
    FLY:
      description: "&fMonitora il movimento verticale e le transizioni di stato (aria, terra) per rilevare il &dFly."
    SPEED:
      description: "&fConfronta la velocità del giocatore con i massimi consentiti per il suo stato per rilevare &dSpeed Hack."
    JESUS:
      description: "&fControlla le interazioni con i liquidi, impedendo ai giocatori di &dcamminare sull'acqua."
    NOFALL:
      description: "&fSi assicura che i giocatori ricevano danno da caduta dopo essere caduti da altezze significative, rilevando &dNoFall."
    BADPACKETS:
      description: "&fIntercetta e analizza i pacchetti inviati dal client, bloccando valori anomali come un &dPitch invalido."
    KILLAURA:
      description: "&fRileva una serie di hack da combattimento analizzando:\n&8- &dReach&7: Distanza d'attacco anomala\n&8- &dAngle&7: Attacchi con un angolo di mira impossibile\n&8- &dNoSwing&7: Attacchi senza l'animazione del braccio\n&8- &dGCD&7: Movimenti della visuale troppo perfetti e non umani\n&8- &dMultiAura&7: Attacchi a più bersagli simultaneamente"
    toggle-item:
      name-enabled: "&a&lDisattiva {check}"
      name-disabled: "&c&lAttiva {check}"
      lore:
        - ""
        - "&7Clicca per {action}"
    settings-item:
      name: "&e&lImpostazioni &8- &f{check}"
      lore:
        - ""
        - "&7Visualizza o modifica le impostazioni (prossimamente)"
    back-item:
      name: "&c&lIndietro"
      lore:
        - ""
        - "&7Torna al pannello principale"
  roles:
    admin:
      permission: "virgolaac.admin"
      material: "DIAMOND_BLOCK"
    mod:
      permission: "virgolaac.mod"
      material: "EMERALD_BLOCK"
    default:
      material: "MAGENTA_STAINED_GLASS_PANE"
  # (visual helpers moved into existing gui.panel/gui.detail sections)
```

`src/main/resources/plugin.yml`:

```yml
name: VirgolaAntiCheat
version: ${project.version}
main: it.virgola.anticheat.VirgolaAntiCheat
api-version: '1.21'
author: Virgola

# Hard-depend: we require PacketEvents
depend: [packetevents]

commands:
  ac:
    description: VirgolaAntiCheat main command
    usage: /ac <help|panel|alerts|info|freeze|spectate|top|list|kick|ban|reload>
    permission: virgolaac.alerts

permissions:
  virgolaac.*:
    description: Tutti i permessi di VirgolaAntiCheat
    default: op
    children:
      virgolaac.admin: true
      virgolaac.staff: true
      virgolaac.alerts: true
      virgolaac.bypass: true
  
  virgolaac.admin:
    description: Permessi amministrativi (reload, reset, kick, ban, verbose, panel)
    default: op
    children:
      virgolaac.staff: true
      virgolaac.alerts: true
  
  virgolaac.staff:
    description: Permessi staff (info, freeze, spectate, tp, top, list, broadcast)
    default: op
    children:
      virgolaac.alerts: true
  
  virgolaac.alerts:
    description: Ricevi gli alert dell'anticheat
    default: op
  
  virgolaac.bypass:
    description: Bypass tutti i check dell'anticheat
    default: false

```

`target/classes/checks.yml`:

```yml
# VirgolaAntiCheat - Checks Configuration
# All checks use buffer-based detection with lag compensation

checks:
  # ============================================
  # SPEED - Ground Speed Hack Detection
  # ============================================
  SPEED:
    enabled: true
    params:
      # --- existing / core ---
      threshold: 4.5
      alert_cooldown_ms: 1500
      post_flag_reduce: 2.5

      min_ground_ticks: 3
      min_bps: 0.5
      min_max_bps: 1.5
      hard_cap_bps: 20.0

      # Buffer decay
      decay_ok: 0.25
      decay_air: 0.15
      decay_still: 0.10
      decay_slight_over: 0.15

      # Weights
      weight_borderline: 0.25
      weight_low: 0.50
      weight_medium: 0.80
      weight_high: 1.20

      # Sneak grace (spam / momentum)
      sneak_grace_ms: 800

      # --- SOUL-SPECIFIC IMPROVEMENTS ---
      # Tight tolerance on soul blocks (to catch small-margin hacks on SS3)
      soul_recent_ms: 250
      ratio_ignore: 1.10
      ratio_borderline: 1.15
      ratio_ignore_soul: 1.07
      ratio_borderline_soul: 1.12

      # Grace when equipping/unequipping Soul Speed boots while moving on soul sand/soil
      soul_speed_change_grace_ms: 650
      decay_soul_speed_change: 1.25

      # Existing soul/ice/bounce/damage modifiers you already reference (keep/adjust as needed)
      soul_grace_ms: 400
      soul_no_enchant_sneak_mult: 0.7

      ice_mult: 1.2
      packed_ice_mult: 1.3
      blue_ice_mult: 1.4
      ice_grace_ms: 500
      ice_grace_mult: 1.2

      bounce_grace_ms: 800
      bounce_grace_mult: 1.3

      cobweb_mult: 0.15
      berry_mult: 0.25

      block_change_grace_ms: 600
      block_transition_mult: 1.25

      damage_grace_ms: 600
      damage_grace_mult: 1.4

  # ============================================
  # FLY - Flight Detection (Air + Water)
  # ============================================
  FLY:
    enabled: true
    params:
      # Air fly detection
      air_weight: 1.0
      air_threshold: 4.0
      stable_air_min: 6
      
      # Water fly detection
      water_weight: 0.8
      water_ascend_weight: 1.2
      water_threshold: 5.0
      water_stable_min: 10
      
      # General
      alert_cooldown_ms: 1000
      post_flag_reduce: 2.0

  # ============================================
  # JESUS - Water Walking Detection
  # ============================================
  JESUS:
    enabled: true
    params:
      # Detection
      min_flat_ticks: 8
      weight: 1.2
      threshold: 3.0
      
      # Alert settings
      alert_cooldown_ms: 1200
      post_flag_reduce: 1.5

  # ============================================
  # SCAFFOLD - Scaffold/Tower Detection
  # ============================================
  SCAFFOLD:
    enabled: true
    params:
      max_angle: 75.0
      min_pitch: 40.0
      min_pitch_other_face: 20.0
      min_violations: 2
      alert_cooldown_ms: 1000

  # ============================================
  # NOFALL - NoFall Hack Detection (Damage Event Based)
  # ============================================
  # Detection: Tracks real fall distance via packets, then verifies
  # that EntityDamageEvent(FALL) is received when player lands.
  # If no damage event within grace period -> NoFall hack
  NOFALL:
    enabled: true
    params:
      # Minimum fall distance that should cause damage (vanilla = 3 blocks)
      min_fall_blocks: 3.5
      
      # Grace period to receive fall damage event after landing (ms)
      # Set higher if lag causes delayed damage events
      damage_grace_ms: 200
      
      # Grace period after Mace hit during fall (ms)
      mace_grace_ms: 2000
      
      # Alert cooldown (ms)
      alert_cooldown_ms: 1500
      
      # Minimum fall distance to consider dripstone damage (blocks)
      # Small falls onto pointed dripstone can still deal damage (default 1.2)
      min_dripstone_fall: 1.2
      
      # Tolerance ratio when comparing received damage to expected damage
      # (e.g. 0.8 means received >= expected * 0.8 satisfies expectation)
      damage_tolerance: 0.8
      # Allow small slack when comparing fall distance to min dripstone threshold
      # This helps account for packet rounding and measurement differences (default 0.15)
      dripstone_slack: 0.15
      # Enable debug logs for NOFALL (set true to print diagnostic logs)
      debug_logs: false

  # ============================================
  # BADPACKETS - Invalid Packet Detection
  # ============================================
  BADPACKETS:
    enabled: true
    params:
      threshold: 2.0
      weight: 1.0
      alert_cooldown_ms: 1000
      post_flag_reduce: 1.0

  # ============================================
  # KILLAURA - Combat Hack Detection
  # ============================================
  # Sub-checks: Reach, Angle, NoSwing, GCD, MultiAura, Pattern
  # Each sub-check has its own threshold and can be toggled
  KILLAURA:
    enabled: true
    
    # Global settings
    alert_cooldown_ms: 1000
    post_flag_reduce: 2.0
    
    sub_checks:
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # REACH - Hit distance beyond vanilla 3.0
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      reach:
        enabled: true
        # Vanilla reach is 3.0 blocks
        max_distance: 3.0
        # Creative mode reach (default 5.0 blocks)
        creative_max_distance: 5.0
        # Extra distance allowed per lag multiplier
        lag_buffer: 0.3
        # Modes to ignore this sub-check for (optional list of GameMode names)
        # ignore_in_modes: [SPECTATOR]
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 1.0
        threshold: 3.0
        decay: 0.5
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # ANGLE - Hit while not looking at target
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      angle:
        enabled: true
        # Max angle (degrees) between look direction and target
        max_angle: 60.0
        # Extra angle allowed per lag multiplier
        lag_buffer: 10.0
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 1.5
        threshold: 4.0
        decay: 0.4
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # NOSWING - Attack without swing animation
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      noswing:
        enabled: true
        # Max ms between swing and hit
        max_delay_ms: 100
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 2.0
        threshold: 4.0
        decay: 1.0
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # GCD - Invalid rotation sensitivity
      # Only analyzed during active combat (3s after hit)
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      gcd:
        enabled: true
        # Sample analysis (more samples = fewer false positives)
        sample_size: 30
        min_samples: 20
        # GCD thresholds - very low GCD + low stdDev = aimbot
        min_valid_gcd: 0.005
        max_std_dev: 0.0005
        # Buffer settings (conservative to avoid false positives)
        weight: 1.0
        threshold: 6.0
        decay: 0.5
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # MULTIAURA - Multiple targets too fast
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      multiaura:
        enabled: true
        # Time window to count targets (ms)
        window_ms: 500
        # Max different targets in window (sword cleave = 2)
        max_targets: 2
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 2.0
        threshold: 3.0
      
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      # PATTERN - Robotic rotation consistency
      # ────────────────────────────────────────
      pattern:
        enabled: true
        # Minimum samples for analysis
        min_samples: 10
        # Minimum variance (humans have natural jitter)
        min_variance: 0.5
        # Buffer settings
        weight: 1.0
        threshold: 5.0
        decay: 0.2

    # Mode-specific overrides for Killaura behavior.
    # Example: CREATIVE can relax some thresholds or increase reach.
    # Format: checks.KILLAURA.mode_overrides.<GAMEMODE>.sub_checks.<subcheck>.<key>
    mode_overrides:
      CREATIVE:
        sub_checks:
          reach:
            # Keep normal base, but increase creative reach and lag buffer
            creative_max_distance: 5.0
            lag_buffer: 0.35
            threshold: 4.0
          angle:
            max_angle: 65.0
            lag_buffer: 12.0
          noswing:
            max_delay_ms: 150
          gcd:
            min_valid_gcd: 0.006
            max_std_dev: 0.001
          multiaura:
            max_targets: 3
          pattern:
            min_variance: 0.6

```

`target/classes/config.yml`:

```yml
# VirgolaAntiCheat - main configuration

prefix: "&d&lVirgolaAC &8» &7"

debug:
  console: true

alerts:
  # Permission that receives alerts in-game
  permission: "virgolaac.alerts"
  # Also log alerts to console
  console: true

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         DISCORD WEBHOOK
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
discord:
  # Enable Discord notifications
  enabled: false
  # The full Discord webhook URL to post messages to
  webhook-url: ""
  # Optional: list of webhook URLs (used if present). Example:
  # webhooks:
  #   - "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/ID/TOKEN"
  webhooks: []
  # Optional: per-check webhooks mapping
  # check-webhooks:
  #   Speed:
  #     - "https://discord.com/api/webhooks/..."
  check-webhooks: {}
  # Username override for the webhook (optional)
  username: "VirgolaAC"
  # Avatar URL for the webhook (optional)
  avatar-url: ""
  # Embed (rich message) settings
  embed:
    enabled: true
    # Decimal color for the embed (e.g., 16711680 = red)
    color: 16711680

lag-compensation:
  # Ping is used as a multiplier for timings.
  # 1.0 means neutral.
  ping_multiplier:
    base: 1.0
    per_100ms: 0.15
  # TPS is used as a multiplier for timings.
  tps_multiplier:
    base: 1.0
    per_tps_below_20: 0.20

mitigation:
  enabled: false
  # Future: soft mitigations like hitbox clamp, place delay, etc.

gui:
  title: "&dVirgolaAntiCheat"
```
