`target/classes/messages.yml`:

```yml
# ================================================================
#                      VIRGOLA ANTICHEAT - MESSAGES
#                  All messages are customizable below
# ================================================================
# Available placeholders:
#   {prefix}   - Plugin prefix (from config.yml)
#   {player}   - Player name
#   {staff}    - Staff member name
#   {check}    - Check name
#   {vl}       - Violation level
#   {reason}   - Kick/ban reason
#   {message}  - Broadcast message
#   {state}    - ON/OFF state
#   {ping}     - Player ping
#   {gamemode} - Player gamemode

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         GENERAL MESSAGES
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
general:
  no-permission: "{prefix}&cYou do not have permission."
  only-ingame: "{prefix}&cThis command can only be used in-game."
  unknown-command: "{prefix}&7Unknown command. Use &d/ac help"
  player-not-found: "{prefix}&cPlayer &f{player} &cnot found."
  reloaded: "{prefix}&aConfiguration reloaded successfully."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         COMMAND USAGE MESSAGES
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
usage:
  info: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac info &8<&fplayer&8>"
  reset: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac reset &8<&fplayer&7|&fall&8>"
  kick: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac kick &8<&fplayer&8> &7[reason]"
  ban: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac ban &8<&fplayer&8> &7[reason]"
  freeze: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac freeze &8<&fplayer&8>"
  unfreeze: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac unfreeze &8<&fplayer&8>"
  spectate: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac spectate &8<&fplayer&8>"
  tp: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac tp &8<&fplayer&8>"
  broadcast: "{prefix}&7Usage: &f/ac broadcast &8<&fmessage&8>"
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         TOGGLE COMMANDS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
toggle:
  alerts: "{prefix}Alerts &8» {state}"
  verbose: "{prefix}Verbose &8» {state}"
  check: "{prefix}&f{check} &8» {state}"
  state-on: "&aON"
  state-off: "&cOFF"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         VIOLATIONS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
violations:
  reset-player: "{prefix}&aVL reset &7for &f{player}"
  reset-all: "{prefix}&aVL reset &7for &fall players"
  unknown-check: "{prefix}&cCheck &f{check} &cnot found."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         KICK & BAN
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
punishment:
  kicked: "{prefix}&f{player} &7was kicked &8• &c{reason}"
  kick-broadcast: "{prefix}&f{staff} &8➤ &7kicked &f{player} &8• &c{reason}"
  banned: "{prefix}&f{player} &7was banned &8• &c{reason}"
  ban-broadcast: "{prefix}&f{staff} &8➤ &7banned &f{player} &8• &c{reason}"
  default-reason: "Cheating"

  # Kick screen (multiline using |)
  kick-screen: |

    &c&l⚠ DISCONNECTED

    &7You have been &ckicked &7from the server.
    &7Reason: &f{reason}


  # Ban screen (multiline using |)
  ban-screen: |

    &4&l✖ BANNED

    &7You have been &4permanently banned&7.
    &7Reason: &f{reason}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         FREEZE SYSTEM
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
freeze:
  frozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &8» &efrozen"
  unfrozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &8» &aunfrozen"
  broadcast: "{prefix}&f{staff} &7has frozen &f{player}"
  already-frozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &7is already &efrozen&7."
  not-frozen: "{prefix}&f{player} &7is not frozen."

  # Message shown to frozen player
  frozen-screen: |

    &c&l❄ FROZEN

    &7You have been stopped by &fstaff&7.
    &7Please wait for &instrunctions&7 and do not move.

    &8&oDisconnecting = automatic ban


  # Message shown when player is unfrozen
  unfrozen-screen: |

    &a&l✓ UNFROZEN

    &7You can now resume playing &anormally&7.


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         SPECTATE SYSTEM
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
spectate:
  start: "{prefix}&7Spectating &f{player} &8• &7use &d/ac unspec &7to exit"
  stop: "{prefix}&aSpectate ended &7• position restored"
  cannot-self: "{prefix}&7You cannot spectate &fyourself&7."
  not-spectating: "{prefix}&7You are not spectating anyone."


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         TELEPORT
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
teleport:
  success: "{prefix}&aTeleported &7to &f{player}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         STAFF BROADCAST
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
staff:
  broadcast-format: "&8[&d&lSTAFF&8] &f{staff}&8: &7{message}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         PLAYER INFO (/ac info)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
info:
  header: "\n&d&l─── &f&l{player} &d&l───"
  status-line: "&7Ping: {ping_color}{ping}ms &8• &7Mode: &f{gamemode}"
  no-violations: "  &7No violations &a✔"
  violation-line: "  &8▸ &7{check} &8• {vl_color}{vl}"
  total-line: "  &7Total VL: {total_color}{total}"
  footer: "&d&l───────────────────\n"
  # Dynamic colors for ping
  ping-good: "&a"      # < 80ms
  ping-medium: "&e"    # < 150ms
  ping-bad: "&c"       # >= 150ms
  # Dynamic colors for VL
  vl-low: "&7"         # < 5
  vl-medium: "&e"      # < 10
  vl-high: "&c&l"      # >= 10
  total-low: "&f"      # < 10
  total-medium: "&e"   # < 20
  total-high: "&c&l"   # >= 20


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         PLAYER LIST (/ac list)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
list:
  header: "\n&d&l─── &f&lSuspicious Players &d&l───"
  player-line: "  &8▸ &f{player} &8• &7VL: {vl_color}{vl}"
  empty: "  &7No suspicious players &a✔"
  footer: "&d&l──────────────────────\n"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         TOP VIOLATIONS (/ac top)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
top:
  header: "\n&d&l─── &f&lTop 10 Violations &d&l───"
  player-line: "  {medal} &f#{rank} &7{player} &8• &7VL: {vl_color}{vl}"
  empty: "  &7No violations recorded &a✔"
  footer: "&d&l────────────────────────\n"
  # Medals for positions
  medal-1: "&c❤"
  medal-2: "&6●"
  medal-3: "&e●"
  medal-other: "&7○"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         ALERTS
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
alerts:
  format: "&8[&c&l!&8] &f{player} &8» &e{check} &7(&c+{vl}&7) &8• &7{info}"
  # Format used when sending alerts to a Discord webhook. Use placeholders: {player}, {check}, {vl}, {info}
  # Markdown is supported by Discord (bold, code, etc.). If empty, a default format will be used.


# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                         HELP SYSTEM
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
help:
  # Header and footer for help list
  header: "\n&d&l─── &f&lVirgolaAC &d&lHelp &d&l───"
  subtitle: "&8Hover: &7description &8• &8Click: &7details"
  footer: "&d&l────────────────────────\n"
  
  # Command detail page
  command-header: "\n&d&l─── &f&l/ac {command} &d&l───"
  command-footer: "&d&l─────────────────────\n"
  back-button: "&8[&d◀ &7Back&8]"
  back-hover: "&7Return to command list"
  
  # Detail sections
  section-usage: "&d▸ &7Usage"
  section-permission: "&d▸ &7Permission"
  section-description: "&d▸ &7Description"
  section-details: "&d▸ &7Details"
  section-examples: "&d▸ &7Examples"
  detail-bullet: "  &8• &7"
  example-color: "&a"
  example-hover: "&7Click to copy"
  
  # Command not found
  not-found: "{prefix}&7Command &f{command} &7not found."
  not-found-hint: "{prefix}&8Use &d/ac help &8for the list."
  
  # ─────────────── COMMAND DEFINITIONS ───────────────
  # Each command has: usage, description, details (list), examples (list)
  
  commands:
    help:
      usage: "/ac help [comando]"
      description: "&fMostra la lista comandi o il dettaglio di un comando."
      details:
        - "&7Nessun argomento: mostra tutti i comandi."
        - "&7Con argomento: mostra i dettagli per quel comando."
      examples:
        - "/ac help"
        - "/ac help freeze"
    
    alerts:
      usage: "/ac alerts"
      description: "&fAttiva/disattiva la ricezione degli avvisi."
      details:
        - "&7Abilitati: ricevi notifiche per ogni violazione."
        - "&7Disabilitati: non riceverai notifiche."
      examples:
        - "/ac alerts"
    
    info:
      usage: "/ac info <player>"
      description: "&fMostra le violazioni di un giocatore."
      details:
        - "&7Mostra ogni check con il suo livello di violazione (VL)."
        - "&7Include anche informazioni su ping e gamemode."
      examples:
        - "/ac info Steve"
    
    list:
      usage: "/ac list"
      description: "&fElenca i giocatori online con violazioni."
      details:
        - "&7Mostra i giocatori ordinati per VL totali."
        - "&7Colori: &cRosso (>=10)&7, &eGiallo (>=5)&7, &7Grigio (<5)."
      examples:
        - "/ac list"
    
    top:
      usage: "/ac top"
      description: "&fMostra la top 10 dei giocatori con più violazioni."
      details:
        - "&7Utile per identificare rapidamente i cheater."
      examples:
        - "/ac top"
    
    freeze:
      usage: "/ac freeze <player>"
      description: "&fCongela un giocatore sospetto."
      details:
        - "&7Il giocatore non potrà muoversi, attaccare o usare comandi."
        - "&7Se si disconnette, lo staff verrà avvisato."
      examples:
        - "/ac freeze Steve"
    
    unfreeze:
      usage: "/ac unfreeze <player>"
      description: "&fScongela un giocatore."
      details:
        - "&7Il giocatore potrà tornare a giocare normalmente."
      examples:
        - "/ac unfreeze Steve"
    
    spectate:
      usage: "/ac spectate <player>"
      description: "&fEntra in modalità spettatore su un giocatore."
      details:
        - "&7Entri in modalità spettatore invisibile."
        - "&7Segui automaticamente il giocatore."
        - "&7Posizione e gamemode vengono salvati e ripristinati."
      examples:
        - "/ac spectate Steve"
        - "/ac spec Steve"
    
    unspectate:
      usage: "/ac unspectate"
      description: "&fEsci dalla modalità spettatore."
      details:
        - "&7Torni alla tua posizione e gamemode originali."
      examples:
        - "/ac unspectate"
        - "/ac unspec"
    
    tp:
      usage: "/ac tp <player>"
      description: "&fTeletrasportati da un giocatore."
      details:
        - "&7Utile per raggiungere rapidamente un sospetto."
      examples:
        - "/ac tp Steve"
        - "/ac goto Steve"
    
    broadcast:
      usage: "/ac broadcast <messaggio>"
      description: "&fInvia un messaggio allo staff online."
      details:
        - "&7Solo gli utenti con il permesso virgolaac.staff lo vedono."
        - "&7Utile per comunicazioni interne."
      examples:
        - "/ac broadcast Controllate Steve"
        - "/ac bc Sospetto allo spawn"
    
    panel:
      usage: "/ac panel"
      description: "&fApri il pannello di gestione grafico (GUI)."
      details:
        - "&7Visualizza e attiva/disattiva i check."
        - "&7Mostra statistiche del server (TPS, RAM, etc)."
      examples:
        - "/ac panel"
        - "/ac gui"
    
    reset:
      usage: "/ac reset <player|all>"
      description: "&fResetta le violazioni di un giocatore."
      details:
        - "&7Con un nome: resetta solo quel giocatore."
        - "&7Con 'all': resetta tutti i giocatori online."
      examples:
        - "/ac reset Steve"
        - "/ac reset all"
    
    verbose:
      usage: "/ac verbose"
      description: "&fAttiva/disattiva la modalità verbose/debug."
      details:
        - "&7Mostra informazioni aggiuntive sulle rilevazioni."
        - "&7Utile per il debug e per testare i check."
      examples:
        - "/ac verbose"
        - "/ac debug"
    
    kick:
      usage: "/ac kick <player> [motivo]"
      description: "&fEspelli un giocatore dal server."
      details:
        - "&7Il giocatore vedrà il motivo sullo schermo."
        - "&7Lo staff riceverà una notifica."
      examples:
        - "/ac kick Steve"
        - "/ac kick Steve Speed Hack"
    
    ban:
      usage: "/ac ban <player> [motivo]"
      description: "&fBanna permanentemente un giocatore."
      details:
        - "&7Usa il sistema di ban di default di Bukkit."
        - "&7Lo staff riceverà una notifica."
      examples:
        - "/ac ban Steve"
        - "/ac ban Steve Fly Hack"
    
    reload:
      usage: "/ac reload"
      description: "&fRicarica le configurazioni del plugin."
      details:
        - "&7Ricarica config.yml, checks.yml, messages.yml."
        - "&7Non richiede un riavvio del server."
      examples:
        - "/ac reload"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------
#                       SETTINGS ITEM DESCRIPTIONS
#  Descriptions displayed in the settings GUI for each parameter.
#  Keys are: gui.detail.settings-item.descriptions.<CHECK>.<param>
# ---------------------------------------------------------------
gui:
  detail:
    settings-item:
      descriptions:
        SPEED:
          base_walk_bps: "Base walking speed (blocks/sec). Increasing this value raises the allowed baseline movement speed — players can move faster before the check flags them. Decreasing makes the check stricter and may increase false positives for legitimate players."
          min_bps: "Minimum considered bps for movement measurements. Raising will ignore very small movements; lowering makes the detector more sensitive to tiny motion."
          threshold: "Detection threshold for accumulated speed buffer. Higher threshold reduces false positives but delays flagging; lower threshold makes the system stricter and may increase alerts."
          weight_high: "High severity weight applied to strong speed violations. Increasing this increases the VL per violation; decreasing reduces punishment severity."
          decay_ok: "How quickly the speed buffer decays when behavior is normal. Higher decay clears violations faster; lower decay keeps historical violations longer."

        FLY:
          air_weight: "Relative weight for air-fly evidence. Increasing makes air detections contribute more to the buffer; lowering reduces their influence."
          air_threshold: "Threshold for air fly detection buffer. Raising requires stronger proof to flag; lowering is more sensitive."

        JESUS:
          min_flat_ticks: "Minimum ticks considered 'flat on water' before triggering detection. Increasing makes detector more patient; lowering detects faster but may false-trigger on brief contacts."
          threshold: "Detection threshold for Jesus buffer. Higher = stricter to trigger; lower = more sensitive."

        SCAFFOLD:
          max_angle: "Maximum allowed angle for scaffold-like placement behavior. Increasing allows steeper placements without flags; decreasing tightens detection for straighter builds."
          min_violations: "Number of violations required before an alert is issued. Increasing reduces noisy alerts; lowering makes detection quicker."

        NOFALL:
          min_fall_blocks: "Minimum fall distance (blocks) that should normally cause fall damage. Increasing raises the bar (fewer falls trigger damage expectations); decreasing makes the check consider smaller falls."
          damage_grace_ms: "Grace period (ms) to wait for the damage event after landing. Increase to tolerate lag (avoids false positives), decrease to be stricter."

        BADPACKETS:
          threshold: "Buffer threshold for invalid packets. Higher = fewer reports (more tolerant); lower = more sensitive to malformed or unexpected packets."

        KILLAURA:
          sub_checks.reach.max_distance: "Maximum allowed attack reach (blocks). Increase to allow longer legitimate hits (creative/laggy); decrease to detect extended reach."
          sub_checks.angle.max_angle: "Maximum angle difference allowed between look direction and target for a valid hit. Increasing allows wider aim; decreasing makes the check stricter (flags when not looking)."
          sub_checks.noswing.max_delay_ms: "Maximum allowed ms between swing and hit. Increase to tolerate slower swings/lag; decrease to detect hits without proper swing timing."
          sub_checks.gcd.sample_size: "Number of rotation samples used for GCD analysis. Increasing makes analysis more stable but slower; decreasing makes detection quicker but possibly noisier."
          sub_checks.multiaura.max_targets: "Maximum different targets allowed within the time window. Increasing allows more simultaneous hits; decreasing detects multi-target aimbot behavior sooner."
          sub_checks.pattern.min_variance: "Minimum rotational variance expected from a human. Increasing this requires more jitter (harder to flag); decreasing makes the detector sensitive to robotic consistency."
    discord:
      usage: "/ac discord <subcommand>"
      description: "Manage Discord webhook integration."
      details:
        - "set <webhook>: save the webhook URL and enable Discord alerts (legacy single)"
        - "add <webhook>: add a webhook to the global list"
        - "remove <webhook>: remove a webhook from the global list"
        - "list: show configured global webhooks"
        - "setcheck <check> <webhook>: set a webhook for a specific check"
        - "unsetcheck <check>: remove per-check webhook"
        - "test: send a test alert to the configured webhook(s)"
        - "disable: disable Discord alerts"
      examples:
        - "/ac discord add https://discord.com/api/webhooks/..."
        - "/ac discord test"
        - "/ac discord disable"

# Discord messages & templates
discord:
  usage: |
    {prefix}&7Discord webhook commands:
    &f/ac discord set <webhook> &8- &7Set legacy webhook and enable
    &f/ac discord add <webhook> &8- &7Add a webhook to the global list
    &f/ac discord remove <webhook> &8- &7Remove a webhook from the global list
    &f/ac discord list &8- &7List configured global webhooks
    &f/ac discord setcheck <check> <webhook> &8- &7Set a webhook for a specific check
    &f/ac discord unsetcheck <check> &8- &7Remove per-check webhook
    &f/ac discord test &8- &7Send a test alert (uses nearest online player)
    &f/ac discord disable &8- &7Disable Discord alerts
  set-success: "{prefix}&aDiscord webhook saved and enabled."
  disable-success: "{prefix}&aDiscord alerts disabled."
  test-no-player: "{prefix}&cNo online player to use for test."
  test-sent: "{prefix}&aTest alert sent to Discord."

  # Format used when sending plain alerts to Discord. Placeholders: {player},{check},{vl},{info}
  format: "**{player}** was detected by **{check}** (+{vl}) - {info}"

  # Embed templates (title/description support placeholders; description used in embed body)
  embed:
    title: "{check} detected"
    description: "{player} was flagged for {check} (+{vl}) {info}"
```

`target/classes/plugin.yml`:

```yml
name: VirgolaAntiCheat
version: 1.6.2
main: it.virgola.anticheat.VirgolaAntiCheat
api-version: '1.21'
author: Virgola

# Hard-depend: we require PacketEvents
depend: [packetevents]

commands:
  ac:
    description: VirgolaAntiCheat main command
    usage: /ac <help|panel|alerts|info|freeze|spectate|top|list|kick|ban|reload>
    permission: virgolaac.alerts

permissions:
  virgolaac.*:
    description: Tutti i permessi di VirgolaAntiCheat
    default: op
    children:
      virgolaac.admin: true
      virgolaac.staff: true
      virgolaac.alerts: true
      virgolaac.bypass: true
  
  virgolaac.admin:
    description: Permessi amministrativi (reload, reset, kick, ban, verbose, panel)
    default: op
    children:
      virgolaac.staff: true
      virgolaac.alerts: true
  
  virgolaac.staff:
    description: Permessi staff (info, freeze, spectate, tp, top, list, broadcast)
    default: op
    children:
      virgolaac.alerts: true
  
  virgolaac.alerts:
    description: Ricevi gli alert dell'anticheat
    default: op
  
  virgolaac.bypass:
    description: Bypass tutti i check dell'anticheat
    default: false

```

```properties
artifactId=VirgolaAntiCheat
```
